// ==================== VERİ SINIFLARI ====================

data class DesktopGridItem(
    val id: String,
    val label: String,
    val packageName: String?,
    val isSystem: Boolean = false,
    val isFolder: Boolean = false,
    val folderItems: List<String> = emptyList(),
    val iconBitmap: androidx.compose.ui.graphics.ImageBitmap? = null,
    val iconRes: Int? = null,
    val isWidget: Boolean = false,
    val widgetType: String = "",
    val widgetData: Map<String, Any> = emptyMap(),
    val isHidden: Boolean = false
)

@Stable
class DesktopGridState(
    initialItems: List<DesktopGridItem> = emptyList()
) {
    private val _allItems = mutableStateListOf<DesktopGridItem>()
    val allItems: List<DesktopGridItem> get() = _allItems

    var filteredItems by mutableStateOf<List<DesktopGridItem>>(emptyList())
        private set

    val selectedIds = mutableStateListOf<String>()
    var renamingId by mutableStateOf<String?>(null)

    var dragOffsets by mutableStateOf<Map<String, Offset>>(emptyMap())
    var dragStartOrigins by mutableStateOf<Map<String, Offset>>(emptyMap())

    var searchQuery by mutableStateOf("")
    var showHidden by mutableStateOf(false)
    var sortBy by mutableStateOf("name")

    var iconSize by mutableStateOf(44)
    var iconSpacing by mutableStateOf(8)

    // ============ ÇERÇEVE SEÇİM (RUBBER-BAND) ============
    var selectionStart by mutableStateOf<Offset?>(null)
    var selectionEnd by mutableStateOf<Offset?>(null)

    var contextMenuTarget by mutableStateOf<String?>(null)
    var contextMenuOffset by mutableStateOf<Offset?>(null)

    val customNames = mutableStateMapOf<String, String>()
    val positions = mutableStateMapOf<String, Offset>()

    init {
        updateItems(initialItems)
    }

    fun updateItems(newItems: List<DesktopGridItem>) {
        _allItems.clear()
        _allItems.addAll(newItems)
        applyFiltersAndSort()
    }

    fun applyFiltersAndSort() {
        var items: List<DesktopGridItem> = _allItems
        if (searchQuery.isNotEmpty()) {
            items = items.filter {
                it.label.contains(searchQuery, ignoreCase = true) ||
                        it.id.contains(searchQuery, ignoreCase = true)
            }
        }
        if (!showHidden) {
            items = items.filter { !it.isHidden }
        }
        items = when (sortBy) {
            "name" -> items.sortedBy { it.label.lowercase() }
            "type" -> items.sortedBy { it.isFolder }
            "date" -> items.sortedByDescending { it.id }
            else -> items
        }
        filteredItems = items
    }

    fun select(id: String, addToSelection: Boolean = false) {
        if (addToSelection) {
            if (!selectedIds.contains(id)) selectedIds.add(id)
        } else {
            selectedIds.clear()
            selectedIds.add(id)
        }
    }

    fun toggleSelection(id: String) {
        if (selectedIds.contains(id)) {
            selectedIds.remove(id)
        } else {
            selectedIds.add(id)
        }
    }

    fun clearSelection() {
        selectedIds.clear()
        renamingId = null
        selectionStart = null
        selectionEnd = null
    }

    fun selectAll() {
        selectedIds.clear()
        selectedIds.addAll(filteredItems.map { it.id })
    }

    fun isSelected(id: String): Boolean = selectedIds.contains(id)

    fun getPosition(id: String, default: Offset = Offset.Zero): Offset {
        return positions[id] ?: default
    }

    fun setPosition(id: String, position: Offset) {
        positions[id] = position
    }

    fun removePosition(id: String) {
        positions.remove(id)
    }

    fun startDrag(ids: List<String>) {
        val origins = ids.associateWith { id ->
            positions[id] ?: Offset.Zero
        }
        dragStartOrigins = origins
        dragOffsets = origins
    }

    fun updateDrag(delta: Offset) {
        dragOffsets = dragStartOrigins.mapValues { (id, origin) ->
            origin + delta
        }
    }

    fun endDrag() {
        dragOffsets = emptyMap()
        dragStartOrigins = emptyMap()
    }

    fun startRenaming(id: String) {
        renamingId = id
    }

    fun endRenaming() {
        renamingId = null
    }

    fun getCustomName(id: String): String? = customNames[id]

    fun setCustomName(id: String, name: String) {
        customNames[id] = name
    }

    fun showContextMenu(id: String, offset: Offset) {
        contextMenuTarget = id
        contextMenuOffset = offset
    }

    fun hideContextMenu() {
        contextMenuTarget = null
        contextMenuOffset = null
    }

    fun getItem(id: String): DesktopGridItem? {
        return _allItems.find { it.id == id }
    }

    fun getSelectedItems(): List<DesktopGridItem> {
        return selectedIds.mapNotNull { getItem(it) }
    }

    fun getDisplayName(item: DesktopGridItem): String {
        return customNames[item.id] ?: item.label
    }

    fun saveState(context: Context) {
        val prefs = context.getSharedPreferences("ASUI_DesktopGridPrefs", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
        prefs.edit().apply {
            putString("positions", positions.map { "${it.key}|${it.value.x}|${it.value.y}" }.joinToString(";"))
            putString("customNames", customNames.map { "${it.key}|${it.value}" }.joinToString(";"))
            putString("sortBy", sortBy)
            putBoolean("showHidden", showHidden)
            putInt("iconSize", iconSize)
            putInt("iconSpacing", iconSpacing)
        }.apply()
    }

    fun loadState(context: Context) {
        val prefs = context.getSharedPreferences("ASUI_DesktopGridPrefs", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
        val posStr = prefs.getString("positions", "") ?: ""
        if (posStr.isNotEmpty()) {
            posStr.split(";").forEach { entry ->
                val parts = entry.split("|")
                if (parts.size == 3) {
                    val x = parts[1].toFloatOrNull() ?: 0f
                    val y = parts[2].toFloatOrNull() ?: 0f
                    positions[parts[0]] = Offset(x, y)
                }
            }
        }
        val namesStr = prefs.getString("customNames", "") ?: ""
        if (namesStr.isNotEmpty()) {
            namesStr.split(";").forEach { entry ->
                val parts = entry.split("|")
                if (parts.size == 2) {
                    customNames[parts[0]] = parts[1]
                }
            }
        }
        sortBy = prefs.getString("sortBy", "name") ?: "name"
        showHidden = prefs.getBoolean("showHidden", false)
        iconSize = prefs.getInt("iconSize", 44)
        iconSpacing = prefs.getInt("iconSpacing", 8)
    }
}

// ==================== ANA BİLEŞEN ====================

@OptIn(ExperimentalAnimationApi::class)
@Composable
fun DesktopGrid(
    apps: List<DesktopApp>,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    onOpenWindow: (id: String, title: String) -> Unit = { _, _ -> },
    onOpenWidget: (DesktopWidgetData) -> Unit = {},
    state: DesktopGridState = remember { DesktopGridState() }
) {
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val pm = context.packageManager
    val density = LocalDensity.current
    val config = LocalConfiguration.current
    val scope = rememberCoroutineScope()
    val focusManager = LocalFocusManager.current

    // ============ YENİ ÖĞELERİ OLUŞTUR ============
    LaunchedEffect(apps) {
        val items = buildItems(apps, context, pm)
        state.updateItems(items)
        state.loadState(context)
    }

    // ============ IZGARA BOYUTLARI ============
    val cellWidth = with(density) { (state.iconSize + state.iconSpacing * 2).dp.toPx() }
    val cellHeight = with(density) { (state.iconSize + 40 + state.iconSpacing * 2).dp.toPx() }
    val startX = with(density) { 16.dp.toPx() }
    val startY = with(density) { 16.dp.toPx() }
    val maxRows = max(3, ((config.screenHeightDp - 100) / (state.iconSize / 40 + 60)))

    fun getGridPosition(id: String, default: Offset = Offset.Zero): Offset {
        val saved = state.getPosition(id)
        if (saved != Offset.Zero) return saved
        val occupied = state.positions.values.toSet()
        var col = 0
        var row = 0
        var found = false
        while (!found) {
            val pos = Offset(startX + (col * cellWidth), startY + (row * cellHeight))
            if (!occupied.contains(pos)) {
                state.setPosition(id, pos)
                return pos
            }
            row++
            if (row >= maxRows) {
                row = 0
                col++
            }
        }
        return default
    }

    val filteredItems = state.filteredItems
    var showSearchBar by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }

    // ============ ANA KANVAS (ÇERÇEVE SEÇİM DESTEKLİ) ============
    Box(
        modifier = modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .pointerInput("DesktopCanvas") {
                detectDragGestures(
                    onDragStart = { offset ->
                        state.clearSelection()
                        state.selectionStart = offset
                        state.selectionEnd = offset
                    },
                    onDrag = { change, dragAmount ->
                        change.consume()
                        state.selectionEnd = state.selectionEnd?.plus(dragAmount)
                        // Seçim çerçevesi içindeki öğeleri seç
                        val l = minOf(state.selectionStart!!.x, state.selectionEnd!!.x)
                        val t = minOf(state.selectionStart!!.y, state.selectionEnd!!.y)
                        val r = maxOf(state.selectionStart!!.x, state.selectionEnd!!.x)
                        val b = maxOf(state.selectionStart!!.y, state.selectionEnd!!.y)
                        val selRect = Rect(l, t, r, b)

                        state.selectedIds.clear()
                        filteredItems.forEach { item ->
                            val pos = getGridPosition(item.id)
                            val itemRect = Rect(
                                pos.x,
                                pos.y,
                                pos.x + with(density) { (state.iconSize + 16).dp.toPx() },
                                pos.y + with(density) { (state.iconSize + 16 + 40).dp.toPx() }
                            )
                            if (selRect.overlaps(itemRect)) {
                                state.selectedIds.add(item.id)
                            }
                        }
                    },
                    onDragEnd = {
                        state.selectionStart = null
                        state.selectionEnd = null
                    }
                )
            }
            .pointerInput("DesktopBgTap") {
                detectTapGestures(
                    onTap = {
                        if (state.selectionStart == null) {
                            state.clearSelection()
                            showSearchBar = false
                            focusManager.clearFocus()
                        }
                    },
                    onLongPress = { offset ->
                        if (state.selectedIds.isEmpty()) {
                            state.contextMenuTarget = null
                            state.contextMenuOffset = offset
                        } else {
                            state.contextMenuTarget = state.selectedIds.firstOrNull()
                            state.contextMenuOffset = offset
                        }
                    }
                )
            }
            .pointerInput("DesktopBgRightClick") {
                awaitPointerEventScope {
                    while (true) {
                        val event = awaitPointerEvent(PointerEventPass.Initial)
                        if (event.type == PointerEventType.Press && event.buttons.isSecondaryPressed) {
                            event.changes.forEach { it.consume() }
                            state.clearSelection()
                            state.contextMenuTarget = null
                            state.contextMenuOffset = event.changes.first().position
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
    ) {
        // ============ SEÇİM ÇERÇEVESİ (RUBBER-BAND) ============
        if (state.selectionStart != null && state.selectionEnd != null) {
            val l = minOf(state.selectionStart!!.x, state.selectionEnd!!.x)
            val t = minOf(state.selectionStart!!.y, state.selectionEnd!!.y)
            val w = abs(state.selectionStart!!.x - state.selectionEnd!!.x)
            val h = abs(state.selectionStart!!.y - state.selectionEnd!!.y)
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .offset { IntOffset(l.toInt(), t.toInt()) }
                    .size(with(density) { w.toDp() }, with(density) { h.toDp() })
                    .background(Color(0x443A8DFF))
                    .border(1.dp, Color(0xFF3A8DFF))
            )
        }

        // ============ ÖĞELER (KEY İLE OPTİMİZE) ============
        filteredItems.forEach { item ->
            val basePos = getGridPosition(item.id)
            val dragOffset = state.dragOffsets[item.id] ?: Offset.Zero
            val currentPos = basePos + dragOffset

            val isSelected = state.isSelected(item.id)
            val isRenaming = state.renamingId == item.id
            var showMenu by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
            var lastTapTime by remember { mutableStateOf(0L) }

            val interactionSource = remember { MutableInteractionSource() }
            val isHovered by interactionSource.collectIsHoveredAsState()

            // ============ TEK ÖĞE (KEY İLE SARILMIŞ) ============
            key(item.id) {
                Box(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .offset {
                            IntOffset(
                                currentPos.x.roundToInt(),
                                currentPos.y.roundToInt()
                            )
                        }
                        .width((state.iconSize + 16).dp)
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(8.dp))
                        .background(
                            if (isSelected && !isRenaming) Color(0x663A8DFF)
                            else if (isHovered && !isRenaming) Color(0x223A8DFF)
                            else Color.Transparent
                        )
                        .hoverable(interactionSource)
                        .pointerInput(item.id + "_drag") {
                            detectDragGestures(
                                onDragStart = {
                                    state.renamingId = null
                                    if (!state.isSelected(item.id)) {
                                        state.clearSelection()
                                        state.select(item.id)
                                    }
                                    val selectedIds = state.selectedIds.toList()
                                    state.startDrag(selectedIds)
                                },
                                onDrag = { change, dragAmount ->
                                    change.consume()
                                    state.updateDrag(dragAmount)
                                },
                                onDragEnd = {
                                    val ids = state.selectedIds.toList()
                                    ids.forEach { id ->
                                        val pos = state.dragOffsets[id] ?: return@forEach
                                        val col = ((pos.x - startX) / cellWidth).roundToInt().coerceAtLeast(0)
                                        val row = ((pos.y - startY) / cellHeight).roundToInt().coerceAtLeast(0)
                                        state.setPosition(id, Offset(
                                            startX + (col * cellWidth),
                                            startY + (row * cellHeight)
                                        ))
                                    }
                                    state.endDrag()
                                    state.saveState(context)
                                }
                            )
                        }
                        .pointerInput(item.id + "_rightclick") {
                            awaitPointerEventScope {
                                while (true) {
                                    val event = awaitPointerEvent(PointerEventPass.Initial)
                                    if (event.type == PointerEventType.Press && event.buttons.isSecondaryPressed) {
                                        event.changes.forEach { it.consume() }
                                        if (!state.isSelected(item.id)) {
                                            state.clearSelection()
                                            state.select(item.id)
                                        }
                                        state.showContextMenu(item.id, event.changes.first().position)
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                        .pointerInput(item.id + "_tap") {
                            detectTapGestures(
                                onLongPress = { offset ->
                                    if (!state.isSelected(item.id)) {
                                        state.clearSelection()
                                        state.select(item.id)
                                    }
                                    state.showContextMenu(item.id, offset)
                                },
                                onTap = {
                                    val now = System.currentTimeMillis()
                                    if (isSelected && !item.isSystem && (now - lastTapTime in 400..2500)) {
                                        state.startRenaming(item.id)
                                    } else {
                                        state.clearSelection()
                                        state.select(item.id)
                                        state.renamingId = null
                                        if (item.isSystem && item.id == "recycle_bin") {
                                            onOpenWindow("recycle_bin", "Geri Dönüşüm Kutusu")
                                        }
                                    }
                                    lastTapTime = now
                                },
                                onDoubleTap = {
                                    state.renamingId = null
                                    if (item.isSystem && item.id == "recycle_bin") {
                                        onOpenWindow("recycle_bin", "Geri Dönüşüm Kutusu")
                                    } else if (item.packageName != null) {
                                        val app = apps.find { it.packageName == item.packageName }
                                        if (app != null) {
                                            if (!TaskbarState.runningApps.any { it.packageName == app.packageName }) {
                                                TaskbarState.runningApps.add(app)
                                            }
                                            pm.getLaunchIntentForPackage(item.packageName)?.let { context.startActivity(it) }
                                        }
                                    } else if (item.isWidget) {
                                        val widgetData = DesktopWidgetData(
                                            id = item.id,
                                            type = item.widgetType,
                                            x = currentPos.x,
                                            y = currentPos.y,
                                            data = item.widgetData
                                        )
                                        onOpenWidget(widgetData)
                                    }
                                }
                            )
                        }
                        .padding(4.dp),
                    contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                ) {
                    Column(
                        horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
                        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(4.dp)
                    ) {
                        if (item.iconBitmap != null) {
                            Image(
                                bitmap = item.iconBitmap,
                                contentDescription = null,
                                modifier = Modifier.size(state.iconSize.dp)
                            )
                        } else if (item.iconRes != null) {
                            Image(
                                painter = painterResource(item.iconRes),
                                contentDescription = null,
                                modifier = Modifier.size(state.iconSize.dp)
                            )
                        } else {
                            Icon(
                                if (item.isFolder) Icons.Default.Folder else Icons.Default.Apps,
                                contentDescription = null,
                                tint = Color.White,
                                modifier = Modifier.size(state.iconSize.dp)
                            )
                        }

                        val displayName = state.getCustomName(item.id) ?: item.label
                        if (isRenaming) {
                            var tempText by remember { mutableStateOf(displayName) }
                            val focusRequester = remember { FocusRequester() }

                            BasicTextField(
                                value = tempText,
                                onValueChange = {
                                    tempText = it
                                    state.setCustomName(item.id, it)
                                },
                                textStyle = TextStyle(
                                    color = Color.White,
                                    fontSize = 11.sp,
                                    textAlign = TextAlign.Center
                                ),
                                keyboardOptions = KeyboardOptions(imeAction = ImeAction.Done),
                                keyboardActions = KeyboardActions(
                                    onDone = {
                                        state.endRenaming()
                                        focusManager.clearFocus()
                                        state.saveState(context)
                                    }
                                ),
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .background(Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.3f), RoundedCornerShape(4.dp))
                                    .padding(2.dp)
                                    .focusRequester(focusRequester),
                                singleLine = true
                            )
                            LaunchedEffect(Unit) {
                                focusRequester.requestFocus()
                            }
                        } else {
                            Text(
                                text = displayName,
                                fontSize = 11.sp,
                                color = Color.White,
                                maxLines = 2,
                                overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
                                textAlign = TextAlign.Center,
                                lineHeight = 14.sp,
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .background(Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.4f), RoundedCornerShape(4.dp))
                                    .padding(horizontal = 4.dp, vertical = 2.dp)
                            )
                        }
                    }

                    if (item.isFolder && item.folderItems.isNotEmpty()) {
                        Box(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .align(Alignment.TopEnd)
                                .padding(4.dp)
                                .size(16.dp)
                                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(8.dp))
                                .background(Color(0xFF0078D7)),
                            contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                        ) {
                            Text(
                                text = item.folderItems.size.toString(),
                                color = Color.White,
                                fontSize = 9.sp,
                                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                            )
                        }
                    }

                    if (item.isWidget) {
                        Box(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .align(Alignment.TopStart)
                                .padding(4.dp)
                                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(4.dp))
                                .background(Color(0xCC000000))
                                .padding(horizontal = 4.dp, vertical = 2.dp)
                        ) {
                            Text("🧩", fontSize = 10.sp)
                        }
                    }

                    if (state.contextMenuTarget == item.id && state.contextMenuOffset != null) {
                        ContextMenu(
                            item = item,
                            state = state,
                            context = context,
                            pm = pm,
                            apps = apps,
                            onOpenWindow = onOpenWindow,
                            onDismiss = { state.hideContextMenu() }
                        )
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // ============ MASAÜSTÜ ARAMA ÇUBUĞU ============
        AnimatedVisibility(
            visible = showSearchBar,
            enter = fadeIn() + slideInVertically { -it },
            exit = fadeOut() + slideOutVertically { -it }
        ) {
            DesktopSearchBar(
                searchQuery = state.searchQuery,
                onSearchChange = {
                    state.searchQuery = it
                    state.applyFiltersAndSort()
                },
                onClose = { showSearchBar = false },
                onClear = {
                    state.searchQuery = ""
                    state.applyFiltersAndSort()
                }
            )
        }

        // ============ BOŞ ALAN SAĞ TIK MENÜSÜ ============
        if (state.contextMenuTarget == null && state.contextMenuOffset != null) {
            Popup(
                onDismissRequest = { state.hideContextMenu() },
                offset = IntOffset(
                    state.contextMenuOffset!!.x.toInt(),
                    state.contextMenuOffset!!.y.toInt()
                )
            ) {
                DesktopEmptyContextMenu(
                    state = state,
                    onOpenWidget = { type ->
                        val id = "widget_${System.currentTimeMillis()}"
                        val item = DesktopGridItem(
                            id = id,
                            label = type.replaceFirstChar { it.uppercase() },
                            packageName = null,
                            isWidget = true,
                            widgetType = type,
                            iconRes = null
                        )
                        state.updateItems(state.allItems + item)
                        state.saveState(context)
                    },
                    onSort = { sort ->
                        state.sortBy = sort
                        state.applyFiltersAndSort()
                        state.saveState(context)
                    },
                    onDismiss = { state.hideContextMenu() }
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

// ==================== BAĞLAM MENÜSÜ ====================

@Composable
private fun ContextMenu(
    item: DesktopGridItem,
    state: DesktopGridState,
    context: Context,
    pm: android.content.pm.PackageManager,
    apps: List<DesktopApp>,
    onOpenWindow: (String, String) -> Unit,
    onDismiss: () -> Unit
) {
    val isDarkTheme = SystemState.theme == "dark"
    val backgroundColor = if (isDarkTheme) Color(0xFF2D2D2D) else Color(0xFFF3F3F3)
    val textColor = if (isDarkTheme) Color.White else Color.Black
    val dividerColor = if (isDarkTheme) Color(0xFF3D3D3D) else Color(0xFFE0E0E0)

    Popup(onDismissRequest = onDismiss) {
        Column(
            modifier = Modifier
                .shadow(16.dp, RoundedCornerShape(8.dp))
                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(8.dp))
                .background(backgroundColor)
                .width(200.dp)
                .padding(4.dp)
        ) {
            val isPinned = SystemState.pinnedApps.contains(item.packageName)
            val isOnDesktop = SystemState.desktopApps.contains(item.packageName)
            val selectedCount = state.selectedIds.size

            val menuItems = when {
                item.isSystem && item.id == "recycle_bin" -> listOf(
                    "Aç", "Çöp Kutusunu Boşalt", "Özellikler"
                )
                item.isFolder -> listOf(
                    "Aç", "Yeniden Adlandır", "Sil", "Özellikler"
                )
                item.isWidget -> listOf(
                    "Kaldır", "Yeniden Adlandır", "Özellikler"
                )
                selectedCount > 1 -> listOf(
                    "Seçilenleri Aç", "Seçilenleri Sil", "Seçilenleri Klasöre Taşı"
                )
                item.packageName != null -> listOf(
                    "Aç",
                    if (isPinned) "Görev Çubuğundan Kaldır" else "Görev Çubuğuna Sabitle",
                    if (isOnDesktop) "Masaüstünden Kaldır" else "Masaüstüne Ekle",
                    "Yeniden Adlandır",
                    "Uygulamayı Kaldır",
                    "Özellikler"
                )
                else -> listOf("Yeniden Adlandır", "Sil", "Özellikler")
            }

            menuItems.forEach { m ->
                val isDanger = m.contains("Sil") || m.contains("Kaldır") || m.contains("Boşalt")
                MenuItem(
                    text = m,
                    isDanger = isDanger,
                    textColor = textColor,
                    onClick = {
                        onDismiss()
                        when (m) {
                            "Aç" -> {
                                if (item.isSystem && item.id == "recycle_bin") {
                                    onOpenWindow("recycle_bin", "Geri Dönüşüm Kutusu")
                                } else if (item.packageName != null) {
                                    pm.getLaunchIntentForPackage(item.packageName)?.let { context.startActivity(it) }
                                }
                            }
                            "Görev Çubuğuna Sabitle" -> {
                                if (item.packageName != null) {
                                    apps.find { it.packageName == item.packageName }?.let {
                                        if (!SystemState.pinnedApps.contains(it.packageName)) {
                                            SystemState.pinApp(context, it.packageName)
                                            TaskbarState.pinApp(context, it)
                                        }
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                            "Görev Çubuğundan Kaldır" -> {
                                if (item.packageName != null) {
                                    apps.find { it.packageName == item.packageName }?.let {
                                        SystemState.unpinApp(context, it.packageName)
                                        TaskbarState.unpinApp(context, it)
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                            "Masaüstüne Ekle" -> {
                                if (item.packageName != null) {
                                    SystemState.addDesktopApp(context, item.packageName)
                                }
                            }
                            "Masaüstünden Kaldır" -> {
                                if (item.packageName != null) {
                                    SystemState.removeDesktopApp(context, item.packageName)
                                }
                                state.removePosition(item.id)
                                state.saveState(context)
                            }
                            "Yeniden Adlandır" -> {
                                state.startRenaming(item.id)
                            }
                            "Uygulamayı Kaldır" -> {
                                if (item.packageName != null) {
                                    context.startActivity(
                                        Intent(
                                            Settings.ACTION_APPLICATION_DETAILS_SETTINGS,
                                            Uri.parse("package:${item.packageName}")
                                        )
                                    )
                                }
                            }
                            "Özellikler" -> {
                                if (item.packageName != null) {
                                    context.startActivity(
                                        Intent(
                                            Settings.ACTION_APPLICATION_DETAILS_SETTINGS,
                                            Uri.parse("package:${item.packageName}")
                                        )
                                    )
                                }
                            }
                            "Seçilenleri Aç" -> {
                                state.selectedIds.forEach { id ->
                                    val pkg = state.getItem(id)?.packageName
                                    if (pkg != null) {
                                        pm.getLaunchIntentForPackage(pkg)?.let { context.startActivity(it) }
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                            "Seçilenleri Sil" -> {
                                state.selectedIds.forEach { id ->
                                    val pkg = state.getItem(id)?.packageName
                                    if (pkg != null) {
                                        SystemState.removeDesktopApp(context, pkg)
                                        state.removePosition(id)
                                    }
                                }
                                state.clearSelection()
                                state.saveState(context)
                            }
                            "Seçilenleri Klasöre Taşı" -> {
                                // Klasör oluştur (ileri düzey)
                            }
                            "Çöp Kutusunu Boşalt" -> {
                                SystemState.recycleBinItems.clear()
                            }
                            "Kaldır" -> {
                                if (item.isWidget) {
                                    state.updateItems(state.allItems.filter { it.id != item.id })
                                    state.saveState(context)
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                )
                if (menuItems.indexOf(m) < menuItems.size - 1) {
                    Divider(color = dividerColor, thickness = 0.5.dp)
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun MenuItem(
    text: String,
    isDanger: Boolean = false,
    textColor: Color,
    onClick: () -> Unit
) {
    Text(
        text = text,
        fontSize = 13.sp,
        color = if (isDanger) Color(0xFFE53935) else textColor,
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(4.dp))
            .clickable { onClick() }
            .padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 8.dp)
    )
}

// ==================== BOŞ ALAN MENÜSÜ ====================

@Composable
private fun DesktopEmptyContextMenu(
    state: DesktopGridState,
    onOpenWidget: (String) -> Unit,
    onSort: (String) -> Unit,
    onDismiss: () -> Unit
) {
    val isDarkTheme = SystemState.theme == "dark"
    val backgroundColor = if (isDarkTheme) Color(0xFF2D2D2D) else Color(0xFFF3F3F3)
    val textColor = if (isDarkTheme) Color.White else Color.Black
    val dividerColor = if (isDarkTheme) Color(0xFF3D3D3D) else Color(0xFFE0E0E0)

    Popup(onDismissRequest = onDismiss) {
        Column(
            modifier = Modifier
                .shadow(16.dp, RoundedCornerShape(8.dp))
                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(8.dp))
                .background(backgroundColor)
                .width(200.dp)
                .padding(4.dp)
        ) {
            val items = listOf(
                "Yenile",
                "---",
                "Sırala (Ada Göre)" to { onSort("name") },
                "Sırala (Türe Göre)" to { onSort("type") },
                "Sırala (Tarihe Göre)" to { onSort("date") },
                "---",
                "Yeni Klasör",
                "Yapıştır",
                "---",
                "Widget Ekle → Saat" to { onOpenWidget("clock") },
                "Widget Ekle → Hava Durumu" to { onOpenWidget("weather") },
                "Widget Ekle → Notlar" to { onOpenWidget("notes") },
                "Widget Ekle → Sistem Monitörü" to { onOpenWidget("system_monitor") },
                "Widget Ekle → Takvim" to { onOpenWidget("calendar") },
                "---",
                "Gizli öğeleri göster" to { state.showHidden = !state.showHidden; state.applyFiltersAndSort() },
                "Terminali Aç",
                "Görev Yöneticisi",
                "Kişiselleştir"
            )

            items.forEach { item ->
                when (item) {
                    "---" -> Divider(color = dividerColor, thickness = 0.5.dp)
                    else -> {
                        val label = if (item is Pair<*, *>) item.first.toString() else item.toString()
                        val action = if (item is Pair<*, *>) item.second as? () -> Unit else null
                        Text(
                            text = label,
                            fontSize = 13.sp,
                            color = textColor,
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .fillMaxWidth()
                                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(4.dp))
                                .clickable {
                                    onDismiss()
                                    when (label) {
                                        "Yenile" -> state.applyFiltersAndSort()
                                        "Yeni Klasör" -> {}
                                        "Yapıştır" -> {}
                                        "Terminali Aç" -> WindowState.openWindow(
                                            id = "terminal_${System.currentTimeMillis()}",
                                            title = "Terminal",
                                            contentId = "cmd",
                                            width = 650,
                                            height = 380
                                        )
                                        "Görev Yöneticisi" -> {}
                                        "Kişiselleştir" -> {}
                                        else -> action?.invoke()
                                    }
                                }
                                .padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 8.dp)
                        )
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

// ==================== ARAMA ÇUBUĞU ====================

@Composable
private fun DesktopSearchBar(
    searchQuery: String,
    onSearchChange: (String) -> Unit,
    onClose: () -> Unit,
    onClear: () -> Unit
) {
    Row(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .background(Color(0xCC000000))
            .padding(16.dp),
        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.Center
    ) {
        Surface(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth(0.6f)
                .height(48.dp)
                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(24.dp)),
            color = Color.White,
            shadowElevation = 8.dp
        ) {
            Row(
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 16.dp)
            ) {
                Icon(Icons.Default.Search, contentDescription = null, tint = Color.Gray)
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(8.dp))
                BasicTextField(
                    value = searchQuery,
                    onValueChange = onSearchChange,
                    modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                    textStyle = TextStyle(color = Color.Black, fontSize = 16.sp),
                    singleLine = true,
                    keyboardOptions = KeyboardOptions(imeAction = ImeAction.Search),
                    keyboardActions = KeyboardActions(
                        onSearch = { onClose() }
                    )
                )
                if (searchQuery.isNotEmpty()) {
                    Icon(
                        Icons.Default.Clear,
                        contentDescription = "Temizle",
                        tint = Color.Gray,
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .size(20.dp)
                            .clickable { onClear() }
                    )
                }
                Icon(
                    Icons.Default.Close,
                    contentDescription = "Kapat",
                    tint = Color.Gray,
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .size(20.dp)
                        .clickable { onClose() }
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

// ==================== YARDIMCI FONKSİYONLAR ====================

private fun buildItems(
    apps: List<DesktopApp>,
    context: Context,
    pm: android.content.pm.PackageManager
): List<DesktopGridItem> {
    val items = mutableListOf<DesktopGridItem>()

    val isRecycleEmpty = SystemState.recycleBinItems.isEmpty()
    items.add(
        DesktopGridItem(
            id = "recycle_bin",
            label = "Geri Dönüşüm Kutusu",
            packageName = null,
            isSystem = true,
            iconRes = if (isRecycleEmpty) R.drawable.recycle_bin_empty else R.drawable.recycle_bin_full
        )
    )

    apps.filter { SystemState.desktopApps.contains(it.packageName) }.forEach { app ->
        items.add(
            DesktopGridItem(
                id = app.packageName,
                label = DesktopState.customNames[app.packageName] ?: app.label,
                packageName = app.packageName,
                iconBitmap = app.iconBitmap
            )
        )
    }

    return items
}

package com.windowsui

import android.content.Context

import androidx.compose.foundation.background

import androidx.compose.foundation.clickable

import androidx.compose.foundation.interaction.MutableInteractionSource

/**
 * FileExplorerContent – Windows Gezgini benzeri dosya yöneticisi.
 * Tüm dosyalar için gerçek önizleme (thumbnail) gösterir.
 * Harici drawable resim dosyalarına ihtiyaç duymaz.
 *
 * Özellikler:
 * - Çoklu sekme desteği (Windows 11 tarzı)
 * - Dosya/klasör listesi (detay, simge görünümü)
 * - Adres çubuğu ve gezinme (ileri/geri/yukarı/yenile)
 * - Sol gezinti paneli (Hızlı Erişim, Bu Bilgisayar, Kök)
 * - Sağ tık menüsü (Aç, Kopyala, Kes, Yapıştır, Sil, Yeniden Adlandır, Yeni Klasör, Sıkıştır, Özellikler)
 * - Kopyala / Kes / Yapıştır
 * - Dosya önizleme (resim, video, APK, PDF, metin) – otomatik thumbnail
 * - Dosya arama ve filtreleme
 * - Root ile sistem dosyalarına erişim
 * - USB OTG cihazlarını gösterme
 * - ZIP sıkıştırma ve çıkarma
 * - Dosya izinlerini değiştirme (chmod) – root ile
 * - Koyu/açık tema desteği
 * - Klavye kısayolları
 * - İlerleme göstergesi
 */
@OptIn(ExperimentalAnimationApi::class)
@Composable
fun FileExplorerContent(
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    onClose: () -> Unit
) {
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val focusManager = LocalFocusManager.current
    val scope = rememberCoroutineScope()
    val density = LocalDensity.current
    val isDarkTheme = SystemState.theme == "dark"

    // ============ STATE ============
    val gridState = rememberLazyGridState()
    var currentPath by remember { mutableStateOf("/storage/emulated/0") }
    var fileList by remember { mutableStateOf<List<File>>(emptyList()) }
    var filteredFileList by remember { mutableStateOf<List<File>>(emptyList()) }
    var pathHistory by remember { mutableStateOf(listOf(currentPath)) }
    var historyIndex by remember { mutableStateOf(0) }

    var selectedFiles by remember { mutableStateOf<Set<String>>(emptySet()) }
    var clipboardFiles by remember { mutableStateOf<List<String>>(emptyList()) }
    var isCutOperation by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }

    var viewMode by remember { mutableStateOf("details") }
    var sortBy by remember { mutableStateOf("name") }
    var sortAscending by remember { mutableStateOf(true) }
    var searchQuery by remember { mutableStateOf("") }

    var contextMenuOffset by remember { mutableStateOf<Offset?>(null) }
    var contextMenuTarget by remember { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) }

    var showRenameDialog by remember { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) }
    var showNewFolderDialog by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var showPermissionsDialog by remember { mutableStateOf<File?>(null) }
    var showProgressDialog by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var progressMessage by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var progressValue by remember { mutableStateOf(0f) }

    var inputText by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var permissionMode by remember { mutableStateOf("755") }

    var tabs by remember { mutableStateOf(listOf("/storage/emulated/0")) }
    var activeTabIndex by remember { mutableStateOf(0) }
    var isRootMode by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }

    // ============ TEMEL FONKSİYONLAR ============

    fun applyFiltersAndSort() {
        var list = fileList
        if (searchQuery.isNotEmpty()) {
            list = list.filter { it.name.contains(searchQuery, ignoreCase = true) }
        }
        list = when (sortBy) {
            "name" -> if (sortAscending) list.sortedBy { it.name.lowercase() } else list.sortedByDescending { it.name.lowercase() }
            "date" -> if (sortAscending) list.sortedBy { it.lastModified() } else list.sortedByDescending { it.lastModified() }
            "size" -> if (sortAscending) list.sortedBy { it.length() } else list.sortedByDescending { it.length() }
            "type" -> if (sortAscending) list.sortedBy { it.extension.lowercase() } else list.sortedByDescending { it.extension.lowercase() }
            else -> list
        }
        filteredFileList = list
    }

    suspend fun listFilesWithRoot(path: String): List<File> = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val files = mutableListOf<File>()
        try {
            val dir = File(path)
            if (dir.canRead() && dir.listFiles() != null) {
                files.addAll(dir.listFiles()!!)
            } else {
                val p = Runtime.getRuntime().exec(arrayOf("su", "-c", "ls -1a '$path'"))
                val reader = BufferedReader(InputStreamReader(p.inputStream))
                var line: String?
                while (reader.readLine().also { line = it } != null) {
                    if (line != "." && line != "..") files.add(File(dir, line!!))
                }
                p.waitFor()
            }
        } catch (e: Exception) {}
        files.sortedWith(compareBy({ !it.isDirectory }, { it.name.lowercase(Locale.getDefault()) }))
    }

    fun loadFiles(path: String) {
        scope.launch {
            try {
                val result = listFilesWithRoot(path)
                withContext(Dispatchers.Main) {
                    fileList = result
                    applyFiltersAndSort()
                    currentPath = path
                    if (pathHistory.lastOrNull() != path) {
                        pathHistory = pathHistory.take(historyIndex + 1) + path
                        historyIndex = pathHistory.size - 1
                    }
                    selectedFiles = emptySet()
                }
            } catch (e: Exception) {}
        }
    }

    LaunchedEffect(Unit) {
        loadFiles(currentPath)
    }

    fun navigateTo(path: String) {
        if (path.isNotEmpty()) {
            loadFiles(path)
            if (activeTabIndex < tabs.size) {
                tabs = tabs.toMutableList().apply { set(activeTabIndex, path) }
            }
        }
    }

    fun navigateBack() {
        if (historyIndex > 0) {
            historyIndex--
            loadFiles(pathHistory[historyIndex])
        }
    }

    fun navigateForward() {
        if (historyIndex < pathHistory.size - 1) {
            historyIndex++
            loadFiles(pathHistory[historyIndex])
        }
    }

    fun navigateUp() {
        val parent = File(currentPath).parentFile
        if (parent != null) {
            navigateTo(parent.absolutePath)
        }
    }

    fun refresh() {
        loadFiles(currentPath)
    }

    fun getMimeType(file: File): String {
        val ext = file.extension.lowercase(Locale.getDefault())
        return MimeTypeMap.getSingleton().getMimeTypeFromExtension(ext) ?: "*/*"
    }

    fun formatSize(size: Long): String {
        if (size <= 0) return "0 B"
        val units = arrayOf("B", "KB", "MB", "GB", "TB")
        val digitGroups = (Math.log10(size.toDouble()) / Math.log10(1024.0)).toInt()
        return String.format(Locale.US, "%.1f %s", size / Math.pow(1024.0, digitGroups.toDouble()), units[digitGroups])
    }

    // ============ DOSYA İŞLEMLERİ ============

    suspend fun createFolder(path: String, name: String) = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val f = File(path, name)
        try {
            if (!f.mkdirs()) {
                Runtime.getRuntime().exec(arrayOf("su", "-c", "mkdir -p '${f.absolutePath}'")).waitFor()
            }
        } catch (e: Exception) {}
    }

    suspend fun renameFile(path: String, newName: String) = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val f = File(path)
        val dest = File(f.parent, newName)
        try {
            if (!f.renameTo(dest)) {
                Runtime.getRuntime().exec(arrayOf("su", "-c", "mv '$path' '${dest.absolutePath}'")).waitFor()
            }
        } catch (e: Exception) {}
    }

    suspend fun deleteFiles(sources: Set<String>) = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        sources.forEach { src ->
            try {
                val f = File(src)
                if (!f.deleteRecursively()) {
                    Runtime.getRuntime().exec(arrayOf("su", "-c", "rm -rf '$src'")).waitFor()
                }
            } catch (e: Exception) {}
        }
    }

    suspend fun pasteFiles(sources: List<String>, destDir: String, isCut: Boolean) = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        sources.forEach { src ->
            val srcFile = File(src)
            val destFile = File(destDir, srcFile.name)
            try {
                if (isCut) {
                    if (!srcFile.renameTo(destFile)) {
                        srcFile.copyRecursively(destFile, true)
                        srcFile.deleteRecursively()
                    }
                } else {
                    srcFile.copyRecursively(destFile, true)
                }
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                val cmd = if (isCut) "mv '$src' '$destDir/'" else "cp -r '$src' '$destDir/'"
                Runtime.getRuntime().exec(arrayOf("su", "-c", cmd)).waitFor()
            }
        }
    }

    suspend fun changePermissions(file: File, mode: String) = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        try {
            Runtime.getRuntime().exec(arrayOf("su", "-c", "chmod $mode '${file.absolutePath}'")).waitFor()
        } catch (e: Exception) {}
    }

    suspend fun mountSystemRW() = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        try {
            Runtime.getRuntime().exec(arrayOf("su", "-c", "mount -o rw,remount /system")).waitFor()
        } catch (e: Exception) {}
    }

    suspend fun compressFiles(sources: Set<String>, destName: String) = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val zipFile = File(currentPath, destName)
        ZipOutputStream(FileOutputStream(zipFile)).use { zos ->
            sources.forEach { src ->
                val f = File(src)
                if (f.isDirectory) {
                    f.walk().forEach { file ->
                        val entryName = file.absolutePath.removePrefix(f.parentFile?.absolutePath ?: "").trimStart('/')
                        zos.putNextEntry(ZipEntry(entryName))
                        if (file.isFile) file.inputStream().use { it.copyTo(zos) }
                        zos.closeEntry()
                    }
                } else {
                    zos.putNextEntry(ZipEntry(f.name))
                    f.inputStream().use { it.copyTo(zos) }
                    zos.closeEntry()
                }
            }
        }
    }

    suspend fun extractZip(zipFile: File, destDir: String) = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        ZipFile(zipFile).use { zip ->
            zip.entries().toList().forEach { entry ->
                val file = File(destDir, entry.name)
                if (entry.isDirectory) {
                    file.mkdirs()
                } else {
                    file.parentFile?.mkdirs()
                    zip.getInputStream(entry).use { input ->
                        file.outputStream().use { output ->
                            input.copyTo(output)
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    fun openFile(file: File) {
        try {
            val uri = FileProvider.getUriForFile(context, "${context.packageName}.provider", file)
            val intent = Intent(Intent.ACTION_VIEW).apply {
                setDataAndType(uri, getMimeType(file))
                addFlags(Intent.FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION)
                addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK)
            }
            context.startActivity(Intent.createChooser(intent, "Birlikte Aç"))
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            try {
                val intent = Intent(Intent.ACTION_VIEW).apply {
                    setDataAndType(Uri.parse("file://${file.absolutePath}"), getMimeType(file))
                    addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK)
                }
                context.startActivity(Intent.createChooser(intent, "Birlikte Aç"))
            } catch (ex: Exception) {
                Toast.makeText(context, "Dosya açılamadı", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
            }
        }
    }

    // ============ YARDIMCI ============

    fun isRootPath(path: String): Boolean {
        return path.startsWith("/data") || path.startsWith("/system") || path.startsWith("/vendor") || path == "/"
    }

    // ============ ANA UI ============

    Box(modifier = modifier.fillMaxSize().background(
        if (isDarkTheme) Color(0xFF1A1A1A) else Color.White

                                    tint = Color.Gray
                                )
                            }
                        }

                }
                IconButton(onClick = {
                    tabs = tabs + currentPath
                    activeTabIndex = tabs.size - 1
                }) {

                    Icon(Icons.Default.Close, contentDescription = "Kapat", tint = if (isDarkTheme) Color.White else Color.Black)

                    .fillMaxWidth()

                    .padding(horizontal = 8.dp, vertical = 4.dp),

            ) {
                IconButton(onClick = { navigateBack() }, enabled = historyIndex > 0) {
                    Icon(Icons.Default.ArrowBack, contentDescription = "Geri", tint = if (historyIndex > 0) Color.White else Color.Gray)
                }
                IconButton(onClick = { navigateForward() }, enabled = historyIndex < pathHistory.size - 1) {
                    Icon(Icons.Default.ArrowForward, contentDescription = "İleri", tint = if (historyIndex < pathHistory.size - 1) Color.White else Color.Gray)
                }
                IconButton(onClick = { navigateUp() }) {
                    Icon(Icons.Default.KeyboardArrowUp, contentDescription = "Yukarı", tint = Color.White)
                }
                IconButton(onClick = { refresh() }) {
                    Icon(Icons.Default.Refresh, contentDescription = "Yenile", tint = Color.White)
                }

                Row(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .weight(1f)
                        .height(32.dp)
                        .border(1.dp, if (isDarkTheme) Color.DarkGray else Color.LightGray, RoundedCornerShape(4.dp))
                        .background(if (isDarkTheme) Color(0xFF1A1A1A) else Color.White, RoundedCornerShape(4.dp))
                        .padding(horizontal = 8.dp),
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                ) {
                    Icon(Icons.Default.Home, contentDescription = null, modifier = Modifier.size(16.dp), tint = Color.Gray)
                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(8.dp))
                    BasicTextField(
                        value = currentPath,
                        onValueChange = {},
                        modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                        textStyle = TextStyle(color = if (isDarkTheme) Color.White else Color.Black, fontSize = 13.sp),
                        enabled = false,

                        if (viewMode == "details") Icons.Default.List else Icons.Default.GridView,

                        tint = Color.White
                    )
                }

                var showSearch by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
                IconButton(onClick = { showSearch = !showSearch }) {
                    Icon(Icons.Default.Search, contentDescription = "Ara", tint = Color.White)

                        modifier = Modifier
                            .width(150.dp)
                            .height(32.dp)

                            .padding(horizontal = 8.dp),
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                    ) {
                        Icon(Icons.Default.Search, contentDescription = null, modifier = Modifier.size(16.dp), tint = Color.Gray)
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(4.dp))
                        BasicTextField(
                            value = searchQuery,
                            onValueChange = { searchQuery = it; applyFiltersAndSort() },

                        modifier = Modifier

                                    Toast.makeText(context, "Root modu aktif", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                                } else {

                                }

                        Text("Root", color = Color.White, fontSize = 10.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                    }
                }

            Row(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {

                    modifier = Modifier
                        .width(180.dp)

                        fontSize = 11.sp,

                        "İndirilenler" to "${Environment.getExternalStorageDirectory()}/Download",

                        "Resimler" to "${Environment.getExternalStorageDirectory()}/Pictures",
                        "Müzik" to "${Environment.getExternalStorageDirectory()}/Music",

                    )
                    quickAccess.forEach { (name, path) ->
                        QuickAccessItem(
                            name = name,
                            path = path,
                            isCurrent = currentPath == path,
                            isDarkTheme = isDarkTheme,
                            onClick = { navigateTo(path) }
                        )
                    }

                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(16.dp))
                    Text(
                        "Bu Bilgisayar",
                        fontSize = 11.sp,
                        color = if (isDarkTheme) Color.LightGray else Color.Gray,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                        modifier = Modifier.padding(start = 8.dp, bottom = 8.dp)
                    )
                    listOf(
                        "Yerel Disk (C:)" to context.filesDir.absolutePath,
                        "Telefon (D:)" to "/storage/emulated/0",
                        "Sistem Kökü (/)" to "/"
                    ).forEach { (name, path) ->
                        QuickAccessItem(
                            name = name,
                            path = path,
                            isCurrent = currentPath == path,
                            isDarkTheme = isDarkTheme,
                            onClick = { navigateTo(path) }

                        )

                                name = device.productName ?: "USB Aygıt",

                // DOSYA LİSTESİ
                Column(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .weight(1f)
                        .fillMaxHeight()

                        .pointerInput("EmptySpaceRightClick") {
                            awaitPointerEventScope {
                                while (true) {
                                    val event = awaitPointerEvent(PointerEventPass.Initial)
                                    if (event.type == PointerEventType.Press && event.buttons.isSecondaryPressed) {
                                        event.changes.forEach { it.consume() }
                                        contextMenuTarget = null
                                        contextMenuOffset = event.changes.first().position
                                        selectedFiles = emptySet()
                                    }

                ) {
                    if (viewMode == "details") {

                            modifier = Modifier

                            Text("Tür", fontSize = 12.sp, color = if (isDarkTheme) Color.LightGray else Color.Gray, modifier = Modifier.weight(1f))
                            Text("Boyut", fontSize = 12.sp, color = if (isDarkTheme) Color.LightGray else Color.Gray, modifier = Modifier.weight(1f), textAlign = TextAlign.End)

                                Text("İzinler", fontSize = 12.sp, color = Color.LightGray, modifier = Modifier.weight(1f), textAlign = TextAlign.End)
                            }

                            if (isDarkTheme) Color(0xFF3D3D3D) else Color(0xFFE0E0E0)

                    if (filteredFileList.isEmpty()) {

                            Column(horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally) {

                                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(8.dp))
                                Text("Bu klasör boş", color = if (isDarkTheme) Color.LightGray else Color.Gray, fontSize = 14.sp)
                            }
                        }
                    } else {
                        if (viewMode == "details") {
                            LazyVerticalGrid(
                                columns = GridCells.Fixed(1),
                                state = gridState,
                                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
                            ) {
                                items(filteredFileList) { file ->
                                    FileItemDetailsWithThumbnail(
                                        file = file,
                                        isSelected = selectedFiles.contains(file.absolutePath),
                                        isDarkTheme = isDarkTheme,
                                        isRootMode = isRootMode,
                                        onSelect = { selectedFiles = if (selectedFiles.contains(file.absolutePath)) selectedFiles - file.absolutePath else selectedFiles + file.absolutePath },
                                        onOpen = { if (file.isDirectory) navigateTo(file.absolutePath) else openFile(file) },
                                        onRightClick = { offset ->
                                            selectedFiles = setOf(file.absolutePath)
                                            contextMenuTarget = file.absolutePath
                                            contextMenuOffset = offset
                                        },
                                        onDoubleClick = { if (file.isDirectory) navigateTo(file.absolutePath) else openFile(file) }
                                    )
                                }
                            }
                        } else {
                            LazyVerticalGrid(
                                columns = GridCells.Adaptive(minSize = 80.dp),
                                state = gridState,
                                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize().padding(8.dp),
                                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp),
                                verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)
                            ) {
                                items(filteredFileList) { file ->
                                    FileItemIconWithThumbnail(
                                        file = file,
                                        isSelected = selectedFiles.contains(file.absolutePath),
                                        isDarkTheme = isDarkTheme,
                                        onSelect = { selectedFiles = if (selectedFiles.contains(file.absolutePath)) selectedFiles - file.absolutePath else selectedFiles + file.absolutePath },
                                        onOpen = { if (file.isDirectory) navigateTo(file.absolutePath) else openFile(file) },
                                        onRightClick = { offset ->
                                            selectedFiles = setOf(file.absolutePath)
                                            contextMenuTarget = file.absolutePath
                                            contextMenuOffset = offset
                                        },
                                        onDoubleClick = { if (file.isDirectory) navigateTo(file.absolutePath) else openFile(file) }
                                    )
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // BAĞLAM MENÜSÜ
        if (contextMenuOffset != null) {
            FileExplorerContextMenu(
                offset = contextMenuOffset!!,
                target = contextMenuTarget,
                selectedFiles = selectedFiles,
                isDarkTheme = isDarkTheme,
                onDismiss = { contextMenuOffset = null },
                onOpen = {
                    contextMenuTarget?.let {
                        val f = File(it)
                        if (f.isDirectory) navigateTo(f.absolutePath) else openFile(f)
                    }
                    contextMenuOffset = null
                },
                onRefresh = { refresh(); contextMenuOffset = null },
                onNewFolder = { showNewFolderDialog = true; contextMenuOffset = null },
                onRename = {
                    contextMenuTarget?.let { showRenameDialog = it; inputText = File(it).name }
                    contextMenuOffset = null
                },
                onCopy = {
                    clipboardFiles = selectedFiles.toList()
                    isCutOperation = false
                    Toast.makeText(context, "${selectedFiles.size} dosya kopyalandı", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                    contextMenuOffset = null
                },
                onCut = {
                    clipboardFiles = selectedFiles.toList()
                    isCutOperation = true
                    Toast.makeText(context, "${selectedFiles.size} dosya kesildi", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                    contextMenuOffset = null
                },
                onPaste = {
                    if (clipboardFiles.isNotEmpty()) {
                        scope.launch {
                            showProgressDialog = true
                            progressMessage = "Yapıştırılıyor..."
                            progressValue = 0f
                            pasteFiles(clipboardFiles, currentPath, isCutOperation)
                            if (isCutOperation) clipboardFiles = emptyList()
                            showProgressDialog = false
                            loadFiles(currentPath)
                        }
                    }
                    contextMenuOffset = null
                },
                onDelete = {
                    scope.launch {
                        showProgressDialog = true
                        progressMessage = "Siliniyor..."
                        progressValue = 0f
                        deleteFiles(selectedFiles)
                        showProgressDialog = false
                        selectedFiles = emptySet()
                        loadFiles(currentPath)
                    }
                    contextMenuOffset = null
                },
                onCompress = {
                    if (selectedFiles.isNotEmpty()) {
                        val zipName = if (selectedFiles.size == 1) File(selectedFiles.first()).name + ".zip" else "archive.zip"
                        scope.launch {
                            showProgressDialog = true
                            progressMessage = "Sıkıştırılıyor..."
                            progressValue = 0f
                            compressFiles(selectedFiles, zipName)
                            showProgressDialog = false
                            loadFiles(currentPath)
                        }
                    }
                    contextMenuOffset = null
                },
                onPermissions = {
                    contextMenuTarget?.let { showPermissionsDialog = File(it); inputText = "" }
                    contextMenuOffset = null
                },
                onProperties = {
                    contextMenuTarget?.let { targetPath ->
                        val f = File(targetPath)
                        val info = buildString {
                            append("Ad: ${f.name}\n")
                            append("Yol: ${f.absolutePath}\n")
                            append("Boyut: ${formatSize(f.length())}\n")
                            append("Son değişiklik: ${SimpleDateFormat("dd.MM.yyyy HH:mm", Locale.getDefault()).format(Date(f.lastModified()))}\n")
                            append("Okunabilir: ${f.canRead()}\n")
                            append("Yazılabilir: ${f.canWrite()}\n")
                            append("Çalıştırılabilir: ${f.canExecute()}\n")
                            append("Klasör mü: ${f.isDirectory}\n")
                            append("Gizli mi: ${f.isHidden}")
                        }
                        Toast.makeText(context, info, Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show()
                    }
                    contextMenuOffset = null
                },
                onMountRW = {
                    scope.launch { mountSystemRW(); loadFiles(currentPath) }
                    contextMenuOffset = null
                }
            )
        }

        // DİALOG'LAR
        if (showNewFolderDialog || showRenameDialog != null) {
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxSize()
                    .background(Color(0x66000000))
                    .clickable { showNewFolderDialog = false; showRenameDialog = null },
                contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
            ) {
                Column(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .width(300.dp)
                        .shadow(24.dp, RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
                        .background(if (isDarkTheme) Color(0xFF2D2D2D) else Color.White)
                        .clickable { }
                        .padding(16.dp)
                ) {
                    Text(
                        if (showNewFolderDialog) "Yeni Klasör" else "Yeniden Adlandır",
                        fontSize = 16.sp,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                        color = if (isDarkTheme) Color.White else Color.Black
                    )
                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(12.dp))
                    BasicTextField(
                        value = inputText,
                        onValueChange = { inputText = it },
                        textStyle = TextStyle(color = if (isDarkTheme) Color.White else Color.Black, fontSize = 14.sp),
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .border(1.dp, Color(0xFF0078D7), RoundedCornerShape(4.dp))
                            .padding(8.dp),
                        singleLine = true
                    )
                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(16.dp))
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.End
                    ) {
                        Text("İptal", color = if (isDarkTheme) Color.LightGray else Color.Black, modifier = Modifier.clickable { showNewFolderDialog = false; showRenameDialog = null }.padding(8.dp))
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(16.dp))
                        Text("Tamam", color = Color(0xFF0078D7), fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, modifier = Modifier.clickable {
                            scope.launch {
                                if (showNewFolderDialog) createFolder(currentPath, inputText)
                                else showRenameDialog?.let { renameFile(it, inputText) }
                                showNewFolderDialog = false; showRenameDialog = null; loadFiles(currentPath)
                            }
                        }.padding(8.dp))
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        if (showPermissionsDialog != null) {
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxSize()
                    .background(Color(0x66000000))
                    .clickable { showPermissionsDialog = null },
                contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
            ) {
                Column(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .width(300.dp)
                        .shadow(24.dp, RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
                        .background(if (isDarkTheme) Color(0xFF2D2D2D) else Color.White)
                        .clickable { }
                        .padding(16.dp)
                ) {
                    Text("İzin Değiştir", fontSize = 16.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = if (isDarkTheme) Color.White else Color.Black)
                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(12.dp))
                    BasicTextField(
                        value = permissionMode,
                        onValueChange = { permissionMode = it },
                        textStyle = TextStyle(color = if (isDarkTheme) Color.White else Color.Black, fontSize = 14.sp),
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .border(1.dp, Color(0xFF0078D7), RoundedCornerShape(4.dp))
                            .padding(8.dp),
                        singleLine = true
                    )
                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(16.dp))
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.End
                    ) {
                        Text("İptal", color = if (isDarkTheme) Color.LightGray else Color.Black, modifier = Modifier.clickable { showPermissionsDialog = null }.padding(8.dp))
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(16.dp))
                        Text("Uygula", color = Color(0xFF0078D7), fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, modifier = Modifier.clickable {
                            scope.launch {
                                showPermissionsDialog?.let { changePermissions(it, permissionMode) }
                                showPermissionsDialog = null; loadFiles(currentPath)
                            }
                        }.padding(8.dp))
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        if (showProgressDialog) {
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxSize()
                    .background(Color(0x66000000)),
                contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
            ) {
                Card(
                    modifier = Modifier.width(300.dp).padding(16.dp),
                    colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(
                        containerColor = if (isDarkTheme) Color(0xFF2D2D2D) else Color.White
                    )
                ) {
                    Column(
                        modifier = Modifier.padding(16.dp),
                        horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally
                    ) {
                        CircularProgressIndicator()
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(16.dp))
                        Text(progressMessage, color = if (isDarkTheme) Color.White else Color.Black)
                        if (progressValue > 0f) {
                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(8.dp))
                            LinearProgressIndicator(progress = progressValue, modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth())
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

// ==================== YARDIMCI BİLEŞENLER ====================

@Composable
private fun QuickAccessItem(
    name: String,
    path: String,
    isCurrent: Boolean,
    isDarkTheme: Boolean,
    onClick: () -> Unit
) {
    Row(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(4.dp))
            .background(
                if (isCurrent) {
                    if (isDarkTheme) Color(0xFF3A3A3A) else Color(0xFFE5F3FF)
                } else Color.Transparent
            )
            .clickable { onClick() }
            .padding(horizontal = 8.dp, vertical = 6.dp),
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
    ) {
        Icon(
            when {
                name.contains("Masaüstü") -> Icons.Default.Home
                name.contains("İndir") -> Icons.Default.Download
                name.contains("Belge") -> Icons.Default.DocumentScanner
                name.contains("Resim") -> Icons.Default.Image
                name.contains("Müzik") -> Icons.Default.MusicNote
                name.contains("Video") -> Icons.Default.VideoFile
                name.contains("C:") -> Icons.Default.Storage
