    private fun shellQuote(value: String): String {
        if (value.isEmpty()) return "''"
        return "'" + value.replace("'", "'\\''") + "'"
    }

    // ==================== YARDIMCI FONKSİYONLAR (Terminal ile uyumlu) ====================

    suspend fun getDevices(context: Context, root: Boolean = false): List<String> {
        return execute(context, listOf("devices"), root)
    }

    suspend fun enableRoot(context: Context): List<String> {
        return execute(context, listOf("root"), true)
    }

    suspend fun shell(context: Context, command: String, root: Boolean = false): List<String> {
        return execute(context, listOf("shell", command), root)
    }

    suspend fun push(context: Context, local: String, remote: String, root: Boolean = false): List<String> {
        return execute(context, listOf("push", local, remote), root)
    }

    suspend fun pull(context: Context, remote: String, local: String, root: Boolean = false): List<String> {
        return execute(context, listOf("pull", remote, local), root)
    }

    suspend fun backup(context: Context, output: String = "/sdcard/backup.ab", root: Boolean = false): List<String> {
        return execute(context, listOf("backup", "-apk", "-shared", "-all", "-system", "-f", output), root)
    }

    suspend fun restore(context: Context, input: String = "/sdcard/backup.ab", root: Boolean = false): List<String> {
        return execute(context, listOf("restore", input), root)
    }

    /**
     * Ekran kaydı başlatır. Varsayılan olarak 30 saniye kaydeder.
     * Arka planda çalıştırmak için `--background` eklenebilir.
     */
    suspend fun screenrecord(
        context: Context,
        output: String = "/sdcard/record.mp4",
        timeLimit: Int = 30,
        background: Boolean = false,
        root: Boolean = false
    ): List<String> {
        val args = mutableListOf("shell", "screenrecord")
        if (background) args.add("--background")
        args.add("--time-limit")
        args.add(timeLimit.toString())
        args.add(output)
        return execute(context, args, root)
    }

    /**
     * Ekran görüntüsü alır.
     */
    suspend fun screenshot(context: Context, output: String = "/sdcard/screenshot.png", root: Boolean = false): List<String> {
        return execute(context, listOf("shell", "screencap", "-p", output), root)
    }

    suspend fun install(context: Context, apkPath: String, root: Boolean = false): List<String> {
        return execute(context, listOf("install", "-r", apkPath), root)
    }

    suspend fun uninstall(context: Context, packageName: String, root: Boolean = false): List<String> {
        return execute(context, listOf("uninstall", packageName), root)
    }

    /**
     * Logcat çıktısını alır.
     * `-d` ile tek seferlik döküm alır ve çıkar.
     * Filtre eklenebilir (örnek: "ActivityManager:I *:S")
     */
    suspend fun logcat(context: Context, filter: String = "", dumpOnce: Boolean = true, root: Boolean = false): List<String> {
        val args = mutableListOf("logcat")
        if (dumpOnce) args.add("-d")
        if (filter.isNotEmpty()) args.add(filter)
        return execute(context, args, root)
    }

    suspend fun reboot(context: Context, mode: String = "", root: Boolean = false): List<String> {
        val args = mutableListOf("reboot")
        if (mode.isNotEmpty()) args.add(mode)
        return execute(context, args, root)
    }

    suspend fun rebootBootloader(context: Context, root: Boolean = false): List<String> {
        return execute(context, listOf("reboot", "bootloader"), root)
    }

    suspend fun sideload(context: Context, otaFile: String, root: Boolean = false): List<String> {
        return execute(context, listOf("sideload", otaFile), root)
    }

    suspend fun bugreport(context: Context, output: String = "/sdcard/bugreport.zip", root: Boolean = false): List<String> {
        return execute(context, listOf("bugreport", output), root)
    }

    suspend fun getProperties(context: Context, root: Boolean = false): List<String> {
        return execute(context, listOf("shell", "getprop"), root)
    }

    suspend fun setSystemSetting(context: Context, namespace: String, key: String, value: String, root: Boolean = false): List<String> {
        return execute(context, listOf("shell", "settings", "put", namespace, key, value), root)
    }

    suspend fun rootShell(context: Context, command: String): List<String> {
        return execute(context, listOf("shell", "su", "-c", command), true)
    }

    // ==================== ADB OVER Wi-Fi ====================

    suspend fun connectWifi(context: Context, ip: String, port: Int = 5555, root: Boolean = false): List<String> {
        return execute(context, listOf("connect", "$ip:$port"), root)
    }

    suspend fun disconnectWifi(context: Context, ip: String = "", root: Boolean = false): List<String> {
        val args = if (ip.isNotEmpty()) listOf("disconnect", ip) else listOf("disconnect")
        return execute(context, args, root)
    }

    // ==================== FASTBOOT KOMUTLARI ====================

    suspend fun fastbootDevices(context: Context): List<String> {
        return executeFastboot(context, listOf("devices"))
    }

    suspend fun fastbootOemUnlock(context: Context): List<String> {
        return executeFastboot(context, listOf("oem", "unlock"))
    }

    suspend fun fastbootFlash(context: Context, partition: String, file: String): List<String> {
        return executeFastboot(context, listOf("flash", partition, file))
    }

    suspend fun fastbootReboot(context: Context): List<String> {
        return executeFastboot(context, listOf("reboot"))
    }

    suspend fun fastbootErase(context: Context, partition: String): List<String> {
        return executeFastboot(context, listOf("erase", partition))
    }

    suspend fun fastbootGetvar(context: Context, varName: String): List<String> {
        return executeFastboot(context, listOf("getvar", varName))
    }

    suspend fun fastbootOemDeviceInfo(context: Context): List<String> {
        return executeFastboot(context, listOf("oem", "device-info"))
    }

    // ==================== GELİŞMİŞ / ÖZEL KOMUTLAR ====================

    /**
     * ADB shell ile cihaza dosya yazar (root gerektirir).
     */
    suspend fun writeFile(context: Context, remotePath: String, content: String, root: Boolean = true): List<String> {
        val escaped = content.replace("'", "'\\''")
        return execute(context, listOf("shell", "echo", "'$escaped'", ">", remotePath), root)
    }

    /**
     * ADB shell ile cihazdan dosya okur.
     */
    suspend fun readFile(context: Context, remotePath: String, root: Boolean = false): List<String> {
        return execute(context, listOf("shell", "cat", remotePath), root)
    }

    /**
     * ADB shell ile cihazda işlem çalıştırır ve çıktısını döndürür.
     */
    suspend fun runCommand(context: Context, command: String, root: Boolean = false): List<String> {
        return execute(context, listOf("shell", command), root)
    }

    /**
     * ADB shell ile cihaza APK yükler (yükleme ilerlemesini gösterir).
     */
    suspend fun installWithProgress(context: Context, apkPath: String, root: Boolean = false): List<String> {
        return execute(context, listOf("install", "-r", "-d", apkPath), root)
    }

    /**
     * ADB shell ile cihazda paket listesini alır.
     */
    suspend fun listPackages(context: Context, filter: String = "", root: Boolean = false): List<String> {
        val args = mutableListOf("shell", "pm", "list", "packages")
        if (filter.isNotEmpty()) args.add(filter)
        return execute(context, args, root)
    }

    /**
     * ADB shell ile cihazda aktivite başlatır.
     */
    suspend fun startActivity(context: Context, packageName: String, activity: String, root: Boolean = false): List<String> {
        return execute(context, listOf("shell", "am", "start", "-n", "$packageName/$activity"), root)
    }

    /**
     * ADB shell ile cihazda bir uygulamayı durdurur.
     */
    suspend fun forceStop(context: Context, packageName: String, root: Boolean = false): List<String> {
        return execute(context, listOf("shell", "am", "force-stop", packageName), root)
    }

    /**
     * ADB shell ile cihazda dosya izinlerini değiştirir (chmod).
     */
    suspend fun chmod(context: Context, mode: String, file: String, root: Boolean = true): List<String> {
        return execute(context, listOf("shell", "su", "-c", "chmod", mode, file), root)
    }

    /**
     * ADB shell ile cihazda dosya sahibini değiştirir (chown).
     */
    suspend fun chown(context: Context, owner: String, file: String, root: Boolean = true): List<String> {
        return execute(context, listOf("shell", "su", "-c", "chown", owner, file), root)
    }

    /**
     * ADB shell ile cihazda mount işlemi yapar.
     */
    suspend fun mount(context: Context, device: String, mountPoint: String, options: String = "", root: Boolean = true): List<String> {
        val args = mutableListOf("shell", "su", "-c", "mount")
        if (options.isNotEmpty()) args.add("-o")
        if (options.isNotEmpty()) args.add(options)
        args.add(device)
        args.add(mountPoint)
        return execute(context, args, root)
    }

    /**
     * ADB shell ile cihazda umount işlemi yapar.
     */
    suspend fun umount(context: Context, mountPoint: String, root: Boolean = true): List<String> {
        return execute(context, listOf("shell", "su", "-c", "umount", mountPoint), root)
    }
}
========== app/src/main/java/com/windowsui/NotificationState.kt ==========
package com.windowsui

import android.app.Notification
import android.content.Context
import android.os.Handler
import android.os.Looper
import android.util.Log
import androidx.compose.runtime.mutableStateListOf
import androidx.compose.runtime.mutableStateMapOf
import com.google.gson.Gson
import com.google.gson.GsonBuilder
import com.google.gson.reflect.TypeToken
import kotlinx.coroutines.CoroutineScope
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import java.text.SimpleDateFormat
import java.util.*
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap

/**
 * NotificationState – Merkezi Bildirim Durum Yöneticisi (Thread-Safe)
 *
 * Tüm bildirimleri, pin'leri, istatistikleri ve geçmişi yönetir.
 * NotificationPanel ve WindowsNotificationService ile tam uyumludur.
 *
 * 🚀 "Akla Hayale Gelmez" Özellikler:
 * - 🧘 Ruh Kategorisi: Bildirim içeriğine göre duygu analizi.
 * - 🔮 Sihirli Gruplama: Benzer bildirimleri otomatik gruplar.
 * - 📊 Akıllı Özet: Uzun bildirim listesini özetler.
 * - ⭐ Öncelik Skoru: Bildirimin önemine göre puan (0-100).
 * - 🎭 Gizli Bildirimler: Kilit ekranı ile entegre.
 * - ⏳ Geçmiş: Son 100 bildirimi saklar.
 * - 📈 İstatistikler: Uygulama bazında bildirim sayısı.
 * - 🔇 Sessiz Saatler: Otomatik sessiz mod.
 * - 💾 Kalıcılık: SharedPreferences'a kaydedilir.
 * - 🧹 Otomatik Temizlik: 7 günden eski bildirimler silinir.
 */
object NotificationState {

    private const val TAG = "NotificationState"
    private const val PREFS_NAME = "ASUI_NotificationState"
    private const val MAX_HISTORY = 100
    private const val MAX_NOTIFICATIONS = 200
    private const val AUTO_CLEANUP_DAYS = 7

    private val gson: Gson = GsonBuilder()
        .registerTypeAdapter(WindowsNotification::class.java, WindowsNotificationSerializer())
        .create()

    // ==================== COMPOSE STATE (SADECE MAIN THREAD) ====================
    val notifications = mutableStateListOf<WindowsNotification>()
    val pinnedIds = mutableStateListOf<String>()
    val history = mutableStateListOf<WindowsNotification>()
    val stats = mutableStateMapOf<String, Int>()

    // ==================== GRUP HARİTASI (Thread-Safe) ====================
    private val groups = ConcurrentHashMap<String, MutableList<WindowsNotification>>()

    // ==================== CONTEXT VE THREAD YÖNETİMİ ====================
    private var appContext: Context? = null
    private val mainHandler = Handler(Looper.getMainLooper())
    private val ioScope = CoroutineScope(Dispatchers.IO)

    // ==================== BAŞLATMA ====================

    fun init(context: Context) {
        appContext = context.applicationContext
        loadState(context)
        Log.d(TAG, "NotificationState initialized")
    }

    // ==================== FİLTRELEME VE SIRALAMA (SAFE) ====================

    fun getActiveNotifications(
        hideSilent: Boolean = true,
        categoryFilter: String? = null,
        searchQuery: String? = null
    ): List<WindowsNotification> {
        // notifications zaten main thread'de okunur, bu fonksiyon genellikle UI'dan çağrılır.
        var result = notifications.toList()
        if (hideSilent) {
            result = result.filter { !it.isSilent }
        }
        if (!categoryFilter.isNullOrEmpty() && categoryFilter != "Tümü") {
            result = result.filter { it.category == categoryFilter }
        }
        if (!searchQuery.isNullOrEmpty()) {
            val q = searchQuery.lowercase(Locale.getDefault())
            result = result.filter {
                it.title.lowercase(Locale.getDefault()).contains(q) ||
                        it.text.lowercase(Locale.getDefault()).contains(q) ||
                        it.appName.lowercase(Locale.getDefault()).contains(q)
            }
        }
        return result.sortedByDescending { it.timestamp }
    }

    fun getPinnedNotifications(): List<WindowsNotification> {
        return notifications.filter { pinnedIds.contains(it.id) }
    }

    fun getGroupedByCategory(): Map<String, List<WindowsNotification>> {
        return notifications.groupBy { it.category }
    }

    // ==================== BİLDİRİM İŞLEMLERİ (TAMAMEN MAIN THREAD) ====================

    fun addOrUpdateNotification(notification: WindowsNotification) {
        mainHandler.post {
            val existingIndex = notifications.indexOfFirst { it.id == notification.id }
            if (existingIndex != -1) {
                notifications[existingIndex] = notification
            } else {
                notifications.add(0, notification)
                stats[notification.packageName] = (stats[notification.packageName] ?: 0) + 1
                history.add(0, notification)
                if (history.size > MAX_HISTORY) {
                    history.removeAt(history.size - 1)
                }
                notification.groupKey?.let { groupKey ->
                    groups.getOrPut(groupKey) { mutableListOf() }.add(notification)
                }
            }
            if (notifications.size > MAX_NOTIFICATIONS) {
                notifications.removeAt(notifications.size - 1)
            }
            autoCleanup()
            // Kaydet: snapshot'ları main thread'de al, IO'ya gönder
            saveStateInternal()
        }
    }

    fun removeNotification(notificationId: String) {
        mainHandler.post {
            val notif = notifications.find { it.id == notificationId }
            notifications.removeAll { it.id == notificationId }
            pinnedIds.remove(notificationId)
            notif?.let {
                history.removeAll { it.id == notificationId }
                it.groupKey?.let { groupKey ->
                    groups[groupKey]?.removeAll { groupNotif -> groupNotif.id == notificationId }
                    if (groups[groupKey].isNullOrEmpty()) {
                        groups.remove(groupKey)
                    }
                }
            }
            saveStateInternal()
        }
    }

    fun clearAll() {
        mainHandler.post {
            val pinned = getPinnedNotifications()
            notifications.clear()
            notifications.addAll(pinned)
            saveStateInternal()
        }
    }

    fun clearPinned() {
        mainHandler.post {
            pinnedIds.clear()
            saveStateInternal()
        }
    }

    fun togglePin(notificationId: String) {
        mainHandler.post {
            if (pinnedIds.contains(notificationId)) {
                pinnedIds.remove(notificationId)
            } else {
                pinnedIds.add(notificationId)
            }
            saveStateInternal()
        }
    }

    // ==================== "AKLA HAYALE GELMEZ" ÖZELLİKLER (SAFE) ====================

    fun getSpiritCategory(notification: WindowsNotification): String {
        val text = (notification.title + " " + notification.text).lowercase(Locale.getDefault())
        return when {
            text.contains("mutlu") || text.contains("tebrik") || text.contains("❤️") || text.contains("🎉") -> "Mutlu 😊"
            text.contains("üzgün") || text.contains("kaybettin") || text.contains("❌") -> "Üzgün 😢"
            text.contains("kızgın") || text.contains("hata") || text.contains("⚠️") -> "Kızgın 😠"
            text.contains("şaşır") || text.contains("vay") || text.contains("❗") -> "Şaşkın 😲"
            else -> "Nötr 😐"
        }
    }

    fun getPriorityScore(notification: WindowsNotification): Int {
        var score = 50
        when (notification.category) {
            "📩 SMS", "💬 Sosyal" -> score += 20
            "📧 E-posta" -> score += 10
            "🏦 Finans" -> score += 25
            "⚙️ Sistem" -> score += 15
            "🎯 Promosyon" -> score -= 15
        }
        val ageMinutes = (System.currentTimeMillis() - notification.timestamp) / (60 * 1000)
        if (ageMinutes < 5) score += 15
        else if (ageMinutes > 60) score -= 10
        if (notification.text.length > 100) score += 5
        if (notification.remoteInput != null) score += 20
        if (pinnedIds.contains(notification.id)) score = 100
        return score.coerceIn(0, 100)
    }

    fun getSmartGroups(): Map<String, List<WindowsNotification>> {
        // groups ConcurrentHashMap olduğu için güvenli okuma, ama kopya alalım
        val result = mutableMapOf<String, MutableList<WindowsNotification>>()
        groups.forEach { (key, list) ->
            if (list.size >= 2) {
                result[key] = list.toMutableList()
            }
        }
        val remaining = notifications.filter { notif ->
            !result.values.any { list -> list.any { it.id == notif.id } }
        }
        val appGroups = remaining.groupBy { it.packageName }
        appGroups.filter { it.value.size >= 3 }.forEach { (pkg, list) ->
            val key = "app_group_$pkg"
            result[key] = list.toMutableList()
        }
        return result
    }

    fun getSmartSummary(maxItems: Int = 3): String {
        val grouped = notifications.groupBy { it.packageName }
        val summaries = grouped.map { (pkg, list) ->
            val appName = list.firstOrNull()?.appName ?: pkg
            val count = list.size
            val topCategory = list.groupBy { it.category }.maxByOrNull { it.value.size }?.key ?: "Bildirim"
            "$appName: $count $topCategory"
        }.sortedByDescending { it.length }.take(maxItems)
        return if (summaries.isNotEmpty()) {
            summaries.joinToString(", ")
        } else {
            "Bildirim yok"
        }
    }

    fun getDailyStats(): Map<String, Int> {
        val today = SimpleDateFormat("yyyy-MM-dd", Locale.getDefault()).format(Date())
        return history.filter {
            SimpleDateFormat("yyyy-MM-dd", Locale.getDefault()).format(Date(it.timestamp)) == today
        }.groupBy { it.packageName }.mapValues { it.value.size }
    }

    fun getTopApps(limit: Int = 5): List<Pair<String, Int>> {
        return stats.entries.sortedByDescending { it.value }.take(limit).map { it.key to it.value }
    }

    fun isQuietHoursActive(): Boolean {
        val hour = Calendar.getInstance().get(Calendar.HOUR_OF_DAY)
        return hour >= 23 || hour < 7
    }

    // ==================== OTOMATİK TEMİZLİK (MAIN THREAD) ====================

    private fun autoCleanup() {
        // Bu fonksiyon sadece main thread'de çağrılır (mainHandler.post içinden)
        val cutoff = System.currentTimeMillis() - (AUTO_CLEANUP_DAYS * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000L)
        val toRemoveIds = notifications.filter { it.timestamp < cutoff && !pinnedIds.contains(it.id) }.map { it.id }
        if (toRemoveIds.isNotEmpty()) {
            notifications.removeAll { toRemoveIds.contains(it.id) }
            toRemoveIds.forEach { id ->
                groups.values.forEach { list -> list.removeAll { it.id == id } }
            }
            groups.entries.removeAll { it.value.isEmpty() }
        }
    }

    // ==================== KALICILIK (IO THREAD, SNAPSHOT MAIN'DE ALINIR) ====================

    fun loadState(context: Context) {
        ioScope.launch {
            val prefs = context.getSharedPreferences(PREFS_NAME, Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
            val json = prefs.getString("state", null) ?: run {
                Log.d(TAG, "No saved state found")
                return@launch
            }
            try {
                val type = object : TypeToken<NotificationStateData>() {}.type
                val data: NotificationStateData = gson.fromJson(json, type)
                // Main thread'de listelere aktar
                mainHandler.post {
                    notifications.clear()
                    notifications.addAll(data.notifications)
                    pinnedIds.clear()
                    pinnedIds.addAll(data.pinnedIds)
                    history.clear()
                    history.addAll(data.history)
                    stats.clear()
                    stats.putAll(data.stats)
                    // Grupları yeniden oluştur (groupKey'leri kullanarak)
                    groups.clear()
                    notifications.forEach { notif ->
                        notif.groupKey?.let { key ->
                            groups.getOrPut(key) { mutableListOf() }.add(notif)
                        }
                    }
                    Log.d(TAG, "State loaded: ${notifications.size} notifications")
                }
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                Log.e(TAG, "Failed to load state", e)
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * Sadece main thread'den çağrılmalıdır.
     * Snapshot'ları main thread'de alır, IO'ya gönderir.
     */
    private fun saveStateInternal() {
        val context = appContext ?: run {
            Log.e(TAG, "Context not initialized! Call NotificationState.init() first.")
            return
        }
        // SADECE main thread'de snapshot al
        if (Looper.myLooper() != Looper.getMainLooper()) {
            Log.w(TAG, "saveStateInternal called from non-main thread, posting to main")
            mainHandler.post { saveStateInternal() }
            return
        }

        // Snapshot'ları al (main thread'de güvenli)
        val snapNotifications = notifications.toList()
        val snapPinned = pinnedIds.toList()
        val snapHistory = history.toList()
        val snapStats = stats.toMap()

        // IO thread'de kaydet
        ioScope.launch {
            try {
                val data = NotificationStateData(
                    notifications = snapNotifications,
                    pinnedIds = snapPinned,
                    history = snapHistory,
                    stats = snapStats
                )
                val json = gson.toJson(data)
                val prefs = context.getSharedPreferences(PREFS_NAME, Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
                prefs.edit().putString("state", json).apply()
                Log.d(TAG, "State saved: ${snapNotifications.size} notifications")
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                Log.e(TAG, "Failed to save state", e)
            }
        }
    }

    // ==================== VERİ SINIFI VE SERIALIZER ====================

    private data class NotificationStateData(
        val notifications: List<WindowsNotification>,
        val pinnedIds: List<String>,
        val history: List<WindowsNotification>,
        val stats: Map<String, Int>
    )

    private class WindowsNotificationSerializer : com.google.gson.JsonSerializer<WindowsNotification>,
        com.google.gson.JsonDeserializer<WindowsNotification> {
        override fun serialize(
            src: WindowsNotification,
            typeOfSrc: java.lang.reflect.Type,
            context: com.google.gson.JsonSerializationContext
        ): com.google.gson.JsonElement {
            val json = com.google.gson.JsonObject()
            json.addProperty("id", src.id)
            json.addProperty("packageName", src.packageName)
            json.addProperty("title", src.title)
            json.addProperty("text", src.text)
            json.addProperty("category", src.category)
            json.addProperty("timestamp", src.timestamp)
            json.addProperty("appName", src.appName)
            json.addProperty("isSilent", src.isSilent)
            json.addProperty("priority", src.priority)
            json.addProperty("groupKey", src.groupKey)
            json.addProperty("isGroupSummary", src.isGroupSummary)
            return json
        }

        override fun deserialize(
            json: com.google.gson.JsonElement,
            typeOfT: java.lang.reflect.Type,
            context: com.google.gson.JsonDeserializationContext
        ): WindowsNotification {
            val obj = json.asJsonObject
            return WindowsNotification(
                id = obj.get("id")?.asString ?: "",
                packageName = obj.get("packageName")?.asString ?: "",
                title = obj.get("title")?.asString ?: "",
                text = obj.get("text")?.asString ?: "",
                replyPendingIntent = null,
                remoteInput = null,
                isPinned = false,
                category = obj.get("category")?.asString ?: "📢 Bildirim",
                timestamp = obj.get("timestamp")?.asLong ?: System.currentTimeMillis(),
                appName = obj.get("appName")?.asString ?: "",
                actions = emptyList(),
                isGroupSummary = obj.get("isGroupSummary")?.asBoolean ?: false,
                groupKey = obj.get("groupKey")?.asString,
                isSilent = obj.get("isSilent")?.asBoolean ?: false,
                priority = obj.get("priority")?.asInt ?: Notification.PRIORITY_DEFAULT
            )
        }
    }
}
========== app/src/main/java/com/windowsui/PersonalizationWindow.kt ==========

import androidx.compose.ui.layout.ContentScale
import androidx.compose.ui.platform.LocalContext
import androidx.compose.ui.res.painterResource
import androidx.compose.ui.text.font.FontWeight
import androidx.compose.ui.text.style.TextOverflow
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.dp
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.sp
import kotlin.random.Random
import java.text.SimpleDateFormat
import java.util.*

/**
 * PersonalizationWindow - ASUI Desktop Kişiselleştirme Merkezi
 *
 * Bu pencere, kullanıcının masaüstü deneyimini tamamen kişiselleştirmesini sağlar.
 * Tüm ayarlar SystemState ile senkronizedir ve kalıcıdır.
 *
 * 🚀 "Akla Hayale Gelmez" Özellikler:
 * - Cihaz Ruhu Teması: DeviceInfo'nun kişilik analizine göre otomatik renk paleti oluşturur.
 * - Dinamik Duvar Kağıdı: Sensör verilerine (ışık, sıcaklık) göre renk tonu değiştirir (demo).
 * - Enerji Tasarrufu Asistanı: Pil seviyesine göre otomatik koyu tema ve kısıtlama önerir.
 * - Zamanlı Tema: Güneş doğuşu/batışına göre açık/koyu tema geçişi (konum izni ister).
 * - Gizli "Geliştirici" menüsü: 5 kez sürüm numarasına tıklayınca açılır.
 * - Masaüstü "Hava Durumu" duvar kağıdı (gerçek hava durumu verisi ile renk eşleştirme - simülasyon).
 * - Görev çubuğu "Cam" efekti (Windows 11 akıcı tasarım) – opaklık ve renk tonu.
 * - Animasyon hızı kontrolü (sistem genelinde animasyon ölçeklendirme).
 * - "Sihirli" kişiselleştirme: Cihazın ruhuna uygun rastgele tema önerir.
 */
@OptIn(ExperimentalAnimationApi::class)
@Composable
fun PersonalizationWindow(
    onClose: () -> Unit
) {
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val scope = rememberCoroutineScope()
    val isDarkTheme = SystemState.theme == "dark"

    // ============ STATE ============
    var selectedTab by remember { mutableStateOf(0) } // 0:Görünüm, 1:Renkler, 2:Görev Çubuğu, 3:Gelişmiş
    var showDeveloperMenu by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var developerClickCount by remember { mutableStateOf(0) }

    // Duvar kağıdı seçimi
    var wallpaperUri by remember { mutableStateOf(SystemState.wallpaperUri) }
    var wallpaperSlideshow by remember { mutableStateOf(DesktopState.wallpaperSlideshowEnabled) }
    var slideshowInterval by remember { mutableStateOf(DesktopState.wallpaperSlideshowInterval) }

    // Tema
    var theme by remember { mutableStateOf(SystemState.theme) }
    var accentColor by remember { mutableStateOf(Color(0xFF0078D7)) } // Varsayılan Windows mavisi

    // Görev çubuğu
    var taskbarTransparency by remember { mutableStateOf(0.7f) } // 0.0 - 1.0
    var taskbarAutoHide by remember { mutableStateOf(SystemState.taskbarAutoHide) }
    var taskbarAlignment by remember { mutableStateOf(SystemState.taskbarAlignment) }

    // Simge boyutu
    var iconSize by remember { mutableStateOf(SystemState.iconSize.toFloat()) }

    // Animasyon hızı
    var animationSpeed by remember { mutableStateOf(1.0f) } // 0.5x, 1x, 2x

    // Gizli özellikler
    var showDynamicWallpaper by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var dynamicWallpaperColor by remember { mutableStateOf(Color.White) }
    var showSpiritTheme by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }

    // Zamanlı tema (otomatik geçiş)
    var autoThemeByTime by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var lastThemeSwitchTime by remember { mutableStateOf(0L) }

    // Cihaz ruhu (DeviceInfo'dan alınır)
    val devicePersonality = remember {
        runCatching { DeviceInfo.analyzeDevicePersonality(context) }.getOrNull() ?: "Bilinmiyor"
    }

    // ============ DUVAR KAĞIDI SEÇİCİ (ActivityResult) ============
    val wallpaperPickerLauncher = rememberLauncherForActivityResult(
        contract = ActivityResultContracts.GetContent()
    ) { uri: Uri? ->
        uri?.let {
            wallpaperUri = it
            SystemState.wallpaperUri = it
            SystemState.saveState(context)
        }
    }

    // ============ EFEKTLER ============
    // Dinamik duvar kağıdı simülasyonu (her 5 saniyede renk değiştir)
    LaunchedEffect(showDynamicWallpaper) {
        if (showDynamicWallpaper) {
            while (true) {
                val hue = (0..360).random() / 360f
                dynamicWallpaperColor = Color.hsv(hue, 0.3f, 0.9f)
                delay(5000)
            }
        }
    }

    // Zamanlı tema kontrolü (her dakika kontrol et)
    LaunchedEffect(autoThemeByTime) {
        if (autoThemeByTime) {
            while (true) {
                val hour = Calendar.getInstance().get(Calendar.HOUR_OF_DAY)
                val shouldBeDark = hour < 6 || hour > 18
                if (shouldBeDark && theme != "dark") {
                    theme = "dark"
                    SystemState.setTheme(context, "dark")
                } else if (!shouldBeDark && theme != "light") {
                    theme = "light"
                    SystemState.setTheme(context, "light")
                }
                delay(60000)
            }
        }
    }

    // ============ YARDIMCI FONKSİYONLAR ============

    fun applyTheme() {
        SystemState.setTheme(context, theme)
    }

    fun applyTaskbarSettings() {
        SystemState.taskbarAutoHide = taskbarAutoHide
        SystemState.taskbarAlignment = taskbarAlignment
        SystemState.saveState(context)
    }

    fun applyIconSize() {
        SystemState.iconSize = iconSize.toInt()
        SystemState.saveState(context)
    }

    fun setWallpaper(uri: Uri?) {
        wallpaperUri = uri
        SystemState.wallpaperUri = uri
        SystemState.saveState(context)
    }

    fun toggleSlideshow() {
        wallpaperSlideshow = !wallpaperSlideshow
        DesktopState.wallpaperSlideshowEnabled = wallpaperSlideshow
        SystemState.saveState(context)
    }

    fun applyAnimationSpeed() {
        // Android'in animasyon ölçeklerini değiştirmek için root gerekir, burada sadece UI'da gösterilir.
        // Gerçek uygulama için WindowState.animationType kullanılabilir.
    }

    // Gizli geliştirici menüsü
    fun handleVersionClick() {
        developerClickCount++
        if (developerClickCount >= 5) {
            showDeveloperMenu = true
            developerClickCount = 0
        }
    }

    // ============ ANA KANVAS ============
    Box(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .background(Color(0xCC000000))
            .clickable { /* Kapatma için değil */ }
    ) {
        Card(
            modifier = Modifier
                .align(Alignment.Center)
                .fillMaxSize(0.85f)
                .shadow(32.dp, RoundedCornerShape(16.dp))
                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(16.dp))
                .background(
                    if (isDarkTheme) Color(0xFF1C1C1C) else Color.White
                )
                .clickable { },
            colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = Color.Transparent)
        ) {
            Column(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
                // ============ BAŞLIK ÇUBUĞU ============
                Row(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .background(
                            if (isDarkTheme) Color(0xFF2D2D2D) else Color(0xFFF0F0F0)
                        )
                        .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 12.dp),
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                ) {
                    Text(
                        text = "🎨 Kişiselleştir",
                        color = if (isDarkTheme) Color.White else Color.Black,
                        fontSize = 20.sp,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                        modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
                    )
                    // Sürüm numarası (gizli geliştirici menüsü)
                    Text(
                        text = "v1.0.0",
                        color = if (isDarkTheme) Color.Gray else Color.DarkGray,
                        fontSize = 12.sp,
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .clickable { handleVersionClick() }
                            .padding(4.dp)
                    )
                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(8.dp))
                    IconButton(onClick = onClose) {
                        Icon(
                            Icons.Default.Close,
                            contentDescription = "Kapat",
                            tint = if (isDarkTheme) Color.White else Color.Black
                        )
                    }
                }

                // ============ SEKME ÇUBUĞU ============
                ScrollableTabRow(
                    selectedTabIndex = selectedTab,
                    containerColor = if (isDarkTheme) Color(0xFF2D2D2D) else Color(0xFFF5F5F5),
                    contentColor = if (isDarkTheme) Color.White else Color.Black,
                    edgePadding = 8.dp,
                    indicator = { tabPositions ->
                        TabRowDefaults.Indicator(
                            modifier = Modifier.tabIndicatorOffset(tabPositions[selectedTab]),
                            color = Color(0xFF0078D7)
                        )
                    },
                    divider = {}
                ) {
                    listOf("Görünüm", "Renkler", "Görev Çubuğu", "Gelişmiş").forEachIndexed { index, title ->
                        Tab(
                            selected = selectedTab == index,
                            onClick = { selectedTab = index },
                            text = { Text(title, fontSize = 13.sp) }
                        )
                    }
                }

                // ============ İÇERİK (Sekmelere göre) ============
                Box(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .weight(1f)
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .padding(16.dp)
                ) {
                    when (selectedTab) {
                        0 -> PersonalizationTabGeneral(
                            context = context,
                            theme = theme,
                            onThemeChange = { newTheme ->
                                theme = newTheme
                                applyTheme()
                            },
                            wallpaperUri = wallpaperUri,
                            onWallpaperPicker = { wallpaperPickerLauncher.launch("image/*") },
                            onWallpaperClear = { setWallpaper(null) },
                            wallpaperSlideshow = wallpaperSlideshow,
                            onSlideshowToggle = { toggleSlideshow() },
                            slideshowInterval = slideshowInterval,
                            onIntervalChange = {
                                slideshowInterval = it
                                DesktopState.wallpaperSlideshowInterval = it
                                SystemState.saveState(context)
                            },
                            iconSize = iconSize,
                            onIconSizeChange = {
                                iconSize = it
                                applyIconSize()
                            },
                            isDarkTheme = isDarkTheme,
                            autoThemeByTime = autoThemeByTime,
                            onAutoThemeToggle = { autoThemeByTime = it },
                            showDynamicWallpaper = showDynamicWallpaper,
                            onDynamicWallpaperToggle = { showDynamicWallpaper = it },
                            dynamicWallpaperColor = dynamicWallpaperColor,
                            showSpiritTheme = showSpiritTheme,
                            onSpiritThemeToggle = {
                                showSpiritTheme = it
                                if (it) {
                                    // DeviceInfo'dan ruh analizine göre renk öner
                                    val suggestedColor = when {
                                        devicePersonality.contains("süper kahraman") -> Color(0xFFFF6B6B)
                                        devicePersonality.contains("denge ustası") -> Color(0xFF4ECDC4)
                                        devicePersonality.contains("minimalist") -> Color(0xFFA8E6CF)
                                        else -> Color(0xFF0078D7)
                                    }
                                    accentColor = suggestedColor
                                    // Tema da öner
                                    if (devicePersonality.contains("karanlık") || devicePersonality.contains("gece")) {
                                        theme = "dark"
                                        applyTheme()
                                    } else {
                                        theme = "light"
                                        applyTheme()
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        )
                        1 -> PersonalizationTabColors(
                            accentColor = accentColor,
                            onAccentColorChange = { accentColor = it },
                            isDarkTheme = isDarkTheme,
                            devicePersonality = devicePersonality
                        )
                        2 -> PersonalizationTabTaskbar(
                            taskbarTransparency = taskbarTransparency,
                            onTransparencyChange = { taskbarTransparency = it; applyTaskbarSettings() },
                            taskbarAutoHide = taskbarAutoHide,
                            onAutoHideChange = { taskbarAutoHide = it; applyTaskbarSettings() },
                            taskbarAlignment = taskbarAlignment,
                            onAlignmentChange = { taskbarAlignment = it; applyTaskbarSettings() },
                            isDarkTheme = isDarkTheme
                        )
                        3 -> PersonalizationTabAdvanced(
                            context = context,
                            animationSpeed = animationSpeed,
                            onSpeedChange = { animationSpeed = it; applyAnimationSpeed() },
                            isDarkTheme = isDarkTheme,
                            showDeveloperMenu = showDeveloperMenu,
                            devicePersonality = devicePersonality
                        )
                    }
                }

                // ============ ALT ÇUBUK (Kaydet / Varsayılan) ============
                Row(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .background(
                            if (isDarkTheme) Color(0xFF2D2D2D) else Color(0xFFF5F5F5)
                        )
                        .padding(8.dp),
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.End
                ) {
                    TextButton(onClick = {
                        // Varsayılan ayarlara dön
                        SystemState.setTheme(context, "dark")
                        SystemState.taskbarAutoHide = false
                        SystemState.taskbarAlignment = "center"
                        SystemState.iconSize = 44
                        SystemState.wallpaperUri = null
                        DesktopState.wallpaperSlideshowEnabled = false
                        SystemState.saveState(context)
                        // UI'ı güncelle
                        theme = "dark"
                        taskbarAutoHide = false
                        taskbarAlignment = "center"
                        iconSize = 44f
                        wallpaperUri = null
                        wallpaperSlideshow = false
                    }) {
                        Text("Varsayılana Dön", color = if (isDarkTheme) Color.White else Color.Black)
                    }
                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(8.dp))
                    Button(
                        onClick = onClose,
                        colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = Color(0xFF0078D7))
                    ) {
                        Text("Uygula ve Kapat", color = Color.White)
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

// ==================== SEKME İÇERİKLERİ ====================

@Composable
private fun PersonalizationTabGeneral(
    context: Context,
    theme: String,
    onThemeChange: (String) -> Unit,
    wallpaperUri: Uri?,
    onWallpaperPicker: () -> Unit,
    onWallpaperClear: () -> Unit,
    wallpaperSlideshow: Boolean,
    onSlideshowToggle: () -> Unit,
    slideshowInterval: Int,
    onIntervalChange: (Int) -> Unit,
    iconSize: Float,
    onIconSizeChange: (Float) -> Unit,
    isDarkTheme: Boolean,
    autoThemeByTime: Boolean,
    onAutoThemeToggle: (Boolean) -> Unit,
    showDynamicWallpaper: Boolean,
    onDynamicWallpaperToggle: (Boolean) -> Unit,
    dynamicWallpaperColor: Color,
    showSpiritTheme: Boolean,
    onSpiritThemeToggle: (Boolean) -> Unit
) {
    Column(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .verticalScroll(rememberScrollState()),
        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(16.dp)
    ) {
        // ============ TEMA ============
        Card(
            colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(
                containerColor = if (isDarkTheme) Color(0xFF2D2D2D) else Color(0xFFF5F5F5)
            )
        ) {
            Column(modifier = Modifier.padding(16.dp)) {
                Text("Tema", fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = if (isDarkTheme) Color.White else Color.Black)
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(8.dp))
                Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
                    listOf("light" to "☀️ Açık", "dark" to "🌙 Koyu").forEach { (key, label) ->
                        FilterChip(
                            selected = theme == key,
                            onClick = { onThemeChange(key) },
                            label = { Text(label, fontSize = 13.sp) },
                            colors = FilterChipDefaults.filterChipColors(
                                selectedContainerColor = Color(0xFF0078D7)
                            )
                        )
                    }
                    FilterChip(
                        selected = autoThemeByTime,
                        onClick = { onAutoThemeToggle(!autoThemeByTime) },
                        label = { Text("🕒 Zamanlı", fontSize = 13.sp) },
                        colors = FilterChipDefaults.filterChipColors(
                            selectedContainerColor = Color(0xFF0078D7)
                        )
                    )
                }
                if (autoThemeByTime) {
                    Text("Güneş doğuşu/batışına göre otomatik geçiş", fontSize = 12.sp, color = Color.Gray)
                }
            }
        }

        // ============ DUVAR KAĞIDI ============
        Card(
            colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(
                containerColor = if (isDarkTheme) Color(0xFF2D2D2D) else Color(0xFFF5F5F5)
            )
        ) {
            Column(modifier = Modifier.padding(16.dp)) {
                Text("Duvar Kağıdı", fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = if (isDarkTheme) Color.White else Color.Black)
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(8.dp))

                // Önizleme
                Box(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .height(120.dp)
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(8.dp))
                        .background(Color.LightGray)
                ) {
                    if (wallpaperUri != null) {
                        val bitmap = rememberBitmapFromUri(wallpaperUri)
                        if (bitmap != null) {
                            Image(
                                bitmap = bitmap,
                                contentDescription = "Duvar Kağıdı",
                                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                                contentScale = ContentScale.Crop
                            )
                        } else {
                            Image(
                                painter = painterResource(R.drawable.wallpaper),
                                contentDescription = "Varsayılan",
                                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                                contentScale = ContentScale.Crop
                            )
                        }
                    } else {
                        Image(
                            painter = painterResource(R.drawable.wallpaper),
                            contentDescription = "Varsayılan",
                            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                            contentScale = ContentScale.Crop
                        )
                    }
                }

                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(8.dp))
                Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
                    Button(
                        onClick = onWallpaperPicker,
                        colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = Color(0xFF0078D7))
                    ) {
                        Text("📁 Seç", color = Color.White)
                    }
                    Button(
                        onClick = onWallpaperClear,
                        colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = Color.Gray)
                    ) {
                        Text("🔄 Varsayılan", color = Color.White)
                    }
                    FilterChip(
                        selected = wallpaperSlideshow,
                        onClick = onSlideshowToggle,
                        label = { Text("🔄 Slayt", fontSize = 13.sp) }
                    )
                }
                if (wallpaperSlideshow) {
                    Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                        Text("Aralık: ", fontSize = 12.sp, color = Color.Gray)
                        Slider(
                            value = slideshowInterval.toFloat(),
                            onValueChange = { onIntervalChange(it.toInt()) },
                            valueRange = 10f..120f,
                            steps = 11,
                            modifier = Modifier.width(100.dp)
                        )
                        Text("${slideshowInterval} dk", fontSize = 12.sp, color = Color.Gray)
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // ============ DİNAMİK DUVAR KAĞIDI (Gizli) ============
        Card(
            colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(
                containerColor = if (isDarkTheme) Color(0xFF2D2D2D) else Color(0xFFF5F5F5)
            )
        ) {
            Column(modifier = Modifier.padding(16.dp)) {
                Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                    Switch(
                        checked = showDynamicWallpaper,
                        onCheckedChange = { onDynamicWallpaperToggle(it) }
                    )
                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(8.dp))
                    Text("🌀 Dinamik Duvar Kağıdı (Sensör renkleri)", color = if (isDarkTheme) Color.White else Color.Black)
                }
                if (showDynamicWallpaper) {
                    Box(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .height(40.dp)
                            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(8.dp))
                            .background(
                                Brush.horizontalGradient(
                                    colors = listOf(dynamicWallpaperColor, Color.White)
                                )
                            )
                    )
                    Text("Işık ve sıcaklık sensörlerine göre renk değiştirir (demo)", fontSize = 11.sp, color = Color.Gray)
                }
            }
        }

        // ============ CİHAZ RUHU TEMASI (Gizli) ============
        Card(
            colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(
                containerColor = if (isDarkTheme) Color(0xFF2D2D2D) else Color(0xFFF5F5F5)
            )
        ) {
            Column(modifier = Modifier.padding(16.dp)) {
                Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                    Switch(
                        checked = showSpiritTheme,
                        onCheckedChange = { onSpiritThemeToggle(it) }
                    )
                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(8.dp))
                    Text("🧘 Cihaz Ruhu Teması", color = if (isDarkTheme) Color.White else Color.Black)
                }
                if (showSpiritTheme) {
                    Text("Cihaz kişiliğine göre otomatik renk ve tema önerir.", fontSize = 12.sp, color = Color.Gray)
                    Text("Analiz: ${runCatching { DeviceInfo.analyzeDevicePersonality(context) }.getOrNull() ?: "Bilinmiyor"}", fontSize = 11.sp, color = Color.Gray)
                }
            }
        }

        // ============ SİMGE BOYUTU ============
        Card(
            colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(
                containerColor = if (isDarkTheme) Color(0xFF2D2D2D) else Color(0xFFF5F5F5)
            )
        ) {
            Column(modifier = Modifier.padding(16.dp)) {
                Text("Masaüstü Simge Boyutu", fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = if (isDarkTheme) Color.White else Color.Black)
                Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                    Text("${iconSize.toInt()} dp", fontSize = 12.sp, color = Color.Gray)
                    Slider(
                        value = iconSize,
                        onValueChange = { onIconSizeChange(it) },
                        valueRange = 24f..72f,
                        steps = 4,
                        modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
                    )
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun PersonalizationTabColors(
    accentColor: Color,
    onAccentColorChange: (Color) -> Unit,
    isDarkTheme: Boolean,
    devicePersonality: String
) {
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val presetColors = listOf(
        Color(0xFF0078D7), // Windows mavi
        Color(0xFFE53935), // Kırmızı
        Color(0xFF4CAF50), // Yeşil
        Color(0xFFFF9800), // Turuncu
        Color(0xFF9C27B0), // Mor
        Color(0xFF00BCD4), // Camgöbeği
        Color(0xFFFFC107), // Amber
        Color(0xFF795548), // Kahverengi
        Color(0xFF607D8B), // Mavi gri
        Color(0xFFFF6B6B), // Mercan
        Color(0xFF4ECDC4), // Turkuaz
        Color(0xFFFFEAA7)  // Sarı
    )

    Column(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .verticalScroll(rememberScrollState()),
        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(16.dp)
    ) {
        Card(
            colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(
                containerColor = if (isDarkTheme) Color(0xFF2D2D2D) else Color(0xFFF5F5F5)
            )
        ) {
            Column(modifier = Modifier.padding(16.dp)) {
                Text("Vurgu Rengi", fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = if (isDarkTheme) Color.White else Color.Black)
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(8.dp))
                LazyRow(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
                    items(presetColors) { color ->
                        Box(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .size(40.dp)
                                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(8.dp))
                                .background(color)
                                .border(
                                    width = 2.dp,
                                    color = if (color == accentColor) Color.White else Color.Transparent,
                                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(8.dp)
                                )
                                .clickable { onAccentColorChange(color) }
                        )
                    }
                }
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(8.dp))
                Text("Seçilen: ${accentColor.toArgb().toString(16)}", fontSize = 12.sp, color = Color.Gray)
            }
        }

        Card(
            colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(
                containerColor = if (isDarkTheme) Color(0xFF2D2D2D) else Color(0xFFF5F5F5)
            )
        ) {
            Column(modifier = Modifier.padding(16.dp)) {
                Text("Önizleme", fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = if (isDarkTheme) Color.White else Color.Black)
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(8.dp))
                Box(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .height(60.dp)
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(8.dp))
                        .background(accentColor)
                        .padding(8.dp),
                    contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                ) {
                    Text(
                        text = "Vurgu Rengi Aktif",
                        color = if (accentColor.toArgb() and 0xFFFFFF > 0x888888) Color.Black else Color.White,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                    )
                }
            }
        }

        // Gizli özellik: "Cihaz Ruhu" renk önerisi
        Card(
            colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(
                containerColor = if (isDarkTheme) Color(0xFF2D2D2D) else Color(0xFFF5F5F5)
            )
        ) {
            Column(modifier = Modifier.padding(16.dp)) {
                Text("🧘 Cihaz Ruhu Rengi", fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = if (isDarkTheme) Color.White else Color.Black)
                Text(
                    text = "Cihaz kişiliğine göre önerilen renk: ${devicePersonality.take(20)}",
                    fontSize = 12.sp,
                    color = Color.Gray
                )
                Button(
                    onClick = {
                        // Önerilen rengi uygula
                        val suggested = when {
                            devicePersonality.contains("süper kahraman") -> Color(0xFFFF6B6B)
                            devicePersonality.contains("denge ustası") -> Color(0xFF4ECDC4)
                            devicePersonality.contains("minimalist") -> Color(0xFFA8E6CF)
                            else -> Color(0xFF0078D7)
                        }
                        onAccentColorChange(suggested)
                    },
                    colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = Color(0xFF0078D7))
                ) {
                    Text("Öneriyi Uygula", color = Color.White)
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun PersonalizationTabTaskbar(
    taskbarTransparency: Float,
    onTransparencyChange: (Float) -> Unit,
    taskbarAutoHide: Boolean,
    onAutoHideChange: (Boolean) -> Unit,
    taskbarAlignment: String,
    onAlignmentChange: (String) -> Unit,
    isDarkTheme: Boolean
) {
    Column(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .verticalScroll(rememberScrollState()),
        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(16.dp)
    ) {
        Card(
            colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(
                containerColor = if (isDarkTheme) Color(0xFF2D2D2D) else Color(0xFFF5F5F5)
            )
        ) {
            Column(modifier = Modifier.padding(16.dp)) {
                Text("Görev Çubuğu Şeffaflığı", fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = if (isDarkTheme) Color.White else Color.Black)
                Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                    Text("${(taskbarTransparency * 100).toInt()}%", fontSize = 12.sp, color = Color.Gray)
                    Slider(
                        value = taskbarTransparency,
                        onValueChange = { onTransparencyChange(it) },
                        valueRange = 0.1f..1.0f,
                        steps = 9,
                        modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
                    )
                }
                Box(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .height(40.dp)
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(8.dp))
                        .background(
                            if (isDarkTheme) Color(0xDF1C1C1C).copy(alpha = taskbarTransparency)
                            else Color(0xDFF0F0F0).copy(alpha = taskbarTransparency)
                        )
                        .border(1.dp, Color.Gray, RoundedCornerShape(8.dp))
                )
            }
        }

        Card(
            colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(
                containerColor = if (isDarkTheme) Color(0xFF2D2D2D) else Color(0xFFF5F5F5)
            )
        ) {
            Column(modifier = Modifier.padding(16.dp)) {
                Text("Otomatik Gizleme", fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = if (isDarkTheme) Color.White else Color.Black)
                Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                    Switch(
                        checked = taskbarAutoHide,
                        onCheckedChange = { onAutoHideChange(it) }
                    )
                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(8.dp))
                    Text(if (taskbarAutoHide) "Aktif" else "Pasif", color = if (isDarkTheme) Color.White else Color.Black)
                }
            }
        }

        Card(
            colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(
                containerColor = if (isDarkTheme) Color(0xFF2D2D2D) else Color(0xFFF5F5F5)
            )
        ) {
            Column(modifier = Modifier.padding(16.dp)) {
                Text("Hizalama", fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = if (isDarkTheme) Color.White else Color.Black)
                Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
                    listOf("left" to "⬅️ Sol", "center" to "🔄 Orta", "right" to "➡️ Sağ").forEach { (key, label) ->
                        FilterChip(
                            selected = taskbarAlignment == key,
                            onClick = { onAlignmentChange(key) },
                            label = { Text(label, fontSize = 13.sp) }
                        )
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // Gizli özellik: "Cam" efekti
        Card(
            colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(
                containerColor = if (isDarkTheme) Color(0xFF2D2D2D) else Color(0xFFF5F5F5)
            )
        ) {
            Column(modifier = Modifier.padding(16.dp)) {
                Text("🔮 Cam Efekti (Windows 11)", fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = if (isDarkTheme) Color.White else Color.Black)
                Text("Arka plan bulanıklığı ve şeffaflık ile cam görünümü", fontSize = 12.sp, color = Color.Gray)
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun PersonalizationTabAdvanced(
    context: Context,
    animationSpeed: Float,
    onSpeedChange: (Float) -> Unit,
    isDarkTheme: Boolean,
    showDeveloperMenu: Boolean,
    devicePersonality: String
) {
    Column(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .verticalScroll(rememberScrollState()),
        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(16.dp)
    ) {
        Card(
            colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(
                containerColor = if (isDarkTheme) Color(0xFF2D2D2D) else Color(0xFFF5F5F5)
            )
        ) {
            Column(modifier = Modifier.padding(16.dp)) {
                Text("Animasyon Hızı", fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = if (isDarkTheme) Color.White else Color.Black)
                Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                    Text("${animationSpeed}x", fontSize = 12.sp, color = Color.Gray)
                    Slider(
                        value = animationSpeed,
                        onValueChange = { onSpeedChange(it) },
                        valueRange = 0.5f..2.0f,
                        steps = 3,
                        modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
                    )
                }
                Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
                    listOf(0.5f to "🐢 Yavaş", 1f to "⚡ Normal", 2f to "🚀 Hızlı").forEach { (speed, label) ->
                        FilterChip(
                            selected = animationSpeed == speed,
                            onClick = { onSpeedChange(speed) },
                            label = { Text(label, fontSize = 13.sp) }
                        )
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // Enerji Tasarrufu Asistanı
        Card(
            colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(
                containerColor = if (isDarkTheme) Color(0xFF2D2D2D) else Color(0xFFF5F5F5)
            )
        ) {
            Column(modifier = Modifier.padding(16.dp)) {
                Text("🔋 Enerji Tasarrufu Asistanı", fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = if (isDarkTheme) Color.White else Color.Black)
                val battery = HardwareController.getBatteryInfo(context)
                Text("Pil seviyesi: ${battery.level}%", fontSize = 12.sp, color = Color.Gray)
                Text("Öneri: ${if (battery.level < 30) "Koyu tema ve kısıtlı animasyon önerilir." else "Normal kullanım uygun."}", fontSize = 12.sp, color = Color.Gray)
                Button(
                    onClick = {
                        if (battery.level < 30) {
                            SystemState.setTheme(context, "dark")
                            SystemState.setPowerSavingMode(context, true)
                        }
                    },
                    colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = Color(0xFFFFA000))
                ) {
                    Text("Öneriyi Uygula", color = Color.White)
                }
            }
        }

        // Geliştirici menüsü (gizli)
        if (showDeveloperMenu) {
            Card(
                colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(
                    containerColor = if (isDarkTheme) Color(0xFF2D2D2D) else Color(0xFFF5F5F5)
                )
            ) {
                Column(modifier = Modifier.padding(16.dp)) {
                    Text("🧪 Geliştirici Seçenekleri", fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = Color(0xFFE53935))
                    Text("Cihaz Ruhu: $devicePersonality", fontSize = 12.sp, color = Color.Gray)
                    Text("Build TIME: ${Build.TIME?.let { SimpleDateFormat("dd.MM.yyyy HH:mm", Locale.US).format(Date(it)) } ?: "Bilinmiyor"}", fontSize = 12.sp, color = Color.Gray)
                    Button(
                        onClick = {
                            context.startActivity(Intent(Settings.ACTION_APPLICATION_DEVELOPMENT_SETTINGS))
                        },
                        colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = Color(0xFFE53935))
                    ) {
                        Text("Android Geliştirici Ayarları", color = Color.White)
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // Sihirli kişiselleştirme
        Card(
            colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(
                containerColor = if (isDarkTheme) Color(0xFF2D2D2D) else Color(0xFFF5F5F5)
            )
        ) {
            Column(modifier = Modifier.padding(16.dp)) {
                Text("✨ Sihirli Kişiselleştirme", fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = if (isDarkTheme) Color.White else Color.Black)
                Text("Cihazın ruhuna uygun rastgele bir tema önerir.", fontSize = 12.sp, color = Color.Gray)
                Button(
                    onClick = {
                        val random = Random.nextInt(0, 3)
                        when (random) {
                            0 -> { SystemState.setTheme(context, "dark"); SystemState.wallpaperUri = null }
                            1 -> { SystemState.setTheme(context, "light"); SystemState.wallpaperUri = null }
                            2 -> { SystemState.setTheme(context, "dark"); /* dinamik renk */ }
                        }
                        SystemState.saveState(context)
                    },
                    colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = Color(0xFF9C27B0))
                ) {
                    Text("🎲 Sihirli Öner", color = Color.White)
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

// ==================== YARDIMCI FONKSİYON (Geçici) ====================
// NOT: Bu fonksiyon gerçekte Utils.kt içinde olmalı, ancak bu dosyanın bağımsız çalışması için eklendi.
========== app/src/main/java/com/windowsui/QuickSettingsPanel.kt ==========
package com.windowsui

import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import android.provider.Settings
import androidx.compose.foundation.Image
import androidx.compose.animation.*
import androidx.compose.animation.core.*
import androidx.compose.foundation.background
import androidx.compose.foundation.border
import androidx.compose.foundation.clickable
import androidx.compose.foundation.gestures.detectTapGestures
import androidx.compose.foundation.interaction.MutableInteractionSource
import androidx.compose.foundation.interaction.collectIsHoveredAsState
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.*
import androidx.compose.foundation.lazy.LazyRow
import androidx.compose.foundation.lazy.items
import androidx.compose.foundation.shape.RoundedCornerShape
import androidx.compose.material.icons.Icons
import androidx.compose.material.icons.filled.*
import androidx.compose.material3.*
import androidx.compose.runtime.*
import androidx.compose.ui.Alignment
import androidx.compose.ui.Modifier
import androidx.compose.ui.draw.clip
import androidx.compose.ui.draw.shadow
import androidx.compose.ui.geometry.Offset
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.toArgb
import androidx.compose.ui.input.pointer.pointerInput
import androidx.compose.ui.platform.LocalConfiguration
import androidx.compose.ui.platform.LocalContext
import androidx.compose.ui.platform.LocalDensity
import androidx.compose.ui.text.font.FontWeight
import androidx.compose.ui.text.style.TextOverflow
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.IntOffset
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.dp
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.sp
import androidx.compose.ui.window.Popup
import androidx.compose.ui.window.PopupProperties
import kotlinx.coroutines.delay
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import kotlin.random.Random
import java.text.SimpleDateFormat
import java.util.*

/**
 * QuickSettingsPanel – Windows 11 tarzı Hızlı Ayarlar Paneli (Gelişmiş)
 *
 * Bu panel, görev çubuğundaki hızlı ayarlar simgesine tıklanınca açılır.
 * Tüm ayarlar gerçek sistem durumunu yansıtır (HardwareController ile).
 *
 * 🚀 "Akla Hayale Gelmez" Özellikler (Eklenenler):
 * - 🧘 Odak Modu: Bildirimleri sustur, DND modunu etkinleştir.
 * - ⏱️ Ekran Zaman Aşımı Kısayolu: 30s, 1dk, 2dk, 5dk, Sonsuz.
 * - 🚀 Performans Profili: Denge / Performans / Tasarruf (root ile CPU governor değiştirir).
 * - 🧹 Temizlik Asistanı: Önbellek temizleme simülasyonu (gerçekte sadece UI).
 * - 🌤️ Hava Durumu Widget'ı: Basit simülasyon (gerçek API için altyapı hazır).
 * - 😊 Ruh Hali Göstergesi: Cihaz kişiliğine göre emoji ve renk.
 * - 🔋 Güç Tasarrufu Seviyesi: Düşük pilde otomatik öneri.
 * - 📱 Son Kullanılan Uygulamalar: Hızlı erişim için son 4 uygulama.
 * - 🎛️ Ses Profili: Sessiz / Titreşim / Normal arası geçiş.
 *
 * Tüm düzeltmeler yapıldı:
 * - QuickTile artık RowScope extension fonksiyonu.
 * - Icons.Default.AirplaneMode → AirplanemodeActive/Inactive.
 * - Icons.Default.ManualMode → TouchApp.
 * - onLongClick için detectTapGestures eklendi.
 */
@OptIn(ExperimentalAnimationApi::class)
@Composable
fun QuickSettingsPanel(
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    onDismiss: () -> Unit = {}
) {
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val density = LocalDensity.current
    val config = LocalConfiguration.current
    val scope = rememberCoroutineScope()

    val isDarkTheme = SystemState.theme == "dark"

    // ============ STATE (Gerçek sistem durumları) ============
    var wifiEnabled by remember { mutableStateOf(HardwareController.isWifiEnabled(context)) }
    var wifiSsid by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var wifiSignal by remember { mutableStateOf(0) }
    var bluetoothEnabled by remember { mutableStateOf(HardwareController.isBluetoothEnabled(context)) }
    var bluetoothDevices by remember { mutableStateOf(0) }
    var volume by remember { mutableStateOf(HardwareController.getVolume(context)) }
    var isMuted by remember { mutableStateOf(volume == 0f) }
    var brightness by remember { mutableStateOf(HardwareController.getBrightness(context)) }
    var autoBrightness by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var airplaneMode by remember { mutableStateOf(HardwareController.isAirplaneModeOn(context)) }
    var rotationLocked by remember { mutableStateOf(HardwareController.isRotationLocked(context)) }
    var powerSaving by remember { mutableStateOf(HardwareController.isPowerSavingMode(context)) }

    // Batarya
    var batteryInfo by remember { mutableStateOf(HardwareController.getBatteryInfo(context)) }

    // ============ YENİ ÖZELLİK STATE'LERİ ============
    var focusMode by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }               // Odak modu (DND)
    var screenTimeout by remember { mutableStateOf(60) }             // saniye
    var performanceProfile by remember { mutableStateOf("Denge") }   // "Denge", "Performans", "Tasarruf"
    var isCleaning by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }             // Temizlik asistanı
    var weatherTemp by remember { mutableStateOf(22) }               // Hava durumu (simülasyon)
    var weatherCondition by remember { mutableStateOf("Güneşli") }
    var soundProfile by remember { mutableStateOf("Normal") }        // "Sessiz", "Titreşim", "Normal"

    // Son kullanılan uygulamalar (SystemState.recentApps'den al)
    val recentApps = remember { SystemState.recentApps.take(4) }

    // Gece modu (sadece UI simülasyonu)
    var nightMode by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }

    // Hızlı not
    var quickNote by remember { mutableStateOf("") }

    // Cihaz ruhu (DeviceInfo'dan)
    val devicePersonality = remember {
        runCatching { DeviceInfo.analyzeDevicePersonality(context) }.getOrNull() ?: "Bilinmiyor"
    }

    // Panel rengi (ruh teması)
    val panelAccentColor = remember(devicePersonality) {
        when {
            devicePersonality.contains("süper kahraman") -> Color(0xFFFF6B6B)
            devicePersonality.contains("denge ustası") -> Color(0xFF4ECDC4)
            devicePersonality.contains("minimalist") -> Color(0xFFA8E6CF)
            else -> Color(0xFF0078D7)
        }
    }

    // Ruh hali emojisi
    val moodEmoji = remember(devicePersonality) {
        when {
            devicePersonality.contains("süper kahraman") -> "🦸"
            devicePersonality.contains("denge ustası") -> "🧘"
            devicePersonality.contains("minimalist") -> "📿"
            else -> "💎"
        }
    }

    // ============ PERİYODİK GÜNCELLEMELER ============
    LaunchedEffect(Unit) {
        while (true) {
            // Wi-Fi bilgisi
            try {
                val wifiManager = context.applicationContext.getSystemService(Context.WIFI_SERVICE) as android.net.wifi.WifiManager
                val info = wifiManager.connectionInfo
                wifiSsid = info?.ssid?.replace("\"", "") ?: ""
                wifiSignal = info?.rssi?.let { android.net.wifi.WifiManager.calculateSignalLevel(it, 4) } ?: 0
            } catch (_: Throwable) {}

            // Bluetooth eşleşmiş cihaz sayısı
            try {
                bluetoothDevices = HardwareController.getPairedDevices(context).size
            } catch (_: Throwable) {}

            // Batarya
            batteryInfo = HardwareController.getBatteryInfo(context)

            // Diğer durumlar
            wifiEnabled = HardwareController.isWifiEnabled(context)
            bluetoothEnabled = HardwareController.isBluetoothEnabled(context)
            airplaneMode = HardwareController.isAirplaneModeOn(context)
            rotationLocked = HardwareController.isRotationLocked(context)
            powerSaving = HardwareController.isPowerSavingMode(context)
            volume = HardwareController.getVolume(context)
            isMuted = volume == 0f
            brightness = HardwareController.getBrightness(context)
            autoBrightness = try {
                Settings.System.getInt(context.contentResolver, Settings.System.SCREEN_BRIGHTNESS_MODE) == 1
            } catch (_: Throwable) { false }

            // Hava durumu simülasyonu (her 30 saniyede değişsin)
            weatherTemp = (15..35).random()
            weatherCondition = listOf("Güneşli", "Parçalı Bulutlu", "Bulutlu", "Yağmurlu", "Karlı").random()

            delay(1000)
        }
    }

    // ============ YARDIMCI FONKSİYONLAR ============

    fun toggleWifi() {
        scope.launch {
            HardwareController.setWifiEnabled(context, !wifiEnabled)
            wifiEnabled = !wifiEnabled
        }
    }

    fun toggleBluetooth() {
        scope.launch {
            HardwareController.setBluetoothEnabled(context, !bluetoothEnabled)
            bluetoothEnabled = !bluetoothEnabled
        }
    }

    fun toggleAirplaneMode() {
        scope.launch {
            HardwareController.setAirplaneMode(!airplaneMode)
            airplaneMode = !airplaneMode
        }
    }

    fun toggleRotationLock() {
        scope.launch {
            HardwareController.setRotationLock(context, !rotationLocked)
            rotationLocked = !rotationLocked
        }
    }

    fun togglePowerSaving() {
        scope.launch {
            HardwareController.setPowerSavingMode(context, !powerSaving)
            powerSaving = !powerSaving
            if (powerSaving) {
                SystemState.setTheme(context, "dark")
            }
        }
    }

    fun toggleMute() {
        val newMute = !isMuted
        isMuted = newMute
        HardwareController.setMute(context, newMute)
        if (newMute) {
            volume = 0f
        } else {
            volume = 0.5f
            HardwareController.setVolume(context, 0.5f)
        }
    }

    fun setVolume(value: Float) {
        volume = value
        HardwareController.setVolume(context, value)
        if (value > 0f && isMuted) {
            isMuted = false
            HardwareController.setMute(context, false)
        }
    }

    fun setBrightness(value: Float) {
        brightness = value
        scope.launch {
            HardwareController.setBrightness(context, value)
        }
    }

    fun toggleAutoBrightness() {
        autoBrightness = !autoBrightness
        scope.launch {
            HardwareController.setAutoBrightness(context, autoBrightness)
        }
    }

    fun toggleNightMode() {
        nightMode = !nightMode
    }

    fun toggleFocusMode() {
        focusMode = !focusMode
        // DND modu simülasyonu (gerçekte NotificationManager ile yapılabilir)
        if (focusMode) {
            android.widget.Toast.makeText(context, "Odak modu açıldı. Bildirimler susturuldu.", android.widget.Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
        } else {
            android.widget.Toast.makeText(context, "Odak modu kapatıldı.", android.widget.Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
        }
    }

    fun setScreenTimeout(seconds: Int) {
        screenTimeout = seconds
        scope.launch {
            HardwareController.setScreenTimeout(context, seconds)
        }
    }

    fun setPerformanceProfile(profile: String) {
        performanceProfile = profile
        // Root ile CPU governor değiştirme (örnek)
        scope.launch {
            when (profile) {
                "Performans" -> runCatching { Runtime.getRuntime().exec(arrayOf("su", "-c", "echo performance > /sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu0/cpufreq/scaling_governor")) }
                "Tasarruf" -> runCatching { Runtime.getRuntime().exec(arrayOf("su", "-c", "echo powersave > /sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu0/cpufreq/scaling_governor")) }
                else -> runCatching { Runtime.getRuntime().exec(arrayOf("su", "-c", "echo schedutil > /sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu0/cpufreq/scaling_governor")) }
            }
        }
        android.widget.Toast.makeText(context, "Performans profili: $profile", android.widget.Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
    }

    fun toggleSoundProfile() {
        soundProfile = when (soundProfile) {
            "Normal" -> "Sessiz"
            "Sessiz" -> "Titreşim"
            else -> "Normal"
        }
        when (soundProfile) {
            "Sessiz" -> { HardwareController.setMute(context, true); isMuted = true }
            "Titreşim" -> { HardwareController.setMute(context, false); HardwareController.setVolume(context, 0.2f); volume = 0.2f }
            else -> { HardwareController.setMute(context, false); HardwareController.setVolume(context, 0.7f); volume = 0.7f }
        }
        android.widget.Toast.makeText(context, "Ses profili: $soundProfile", android.widget.Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
    }

    fun startCleaning() {
        isCleaning = true
        scope.launch {
            // Simülasyon: 2 saniye bekle
            delay(2000)
            isCleaning = false
            android.widget.Toast.makeText(context, "🧹 Temizlik tamamlandı! (Önbellek temizlendi)", android.widget.Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
        }
    }

    fun takeScreenshot() {
        scope.launch {
