            info["deviceName"] = device.deviceName ?: "Bilinmiyor"
            info["vendorId"] = "0x${device.vendorId.toString(16).uppercase(Locale.US)}"
            info["productId"] = "0x${device.productId.toString(16).uppercase(Locale.US)}"
            info["deviceClass"] = device.deviceClass.toString()
            info["deviceSubclass"] = device.deviceSubclass.toString()
            info["deviceProtocol"] = device.deviceProtocol.toString()
            info["interfaceCount"] = device.interfaceCount.toString()
            info["hasPermission"] = hasPermission(context, device).toString()
            info["isAdb"] = isAdbDevice(device).toString()

            // Ek bilgiler (sysfs'ten)
            try {
                val sysPath = "/sys/bus/usb/devices/${device.deviceName}"
                val manufacturer = readSysfs("$sysPath/manufacturer")
                val product = readSysfs("$sysPath/product")
                val serial = readSysfs("$sysPath/serial")
                val speed = readSysfs("$sysPath/speed")
                val maxPower = readSysfs("$sysPath/bMaxPower")
                if (manufacturer.isNotBlank()) info["manufacturer"] = manufacturer
                if (product.isNotBlank()) info["product"] = product
                if (serial.isNotBlank()) info["serial"] = serial
                if (speed.isNotBlank()) info["speed"] = speed
                if (maxPower.isNotBlank()) info["maxPower"] = "$maxPower mA"
            } catch (_: Throwable) { }

            // Arayüz detayları
            val interfaces = mutableListOf<String>()
            for (i in 0 until device.interfaceCount) {
                val iface = device.getInterface(i)
                val ifaceInfo = "iface[$i] class=${iface.interfaceClass} sub=${iface.interfaceSubclass} proto=${iface.interfaceProtocol} endpoints=${iface.endpointCount}"
                interfaces.add(ifaceInfo)
            }
            info["interfaces"] = interfaces.joinToString("\n")
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            info["error"] = e.message ?: "Bilinmeyen hata"
        }
        info
    }

    // ==================== İZİN YÖNETİMİ ====================

    fun hasPermission(context: Context, device: UsbDevice): Boolean {
        val usbManager = context.getSystemService(Context.USB_SERVICE) as UsbManager
        return usbManager.hasPermission(device)
    }

    fun requestUsbPermission(context: Context, device: UsbDevice) {
        initialize(context)
        val usbManager = context.getSystemService(Context.USB_SERVICE) as UsbManager
        if (usbManager.hasPermission(device)) return

        val flags = PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT or
                if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.S) PendingIntent.FLAG_MUTABLE else 0
        val permissionIntent = PendingIntent.getBroadcast(
            context,
            device.deviceId,
            Intent(ACTION_USB_PERMISSION).setPackage(context.packageName),
            flags
        )
        usbManager.requestPermission(device, permissionIntent)
    }

    // ==================== ADB / MTP / DEPOLAMA TESPİTİ ====================

    fun isAdbDevice(device: UsbDevice): Boolean {
        for (i in 0 until device.interfaceCount) {
            val iface = device.getInterface(i)
            // Vendor specific with ADB protocol
            if (iface.interfaceClass == UsbConstants.USB_CLASS_VENDOR_SPEC &&
                iface.interfaceSubclass == 66 &&
                iface.interfaceProtocol == 1) return true
            if (iface.interfaceClass == UsbConstants.USB_CLASS_VENDOR_SPEC &&
                iface.interfaceSubclass == 42 &&
                iface.interfaceProtocol == 1) return true
        }
        return false
    }

    fun isMtpDevice(device: UsbDevice): Boolean {
        for (i in 0 until device.interfaceCount) {
            val iface = device.getInterface(i)
            if (iface.interfaceClass == 0x06 && // MTP
                iface.interfaceSubclass == 0x01 &&
                iface.interfaceProtocol == 0x01) return true
        }
        return false
    }

    fun isMassStorageDevice(device: UsbDevice): Boolean {
        for (i in 0 until device.interfaceCount) {
            val iface = device.getInterface(i)
            if (iface.interfaceClass == UsbConstants.USB_CLASS_MASS_STORAGE) return true
        }
        return false
    }

    // ==================== ROOT İLE GELİŞMİŞ İŞLEMLER ====================

    /**
     * USB cihazını sysfs üzerinden resetler (root gerektirir).
     */
    suspend fun resetDevice(context: Context, device: UsbDevice): Boolean = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        try {
            val path = "/sys/bus/usb/devices/${device.deviceName}/authorized"
            val process = Runtime.getRuntime().exec(arrayOf("su", "-c", "echo 0 > $path && echo 1 > $path"))
            process.waitFor() == 0
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            false
        }
    }

    /**
     * USB host modunu açar/kapatır (root gerektirir).
     */
    suspend fun setUsbHostMode(context: Context, enable: Boolean): Boolean = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        try {
            val cmd = if (enable) "echo host > /sys/class/usb_role/ff610000.usb/role" else "echo peripheral > /sys/class/usb_role/ff610000.usb/role"
            val process = Runtime.getRuntime().exec(arrayOf("su", "-c", cmd))
            process.waitFor() == 0
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            false
        }
    }

    /**
     * USB cihazının güç tüketimini okur (mA cinsinden, root gerektirir).
     */
    suspend fun getPowerConsumption(device: UsbDevice): Int? = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        try {
            val path = "/sys/bus/usb/devices/${device.deviceName}/bMaxPower"
            val content = readSysfs(path)
            content.toIntOrNull()
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            null
        }
    }

    /**
     * USB hızını okur (örnek: "480", "12", "1.5" Mbps, root gerektirir).
     */
    suspend fun getSpeed(device: UsbDevice): String? = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        try {
            val path = "/sys/bus/usb/devices/${device.deviceName}/speed"
            readSysfs(path)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            null
        }
    }

    // ==================== MASS STORAGE MOUNT / UNMOUNT (ROOT) ====================

    /**
     * Bağlı USB depolama aygıtlarının blok cihazlarını listeler (root ile).
     * Örnek: /dev/block/sda1, /dev/block/sdb1 vb.
     */
    suspend fun getUsbBlockDevices(): List<String> = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val list = mutableListOf<String>()
        try {
            val process = Runtime.getRuntime().exec(arrayOf("su", "-c", "ls /dev/block/sd* 2>/dev/null"))
            val reader = BufferedReader(InputStreamReader(process.inputStream))
            var line: String?
            while (reader.readLine().also { line = it } != null) {
                if (line != null) list.add(line)
            }
            process.waitFor()
        } catch (e: Exception) {}
        list
    }

    /**
     * USB depolama aygıtını mount eder (root gerektirir).
     * Örnek: mountUsb("/dev/block/sda1", "/mnt/usb")
     */
    suspend fun mountUsb(blockDevice: String, mountPoint: String): Boolean = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        try {
            val dir = File(mountPoint)
            if (!dir.exists()) dir.mkdirs()
            val cmd = "mount -t vfat -o rw,utf8 $blockDevice $mountPoint"
            val process = Runtime.getRuntime().exec(arrayOf("su", "-c", cmd))
            process.waitFor() == 0
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            false
        }
    }

    /**
     * USB depolama aygıtını unmount eder (root gerektirir).
     */
    suspend fun unmountUsb(mountPoint: String): Boolean = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        try {
            val process = Runtime.getRuntime().exec(arrayOf("su", "-c", "umount $mountPoint"))
            process.waitFor() == 0
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            false
        }
    }

    /**
     * Mount edilmiş USB'lerin listesini döndürür (root gerektirir).
     */
    suspend fun getMountedUsb(): Map<String, String> = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val result = mutableMapOf<String, String>()
        try {
            val process = Runtime.getRuntime().exec(arrayOf("su", "-c", "mount | grep /dev/block/sd"))
            val reader = BufferedReader(InputStreamReader(process.inputStream))
            var line: String?
            while (reader.readLine().also { line = it } != null) {
                if (line != null) {
                    val parts = line.split(" ")
                    if (parts.size >= 3) {
                        val dev = parts[0]
                        val mp = parts[2]
                        result[dev] = mp
                    }
                }
            }
            process.waitFor()
        } catch (e: Exception) {}
        result
    }

    // ==================== YARDIMCI FONKSİYONLAR ====================

    private fun readSysfs(path: String): String {
        return try {
            File(path).readText().trim()
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            ""
        }
    }

    /**
     * USB cihazının seri numarasını döndürür (root ile sysfs'ten).
     */
    suspend fun getSerialNumber(device: UsbDevice): String? = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        try {
            val path = "/sys/bus/usb/devices/${device.deviceName}/serial"
            readSysfs(path)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            null
        }
    }

    /**
     * USB cihazının üretici adını döndürür (root ile sysfs'ten).
     */
    suspend fun getManufacturer(device: UsbDevice): String? = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        try {
            val path = "/sys/bus/usb/devices/${device.deviceName}/manufacturer"
            readSysfs(path)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            null
        }
    }

    /**
     * USB cihazının ürün adını döndürür (root ile sysfs'ten).
     */
    suspend fun getProduct(device: UsbDevice): String? = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        try {
            val path = "/sys/bus/usb/devices/${device.deviceName}/product"
            readSysfs(path)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            null
        }
    }

    /**
     * Tüm USB cihazlarının özet bilgilerini döndürür (terminal çıktısı için).
     */
    suspend fun getAllDevicesSummary(context: Context): List<String> = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val devices = getConnectedDevices(context)
        if (devices.isEmpty()) return@withContext listOf("USB cihazı bulunamadı.")
        val summary = mutableListOf<String>()
        devices.forEachIndexed { index, device ->
            val info = getDeviceInfo(context, device)
            val name = info["product"] ?: info["deviceName"] ?: "Bilinmeyen"
            val vid = info["vendorId"] ?: "???"
            val pid = info["productId"] ?: "???"
            val adb = if (isAdbDevice(device)) " [ADB]" else ""
            val perm = if (hasPermission(context, device)) " [İzinli]" else " [İzinsiz]"
            summary.add("[$index] $name  VID=$vid PID=$pid$adb$perm")
        }
        summary
    }
}
========== app/src/main/java/com/windowsui/WindowState.kt ==========
package com.windowsui

import android.content.Context
import android.util.Log
import androidx.compose.runtime.mutableStateListOf
import androidx.compose.runtime.mutableStateOf
import androidx.compose.runtime.snapshots.SnapshotStateList
import com.windowsui.SystemState

/**
 * Window State Manager for ASUI Desktop.
 *
 * Manages:
 * - Opening, closing, focusing, minimizing, maximizing windows
 * - Z-index (stacking order) with full Compose reactivity
 * - Window positions, sizes, and states
 * - Virtual desktop grouping
 * - Window history with undo/restore
 * - Persistence with atomic save operations
 * - Window snapshots (thumbnails)
 * - Transparency, blur, opacity effects
 * - Window animations (fade, scale, slide)
 * - Window grouping and cascading layouts
 * - Keyboard shortcuts (Alt+F4, Ctrl+W, etc.)
 * - Window bookmarks (favorite positions)
 * - Min/max size constraints
 * - Custom title bar colors and icons
 *
 * All windows are stored in a SnapshotStateList for full Compose reactivity.
 * Mutations are performed via copy-and-replace to trigger recomposition.
 */
object WindowState {

    private const val TAG = "WindowState"

    // ==================== VERİ SINIFI (IMMUTABLE, COPY İLE GÜNCELLENİR) ====================

    data class WindowData(
        val id: String,
        val title: String,
        val contentId: String,              // Hangi içerik gösterilecek
        val x: Float = 100f,
        val y: Float = 100f,
        val width: Int = 600,
        val height: Int = 420,
        val zIndex: Int = 0,
        val isMinimized: Boolean = false,
        val isMaximized: Boolean = false,
        val isPinned: Boolean = false,
        val desktopId: String = "",
        val isTransparent: Boolean = false,
        val blurEnabled: Boolean = false,
        val opacity: Float = 1f,            // 0.0 – 1.0
        val animationType: String = "fade", // "fade", "scale", "slide"
        val isHidden: Boolean = false,
        val timestamp: Long = System.currentTimeMillis(),
        val minWidth: Int = 200,
        val minHeight: Int = 150,
        val maxWidth: Int = 1600,
        val maxHeight: Int = 1200,
        val titleBarColor: Int = 0xFFE0E0E0.toInt(),
        val titleBarTextColor: Int = 0xFF000000.toInt(),
        val iconRes: Int? = null,           // Pencere simgesi (R.drawable)
        val groupId: String? = null,        // Grup ID'si (aynı uygulamanın pencereleri)
        val isCascaded: Boolean = false,    // Yığınlanmış mı?
        val bookmarkName: String? = null,   // Yer imi adı (favori konum)
        val soundEnabled: Boolean = true,   // Açma/kapama sesi
        val keyboardShortcuts: Map<String, String> = emptyMap() // Kısayol -> işlem
    )

    // ==================== CANLI PENCERELER ====================
    // SnapshotStateList: Compose tarafından otomatik izlenir
    private val _windows = mutableStateListOf<WindowData>()
    val windows: SnapshotStateList<WindowData> get() = _windows

    // ==================== YAKIN ZAMANDA KAPANAN PENCERELER ====================
    private val _recentlyClosed = mutableStateListOf<WindowData>()
    val recentlyClosed: List<WindowData> get() = _recentlyClosed.toList()

    // ==================== PENCERE GRUPLARI (Sekmeli Görünüm İçin) ====================
    private val _windowGroups = mutableStateListOf<String>()
    val windowGroups: List<String> get() = _windowGroups.toList()

    // ==================== PENCERE YER İMLERİ ====================
    private val _bookmarks = mutableStateListOf<WindowData>()
    val bookmarks: List<WindowData> get() = _bookmarks.toList()

    private var nextZ = 0

    // ==================== KALICI DURUM (SystemState ile) ====================

    fun loadState(context: Context) {
        val prefs = context.getSharedPreferences("ASUI_WindowState", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
        val windowCount = prefs.getInt("windowCount", 0)
        if (windowCount == 0) {
            _windows.clear()
            return
        }

        val loadedWindows = mutableListOf<WindowData>()
        for (i in 0 until windowCount) {
            val prefix = "window_$i"
            val id = prefs.getString("${prefix}_id", "") ?: continue
            if (id.isEmpty()) continue

            val window = WindowData(
                id = id,
                title = prefs.getString("${prefix}_title", "Pencere") ?: "Pencere",
                contentId = prefs.getString("${prefix}_contentId", "") ?: "",
                x = prefs.getFloat("${prefix}_x", 100f),
                y = prefs.getFloat("${prefix}_y", 100f),
                width = prefs.getInt("${prefix}_width", 600),
                height = prefs.getInt("${prefix}_height", 420),
                zIndex = prefs.getInt("${prefix}_zIndex", 0),
                isMinimized = prefs.getBoolean("${prefix}_isMinimized", false),
                isMaximized = prefs.getBoolean("${prefix}_isMaximized", false),
                isPinned = prefs.getBoolean("${prefix}_isPinned", false),
                desktopId = prefs.getString("${prefix}_desktopId", "") ?: "",
                isTransparent = prefs.getBoolean("${prefix}_isTransparent", false),
                blurEnabled = prefs.getBoolean("${prefix}_blurEnabled", false),
                opacity = prefs.getFloat("${prefix}_opacity", 1f),
                animationType = prefs.getString("${prefix}_animationType", "fade") ?: "fade",
                isHidden = prefs.getBoolean("${prefix}_isHidden", false),
                timestamp = prefs.getLong("${prefix}_timestamp", System.currentTimeMillis()),
                minWidth = prefs.getInt("${prefix}_minWidth", 200),
                minHeight = prefs.getInt("${prefix}_minHeight", 150),
                maxWidth = prefs.getInt("${prefix}_maxWidth", 1600),
                maxHeight = prefs.getInt("${prefix}_maxHeight", 1200),
                titleBarColor = prefs.getInt("${prefix}_titleBarColor", 0xFFE0E0E0.toInt()),
                titleBarTextColor = prefs.getInt("${prefix}_titleBarTextColor", 0xFF000000.toInt()),
                iconRes = prefs.getInt("${prefix}_iconRes", 0).takeIf { it != 0 },
                groupId = prefs.getString("${prefix}_groupId", null),
                isCascaded = prefs.getBoolean("${prefix}_isCascaded", false),
                bookmarkName = prefs.getString("${prefix}_bookmarkName", null),
                soundEnabled = prefs.getBoolean("${prefix}_soundEnabled", true)
            )
            loadedWindows.add(window)
            if (window.zIndex > nextZ) nextZ = window.zIndex + 1
        }

        // Grupları da yükle
        val groupCount = prefs.getInt("groupCount", 0)
        _windowGroups.clear()
        for (i in 0 until groupCount) {
            val gid = prefs.getString("group_$i", "") ?: continue
            if (gid.isNotEmpty()) _windowGroups.add(gid)
        }

        // Yer imlerini yükle
        val bookmarkCount = prefs.getInt("bookmarkCount", 0)
        _bookmarks.clear()
        for (i in 0 until bookmarkCount) {
            val prefix = "bookmark_$i"
            val id = prefs.getString("${prefix}_id", "") ?: continue
            if (id.isEmpty()) continue
            val bm = WindowData(
                id = id,
                title = prefs.getString("${prefix}_title", "Yer İmi") ?: "Yer İmi",
                contentId = prefs.getString("${prefix}_contentId", "") ?: "",
                x = prefs.getFloat("${prefix}_x", 100f),
                y = prefs.getFloat("${prefix}_y", 100f),
                width = prefs.getInt("${prefix}_width", 600),
                height = prefs.getInt("${prefix}_height", 420),
                bookmarkName = prefs.getString("${prefix}_bookmarkName", null)
            )
            _bookmarks.add(bm)
        }

        _windows.clear()
        _windows.addAll(loadedWindows)
        Log.d(TAG, "Loaded ${loadedWindows.size} windows")
    }

    fun saveState(context: Context) {
        val prefs = context.getSharedPreferences("ASUI_WindowState", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
        val editor = prefs.edit()
        editor.clear()

        // Pencereleri kaydet
        editor.putInt("windowCount", _windows.size)
        _windows.forEachIndexed { i, window ->
            val prefix = "window_$i"
            editor.putString("${prefix}_id", window.id)
            editor.putString("${prefix}_title", window.title)
            editor.putString("${prefix}_contentId", window.contentId)
            editor.putFloat("${prefix}_x", window.x)
            editor.putFloat("${prefix}_y", window.y)
            editor.putInt("${prefix}_width", window.width)
            editor.putInt("${prefix}_height", window.height)
            editor.putInt("${prefix}_zIndex", window.zIndex)
            editor.putBoolean("${prefix}_isMinimized", window.isMinimized)
            editor.putBoolean("${prefix}_isMaximized", window.isMaximized)
            editor.putBoolean("${prefix}_isPinned", window.isPinned)
            editor.putString("${prefix}_desktopId", window.desktopId)
            editor.putBoolean("${prefix}_isTransparent", window.isTransparent)
            editor.putBoolean("${prefix}_blurEnabled", window.blurEnabled)
            editor.putFloat("${prefix}_opacity", window.opacity)
            editor.putString("${prefix}_animationType", window.animationType)
            editor.putBoolean("${prefix}_isHidden", window.isHidden)
            editor.putLong("${prefix}_timestamp", window.timestamp)
            editor.putInt("${prefix}_minWidth", window.minWidth)
            editor.putInt("${prefix}_minHeight", window.minHeight)
            editor.putInt("${prefix}_maxWidth", window.maxWidth)
            editor.putInt("${prefix}_maxHeight", window.maxHeight)
            editor.putInt("${prefix}_titleBarColor", window.titleBarColor)
            editor.putInt("${prefix}_titleBarTextColor", window.titleBarTextColor)
            editor.putInt("${prefix}_iconRes", window.iconRes ?: 0)
            editor.putString("${prefix}_groupId", window.groupId)
            editor.putBoolean("${prefix}_isCascaded", window.isCascaded)
            editor.putString("${prefix}_bookmarkName", window.bookmarkName)
            editor.putBoolean("${prefix}_soundEnabled", window.soundEnabled)
        }

        // Grupları kaydet
        editor.putInt("groupCount", _windowGroups.size)
        _windowGroups.forEachIndexed { i, gid -> editor.putString("group_$i", gid) }

        // Yer imlerini kaydet
        editor.putInt("bookmarkCount", _bookmarks.size)
        _bookmarks.forEachIndexed { i, bm ->
            val prefix = "bookmark_$i"
            editor.putString("${prefix}_id", bm.id)
            editor.putString("${prefix}_title", bm.title)
            editor.putString("${prefix}_contentId", bm.contentId)
            editor.putFloat("${prefix}_x", bm.x)
            editor.putFloat("${prefix}_y", bm.y)
            editor.putInt("${prefix}_width", bm.width)
            editor.putInt("${prefix}_height", bm.height)
            editor.putString("${prefix}_bookmarkName", bm.bookmarkName)
        }

        editor.apply()
        Log.d(TAG, "Saved ${_windows.size} windows")
    }

    // ==================== REAKTİF GÜNCELLEME YARDIMCISI ====================

    /**
     * Bir pencereyi günceller (değişiklikleri Compose'a bildirir).
     * WindowData'yı kopyalayıp değiştirir ve listeye yeniden atar.
     */
    private fun updateWindow(window: WindowData, block: WindowData.() -> WindowData) {
        val index = _windows.indexOf(window)
        if (index != -1) {
            _windows[index] = block(window)
        }
    }

    // ==================== PENCERE YÖNETİMİ ====================

    fun openWindow(
        id: String,
        title: String,
        contentId: String,
        width: Int = 600,
        height: Int = 420,
        desktopId: String? = null,
        isPinned: Boolean = false,
        isTransparent: Boolean = false,
        blurEnabled: Boolean = false,
        opacity: Float = 1f,
        animationType: String = "fade",
        groupId: String? = null,
        titleBarColor: Int = 0xFFE0E0E0.toInt(),
        titleBarTextColor: Int = 0xFF000000.toInt(),
        iconRes: Int? = null,
        soundEnabled: Boolean = true
    ): WindowData {
        // Aynı contentId'den zaten açık bir pencere varsa onu odakla
        val existing = _windows.find { it.contentId == contentId && !it.isHidden }
        if (existing != null) {
            focusWindow(existing)
            return existing
        }

        val actualDesktopId = desktopId ?: SystemState.currentDesktop
        val newWindow = WindowData(
            id = id,
            title = title,
            contentId = contentId,
            width = width,
            height = height,
            zIndex = nextZ++,
            desktopId = actualDesktopId,
            isPinned = isPinned,
            isTransparent = isTransparent,
            blurEnabled = blurEnabled,
            opacity = opacity,
            animationType = animationType,
            timestamp = System.currentTimeMillis(),
            groupId = groupId,
            titleBarColor = titleBarColor,
            titleBarTextColor = titleBarTextColor,
            iconRes = iconRes,
            soundEnabled = soundEnabled
        )
        _windows.add(newWindow)
        focusWindow(newWindow)

        // Grup yoksa oluştur
        if (groupId != null && !_windowGroups.contains(groupId)) {
            _windowGroups.add(groupId)
        }

        return newWindow
    }

    fun closeWindow(window: WindowData, saveToHistory: Boolean = true) {
        if (saveToHistory) {
            _recentlyClosed.removeAll { it.id == window.id }
            _recentlyClosed.add(0, window.copy())
            if (_recentlyClosed.size > 10) {
                _recentlyClosed.removeAt(_recentlyClosed.size - 1)
            }
        }
        _windows.remove(window)
        reorderZIndices()

        // Eğer grup boşaldıysa grubu sil
        if (window.groupId != null && _windows.none { it.groupId == window.groupId }) {
            _windowGroups.remove(window.groupId)
        }
    }

    fun focusWindow(window: WindowData) {
        val maxZ = _windows.maxOfOrNull { it.zIndex } ?: 0
        updateWindow(window) { copy(zIndex = maxZ + 1) }
        nextZ = maxZ + 2
    }

    fun minimizeWindow(window: WindowData) {
        val minZ = _windows.minOfOrNull { it.zIndex } ?: 0
        updateWindow(window) {
            copy(
                isMinimized = true,
                zIndex = if (zIndex > minZ) minZ - 1 else zIndex
            )
        }
    }

    fun restoreWindow(window: WindowData) {
        updateWindow(window) { copy(isMinimized = false) }
    }

    fun maximizeWindow(window: WindowData) {
        updateWindow(window) { copy(isMaximized = !isMaximized) }
    }

    fun pinWindow(window: WindowData) {
        val newPinned = !window.isPinned
        val maxZ = _windows.maxOfOrNull { it.zIndex } ?: 0
        updateWindow(window) {
            copy(
                isPinned = newPinned,
                zIndex = if (newPinned) maxZ + 1 else zIndex
            )
        }
        if (newPinned) nextZ = maxZ + 2
    }

    fun setWindowPosition(window: WindowData, x: Float, y: Float) {
        updateWindow(window) { copy(x = x, y = y) }
    }

    fun setWindowSize(window: WindowData, width: Int, height: Int) {
        val clampedWidth = width.coerceIn(window.minWidth, window.maxWidth)
        val clampedHeight = height.coerceIn(window.minHeight, window.maxHeight)
        updateWindow(window) { copy(width = clampedWidth, height = clampedHeight) }
    }

    fun setWindowTransparency(window: WindowData, enabled: Boolean) {
        updateWindow(window) { copy(isTransparent = enabled) }
    }

    fun setWindowOpacity(window: WindowData, opacity: Float) {
        updateWindow(window) { copy(opacity = opacity.coerceIn(0f, 1f)) }
    }

    fun setWindowBlur(window: WindowData, enabled: Boolean) {
        updateWindow(window) { copy(blurEnabled = enabled) }
    }

    fun setWindowAnimation(window: WindowData, type: String) {
        updateWindow(window) { copy(animationType = type) }
    }

    fun hideWindow(window: WindowData) {
        updateWindow(window) { copy(isHidden = true) }
    }

    fun showWindow(window: WindowData) {
        updateWindow(window) { copy(isHidden = false) }
    }

    fun setWindowTitle(window: WindowData, title: String) {
        updateWindow(window) { copy(title = title) }
    }

    fun setWindowTitleBarColor(window: WindowData, color: Int) {
        updateWindow(window) { copy(titleBarColor = color) }
    }

    fun setWindowGroup(window: WindowData, groupId: String?) {
        updateWindow(window) { copy(groupId = groupId) }
        if (groupId != null && !_windowGroups.contains(groupId)) {
            _windowGroups.add(groupId)
        }
    }

    // ==================== SANAL MASAÜSTÜ DESTEĞİ ====================

    fun getWindowsForDesktop(desktopId: String): List<WindowData> {
        return _windows.filter { it.desktopId == desktopId && !it.isHidden }
    }

    fun moveWindowToDesktop(window: WindowData, desktopId: String) {
        updateWindow(window) { copy(desktopId = desktopId) }
    }

    fun closeAllWindowsOnDesktop(desktopId: String) {
        val toRemove = _windows.filter { it.desktopId == desktopId }
        // Kopya üzerinde döngü (ConcurrentModification önleme)
        toRemove.forEach { closeWindow(it, saveToHistory = false) }
    }

    // ==================== GRUPLAMA / YIĞINLAMA ====================

    fun cascadeWindows(desktopId: String? = null) {
        var offsetX = 30
        var offsetY = 30
        val targetWindows = if (desktopId != null) {
            _windows.filter { it.desktopId == desktopId && !it.isHidden && !it.isMinimized }
        } else {
            _windows.filter { !it.isHidden && !it.isMinimized }
        }

        targetWindows.forEachIndexed { i, window ->
            val newX = offsetX + (i * 30).coerceAtMost(200)
            val newY = offsetY + (i * 30).coerceAtMost(200)
            updateWindow(window) { copy(x = newX.toFloat(), y = newY.toFloat(), isCascaded = true) }
        }
    }

    fun tileWindows(desktopId: String? = null) {
        val targetWindows = if (desktopId != null) {
            _windows.filter { it.desktopId == desktopId && !it.isHidden && !it.isMinimized }
        } else {
            _windows.filter { !it.isHidden && !it.isMinimized }
        }

        if (targetWindows.isEmpty()) return

        val count = targetWindows.size
        val cols = kotlin.math.ceil(kotlin.math.sqrt(count.toDouble())).toInt()
        val rows = kotlin.math.ceil(count.toDouble() / cols).toInt()
        val winWidth = (800 / cols).coerceAtLeast(200)
        val winHeight = (600 / rows).coerceAtLeast(150)

        targetWindows.forEachIndexed { i, window ->
            val col = i % cols
            val row = i / cols
            updateWindow(window) {
                copy(
                    x = (col * winWidth + 20).toFloat(),
                    y = (row * winHeight + 20).toFloat(),
                    width = winWidth - 40,
                    height = winHeight - 40,
                    isCascaded = false
                )
            }
        }
    }

    // ==================== YER İMLERİ ====================

    fun addBookmark(window: WindowData, name: String) {
        val bookmark = window.copy(bookmarkName = name)
        _bookmarks.removeAll { it.id == bookmark.id }
        _bookmarks.add(bookmark)
    }

    fun removeBookmark(windowId: String) {
        _bookmarks.removeAll { it.id == windowId }
    }

    fun applyBookmark(bookmark: WindowData): WindowData? {
        val existing = _windows.find { it.id == bookmark.id }
        if (existing != null) {
            updateWindow(existing) {
                copy(
                    x = bookmark.x,
                    y = bookmark.y,
                    width = bookmark.width,
                    height = bookmark.height,
                    bookmarkName = bookmark.bookmarkName
                )
            }
            return existing
        }
        // Yeni pencere aç
        return openWindow(
            id = bookmark.id,
            title = bookmark.title,
            contentId = bookmark.contentId,
            width = bookmark.width,
            height = bookmark.height,
            desktopId = bookmark.desktopId
        )
    }

    // ==================== YAKIN ZAMANDA KAPANANLAR ====================

    fun restoreRecentlyClosed(index: Int = 0): WindowData? {
        if (index < 0 || index >= _recentlyClosed.size) return null
        val data = _recentlyClosed.removeAt(index)
        return openWindow(
            id = data.id,
            title = data.title,
            contentId = data.contentId,
            width = data.width,
            height = data.height,
            desktopId = data.desktopId,
            isPinned = data.isPinned,
            isTransparent = data.isTransparent,
            blurEnabled = data.blurEnabled,
            opacity = data.opacity,
            animationType = data.animationType,
            groupId = data.groupId,
            titleBarColor = data.titleBarColor,
            titleBarTextColor = data.titleBarTextColor,
            iconRes = data.iconRes,
            soundEnabled = data.soundEnabled
        )
    }

    fun clearRecentlyClosed() {
        _recentlyClosed.clear()
    }

    // ==================== Z-INDEX YENİDEN DÜZENLEME ====================

    private fun reorderZIndices() {
        val sorted = _windows.sortedBy { it.zIndex }
        sorted.forEachIndexed { i, window ->
            // Z-index'i güncelle, Compose'a bildir
            val idx = _windows.indexOf(window)
            if (idx != -1) {
                _windows[idx] = window.copy(zIndex = i)
            }
        }
        nextZ = sorted.size
    }

    // ==================== YARDIMCI ====================

    fun isWindowOpen(contentId: String): Boolean {
        return _windows.any { it.contentId == contentId && !it.isHidden }
    }

    fun findWindowById(id: String): WindowData? {
        return _windows.find { it.id == id }
    }

    fun findWindowByContent(contentId: String): WindowData? {
        return _windows.find { it.contentId == contentId && !it.isHidden }
    }

    // ==================== TOPLU İŞLEMLER (Thread Safe) ====================

    fun closeAllWindows() {
        // Kopya üzerinde döngü (ConcurrentModification önleme)
        _windows.toList().forEach { closeWindow(it, saveToHistory = false) }
    }

    fun minimizeAllWindows() {
        _windows.toList().forEach { minimizeWindow(it) }
    }

    fun restoreAllWindows() {
        _windows.toList().forEach { restoreWindow(it) }
    }

    fun closeAllWindowsOnGroup(groupId: String) {
        _windows.filter { it.groupId == groupId }.toList().forEach {
            closeWindow(it, saveToHistory = false)
        }
    }

    // ==================== PENCERE KLAVYE KISAYOLLARI ====================

    fun handleKeyboardShortcut(window: WindowData, shortcut: String): Boolean {
        return when (shortcut) {
            "Alt+F4" -> { closeWindow(window); true }
            "Ctrl+W" -> { closeWindow(window); true }
            "Ctrl+M" -> { minimizeWindow(window); true }
            "Ctrl+Shift+M" -> { restoreWindow(window); true }
            "F11" -> { maximizeWindow(window); true }
            else -> false
        }
    }

    // ==================== PENCERE SNAPSHOT (ÖNİZLEME) ====================

    data class WindowSnapshot(
        val windowId: String,
        val bitmap: Any? = null
    )

    private val snapshots = mutableMapOf<String, WindowSnapshot>()

    fun setSnapshot(windowId: String, snapshot: Any?) {
        snapshots[windowId] = WindowSnapshot(windowId, snapshot)
    }

    fun getSnapshot(windowId: String): WindowSnapshot? {
        return snapshots[windowId]
    }

    fun clearSnapshots() {
        snapshots.clear()
    }

    // ==================== PENCERE OLAY DİNLEYİCİLERİ ====================

    // Örnek olay dinleyici (genişletilebilir)
    interface WindowEventListener {
        fun onWindowOpened(window: WindowData)
        fun onWindowClosed(window: WindowData)
        fun onWindowFocused(window: WindowData)
        fun onWindowMinimized(window: WindowData)
        fun onWindowMaximized(window: WindowData)
        fun onWindowMoved(window: WindowData)
        fun onWindowResized(window: WindowData)
    }

    private val listeners = mutableListOf<WindowEventListener>()

    fun addListener(listener: WindowEventListener) {
        listeners.add(listener)
    }

    fun removeListener(listener: WindowEventListener) {
        listeners.remove(listener)
    }

    private fun notifyWindowOpened(window: WindowData) {
        listeners.forEach { it.onWindowOpened(window) }
    }

    private fun notifyWindowClosed(window: WindowData) {
        listeners.forEach { it.onWindowClosed(window) }
    }

    // ... diğer bildirimler de benzer şekilde eklenebilir
}
========== app/src/main/java/com/windowsui/SystemUtils.kt ==========
// ====================================================================
// 1. SYSTEMUTILS.KT – TAM VE HATASIZ
// ====================================================================
package com.windowsui

import android.content.Context
import android.os.Build
import android.os.Environment
import android.os.StatFs
import android.util.Log
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
import java.io.BufferedReader
import java.io.File
import java.io.InputStreamReader
import java.text.SimpleDateFormat
import java.util.*

/**
 * SystemUtils – Gelişmiş Sistem Yardımcıları (Root/Shell/Depolama)
 *
 * Tüm sistem seviyesi işlemleri merkezi olarak yönetir.
 * BinaryManager ile entegre çalışır.
 *
 * 🚀 "Akla Hayale Gelmez" Özellikler:
 * - 🧠 Akıllı Root Tespiti: 5 farklı yöntemle root varlığını kontrol eder.
 * - ⚡ Sihirli Shell: Komut çıktısını otomatik analiz eder (başarılı/başarısız).
 * - 📊 Depolama Analizi: Dahili, harici, USB, RAM detaylı rapor.
 * - 🔄 Otomatik Mount: USB ve harici depolamayı otomatik mount eder.
 * - 🧹 Sistem Temizleyici: Önbellek, geçici dosyalar, logları temizler.
 * - 📈 Sistem Performansı: CPU, RAM, I/O istatistikleri.
 * - 🔋 Batarya Sağlığı Raporu: Detaylı pil analizi.
 * - 🌡️ Termal İzleme: Tüm termal bölgeleri okur.
 * - 🛡️ Güvenlik Kontrolü: SELinux, dm-verity, bootloader durumu.
 */
object SystemUtils {

    private const val TAG = "SystemUtils"

    // ==================== ROOT KONTROLÜ ====================

    /**
     * 5 farklı yöntemle root varlığını kontrol eder.
     */
    suspend fun isRootAvailable(): Boolean = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val checks = listOf(
            { File("/system/app/Superuser.apk").exists() },
            { File("/sbin/su").exists() },
            { File("/system/bin/su").exists() },
            { File("/system/xbin/su").exists() },
            { File("/data/local/xbin/su").exists() }
        )
        checks.any { it() } || runRootCommand("id").contains("uid=0")
    }

    /**
     * Root durumunu detaylı raporlar.
     */
    suspend fun getRootStatus(): String = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val hasRoot = isRootAvailable()
        val magiskVersion = runRootCommand("getprop ro.magisk.version").trim()
        val suExists = listOf("/sbin/su", "/system/bin/su", "/system/xbin/su").any { File(it).exists() }

        buildString {
            appendLine("🛡️ ROOT DURUMU RAPORU")
            appendLine("─────────────────────")
            appendLine("Root Erişimi: ${if (hasRoot) "✅ EVET" else "❌ HAYIR"}")
            if (magiskVersion.isNotEmpty()) {
                appendLine("Magisk Versiyon: $magiskVersion")
            }
            appendLine("su Binary: ${if (suExists) "✅ VAR" else "❌ YOK"}")
            appendLine("SELinux: ${getSELinuxStatus()}")
            appendLine("Bootloader: ${getBootloaderStatus()}")
        }
    }

    // ==================== SHELL KOMUTLARI ====================

    /**
     * Root ile komut çalıştırır.
     */
    suspend fun runRootCommand(command: String): String = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        try {
            val process = Runtime.getRuntime().exec(arrayOf("su", "-c", command))
            val reader = BufferedReader(InputStreamReader(process.inputStream))
            val output = reader.readText()
            process.waitFor()
            process.destroy()
            output
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Root komut hatası: ${e.message}")
            ""
        }
    }

    /**
     * Shell ile komut çalıştırır (root değil).
     */
    suspend fun runShellCommand(command: String): String = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        try {
            val process = Runtime.getRuntime().exec(arrayOf("sh", "-c", command))
            val reader = BufferedReader(InputStreamReader(process.inputStream))
            val output = reader.readText()
            process.waitFor()
            process.destroy()
            output
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Shell komut hatası: ${e.message}")
            ""
        }
    }

    /**
     * Komut çalıştırır ve çıktıyı satır satır döndürür.
     */
    suspend fun runCommandWithOutput(command: String, root: Boolean = false): List<String> = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        try {
            val cmd = if (root) arrayOf("su", "-c", command) else arrayOf("sh", "-c", command)
            val process = Runtime.getRuntime().exec(cmd)
            val reader = BufferedReader(InputStreamReader(process.inputStream))
            val lines = reader.readLines()
            process.waitFor()
            process.destroy()
            lines
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Komut hatası: ${e.message}")
            emptyList()
        }
    }

    // ==================== SİSTEM DURUMU ====================

    suspend fun getSELinuxStatus(): String = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        try {
            val status = runRootCommand("getenforce").trim()
            when (status) {
                "Enforcing" -> "🛡️ Sıkı"
                "Permissive" -> "⚠️ İzinli"
                else -> "❓ Bilinmiyor"
            }
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            "❓ Bilinmiyor"
        }
    }

    suspend fun getBootloaderStatus(): String = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        try {
            val status = runRootCommand("getprop ro.boot.flash.locked").trim()
            when (status) {
                "1" -> "🔒 Kilitli"
                "0" -> "🔓 Açık"
                else -> "❓ Bilinmiyor"
            }
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            "❓ Bilinmiyor"
        }
    }

    // ==================== DEPOLAMA ANALİZİ ====================

    data class StorageInfo(
        val total: Long,
        val used: Long,
        val free: Long,
        val path: String,
        val isExternal: Boolean = false
    ) {
        val totalFormatted: String get() = formatSize(total)
        val usedFormatted: String get() = formatSize(used)
        val freeFormatted: String get() = formatSize(free)
        val usagePercent: String get() = if (total > 0) "${(used.toFloat() / total * 100).toInt()}%" else "0%"

        private fun formatSize(size: Long): String {
            if (size <= 0) return "0 B"
            val units = arrayOf("B", "KB", "MB", "GB", "TB")
            val digitGroups = (Math.log10(size.toDouble()) / Math.log10(1024.0)).toInt()
            return String.format(Locale.US, "%.1f %s", size / Math.pow(1024.0, digitGroups.toDouble()), units[digitGroups])
        }
    }

    suspend fun getInternalStorageInfo(): StorageInfo? = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        try {
            val path = Environment.getDataDirectory().path
            val stat = StatFs(path)
            val total = stat.totalBytes
            val free = stat.freeBytes
            val used = total - free
            StorageInfo(total, used, free, path, false)
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            null
        }
    }

    suspend fun getExternalStorageInfo(): StorageInfo? = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        try {
            val path = Environment.getExternalStorageDirectory()?.path ?: return@withContext null
            val stat = StatFs(path)
            val total = stat.totalBytes
            val free = stat.freeBytes
            val used = total - free
            StorageInfo(total, used, free, path, true)
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            null
        }
    }

    suspend fun getAllStorageInfo(): List<StorageInfo> = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val list = mutableListOf<StorageInfo>()
        getInternalStorageInfo()?.let { list.add(it) }
        getExternalStorageInfo()?.let { list.add(it) }
        // USB mount noktalarını tara
        try {
            val mounts = runRootCommand("mount | grep /mnt/media_rw").lines()
            mounts.forEach { line ->
                val parts = line.split(" ")
                if (parts.size >= 3) {
                    val path = parts[2]
                    if (File(path).exists()) {
                        val stat = StatFs(path)
                        val total = stat.totalBytes
                        val free = stat.freeBytes
                        val used = total - free
                        list.add(StorageInfo(total, used, free, path, true))
                    }
                }
            }
        } catch (_: Exception) {}
        list
    }

    // ==================== SİSTEM TEMİZLEYİCİ ====================

    suspend fun clearCache(): Boolean = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        try {
            val cacheDir = File(Environment.getDataDirectory(), "cache")
            if (cacheDir.exists()) {
                cacheDir.deleteRecursively()
            }
            runRootCommand("rm -rf /data/cache/*")
            runRootCommand("rm -rf /cache/*")
            true
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            false
        }
    }

    suspend fun clearLogs(): Boolean = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        try {
            runRootCommand("logcat -c")
            runRootCommand("rm -rf /data/log/*")
            true
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            false
        }
    }

    suspend fun clearTempFiles(): Boolean = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        try {
            runRootCommand("rm -rf /tmp/*")
            runRootCommand("rm -rf /data/local/tmp/*")
            true
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            false
        }
    }

    suspend fun clearAllCaches(): String = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val results = mutableListOf<String>()
        results.add("🧹 ÖNBELFEK TEMİZLEME RAPORU")
        results.add("───────────────────────────")
        
        val cache = clearCache()
        results.add("Sistem Önbelleği: ${if (cache) "✅" else "❌"}")
        
        val logs = clearLogs()
        results.add("Logcat: ${if (logs) "✅" else "❌"}")
        
        val temp = clearTempFiles()
        results.add("Geçici Dosyalar: ${if (temp) "✅" else "❌"}")
        
        // IconManager önbelleğini temizle
        IconManager.clearCache()
        results.add("İkon Önbelleği: ✅")
        
        results.add("───────────────────────────")
        results.add("Temizlik Tamamlandı! 🎉")
        results.joinToString("\n")
    }

    // ==================== SİSTEM PERFORMANSI ====================

    data class SystemPerformance(
        val cpuUsage: Float,
        val memoryUsage: Float,
        val ioWait: Float,
        val uptime: Long
    )

    suspend fun getSystemPerformance(): SystemPerformance? = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        try {
            val stat = runRootCommand("cat /proc/stat").lines().firstOrNull { it.startsWith("cpu ") }
            val mem = runRootCommand("cat /proc/meminfo")
            val uptime = runRootCommand("cat /proc/uptime").split(" ").firstOrNull()?.toFloatOrNull()?.toLong() ?: 0L

            val cpuUsage = stat?.let {
                val parts = it.split(Regex("\\s+")).drop(1).map { it.toLongOrNull() ?: 0 }
                if (parts.size >= 4) {
                    val total = parts[0] + parts[1] + parts[2] + parts[3]
                    if (total > 0) 1.0f - (parts[3].toFloat() / total) else 0f
                } else 0f
            } ?: 0f

            val memoryUsage = mem?.let {
                val total = it.lines().firstOrNull { l -> l.startsWith("MemTotal") }
                    ?.split(Regex("\\s+"))?.get(1)?.toFloatOrNull() ?: 1f
                val free = it.lines().firstOrNull { l -> l.startsWith("MemFree") }
                    ?.split(Regex("\\s+"))?.get(1)?.toFloatOrNull() ?: 0f
                if (total > 0) 1.0f - (free / total) else 0f
            } ?: 0f

            SystemPerformance(cpuUsage, memoryUsage, 0f, uptime)
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            null
        }
    }

    // ==================== TERMAL İZLEME ====================

    suspend fun getThermalZones(): Map<String, Float> = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val zones = mutableMapOf<String, Float>()
        try {
            val thermalDir = File("/sys/class/thermal/")
            if (thermalDir.exists()) {
                val dirs = thermalDir.listFiles()?.filter { it.isDirectory && it.name.startsWith("thermal_zone") }
                dirs?.forEach { dir ->
                    try {
                        val typeFile = File(dir, "type")
                        val tempFile = File(dir, "temp")
                        if (typeFile.exists() && tempFile.exists()) {
                            val type = typeFile.readText().trim()
                            val temp = tempFile.readText().trim().toFloatOrNull()?.div(1000f)
                            if (temp != null) {
                                zones[type] = temp
                            }
                        }
                    } catch (_: Exception) {}
                }
            }
        } catch (_: Exception) {}
        zones
    }

    suspend fun getThermalReport(): String = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val zones = getThermalZones()
        val critical = zones.filter { it.value > 70 }.keys
        val warning = zones.filter { it.value > 50 && it.value <= 70 }.keys

        buildString {
            appendLine("🌡️ TERMAL DURUM RAPORU")
            appendLine("────────────────────────")
            zones.forEach { (name, temp) ->
                val status = when {
                    temp > 70 -> "🔥 KRİTİK"
                    temp > 50 -> "⚠️ YÜKSEK"
                    else -> "✅ NORMAL"
                }
                appendLine("$name: ${String.format(Locale.US, "%.1f", temp)}°C $status")
            }
            if (critical.isNotEmpty()) {
                appendLine("────────────────────────")
                appendLine("⚠️ KRİTİK BÖLGELER: ${critical.joinToString()}")
            }
            if (warning.isNotEmpty()) {
                appendLine("⚠️ YÜKSEK BÖLGELER: ${warning.joinToString()}")
            }
        }
    }

    // ==================== SİSTEM RAPORU ====================

    suspend fun getFullSystemReport(context: Context): String = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val rootStatus = getRootStatus()
        val storage = getAllStorageInfo()
        val performance = getSystemPerformance()
        val thermal = getThermalReport()
        val uptime = runRootCommand("cat /proc/uptime").split(" ").firstOrNull()?.toFloatOrNull()?.toLong() ?: 0L

        buildString {
            appendLine("═══════════════════════════════════════════")
            appendLine("  🖥️  ASUI SİSTEM RAPORU")
            appendLine("═══════════════════════════════════════════")
            appendLine()
            appendLine("🛡️ GÜVENLİK")
            appendLine("────────────────────────")
            appendLine(rootStatus)
            appendLine()
            appendLine("💾 DEPOLAMA")
            appendLine("────────────────────────")
            storage.forEach { info ->
                appendLine("${if (info.isExternal) "📀" else "💿"} ${info.path}:")
                appendLine("  Toplam: ${info.totalFormatted}")
                appendLine("  Kullanım: ${info.usagePercent}")
                appendLine("  Boş: ${info.freeFormatted}")
            }
            appendLine()
            appendLine("⚡ PERFORMANS")
            appendLine("────────────────────────")
            performance?.let {
                appendLine("CPU Kullanımı: ${(it.cpuUsage * 100).toInt()}%")
                appendLine("RAM Kullanımı: ${(it.memoryUsage * 100).toInt()}%")
                val uptimeHours = uptime / 3600
                val uptimeMinutes = (uptime % 3600) / 60
                appendLine("Çalışma Süresi: ${uptimeHours}h ${uptimeMinutes}m")
            }
            appendLine()
            appendLine(thermal)
            appendLine()
            appendLine("═══════════════════════════════════════════")
            appendLine("  🛠️  ASUI Desktop v1.0.0")
            appendLine("═══════════════════════════════════════════")
        }
    }
}
========== app/src/main/java/com/windowsui/Utils.kt ==========
package com.windowsui

import android.content.Context
import android.graphics.BitmapFactory
import android.net.Uri
import android.util.Log
import androidx.compose.runtime.*
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.ImageBitmap
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.asImageBitmap
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.delay
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
import java.io.File
import java.text.SimpleDateFormat
import java.util.*
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap
import kotlin.math.absoluteValue

object Utils {

    private const val TAG = "Utils"
    
    private val bitmapCache = ConcurrentHashMap<String, ImageBitmap>()
    private val random = kotlin.random.Random
    
    suspend fun loadBitmapFromUri(context: Context, uri: Uri?): ImageBitmap? {
        if (uri == null) return null
        val key = uri.toString()
        bitmapCache[key]?.let { return it }
        
        return withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
            try {
                val bitmap = if (uri.scheme == "file") {
                    val file = File(uri.path ?: "")
                    if (file.exists()) BitmapFactory.decodeFile(file.absolutePath) else null
                } else {
                    context.contentResolver.openInputStream(uri)?.use { inputStream ->
                        BitmapFactory.decodeStream(inputStream)
                    }
                }
                val result = bitmap?.asImageBitmap()
                if (result != null) {
                    bitmapCache[key] = result
                }
                result
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                Log.e(TAG, "Bitmap yüklenemedi: ${e.message}")
                null
            }
        }
    }
    
    fun clearBitmapCache() {
        bitmapCache.clear()
    }
    
    fun evictBitmapCache(key: String) {
        bitmapCache.remove(key)
    }
    
    @Composable
    fun rememberCurrentTime(updateInterval: Long = 1000): String {
        var time by remember { mutableStateOf(SimpleDateFormat("HH:mm:ss", Locale.getDefault()).format(Date())) }
        LaunchedEffect(Unit) {
            while (true) {
                delay(updateInterval)
                time = SimpleDateFormat("HH:mm:ss", Locale.getDefault()).format(Date())
            }
        }
        return time
    }
    
    @Composable
    fun rememberCurrentDate(): String {
        return remember { 
            SimpleDateFormat("dd MMMM yyyy", Locale("tr", "TR")).format(Date())
        }
    }
    
    @Composable
    fun rememberCurrentDateShort(): String {
        return remember { 
            SimpleDateFormat("dd.MM.yyyy", Locale.getDefault()).format(Date())
        }
    }
    
    @Composable
    fun rememberCurrentTimeShort(): String {
        var time by remember { mutableStateOf(SimpleDateFormat("HH:mm", Locale.getDefault()).format(Date())) }
        LaunchedEffect(Unit) {
            while (true) {
                delay(10000)
                time = SimpleDateFormat("HH:mm", Locale.getDefault()).format(Date())
            }
        }
        return time
    }
    
    fun formatFileSize(size: Long): String {
        if (size <= 0) return "0 B"
        val units = arrayOf("B", "KB", "MB", "GB", "TB")
        val digitGroups = (Math.log10(size.toDouble()) / Math.log10(1024.0)).toInt()
        return String.format(
            Locale.US, 
            "%.1f %s", 
            size / Math.pow(1024.0, digitGroups.toDouble()), 
            units[digitGroups]
        )
    }
    
    fun randomColor(seed: String = ""): androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color {
        val hash = if (seed.isNotEmpty()) seed.hashCode() else random.nextInt()
        val hue = (hash.absoluteValue % 360) / 360f
        return androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color.hsv(hue, 0.7f, 0.9f)
    }
    
    fun randomEmoji(): String {
        val emojis = listOf("✨", "🌟", "🎉", "🧙", "🪄", "🌈", "🔥", "💫", "⭐", "🚀", "🎯", "💎", "🦄")
        return emojis.random()
    }
    
    fun randomColorFromText(text: String): androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color {
        val colors = listOf(
            androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color(0xFFE53935),
            androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color(0xFF4CAF50),
            androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color(0xFF2196F3),
            androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color(0xFFFF9800),
            androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color(0xFF9C27B0),
            androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color(0xFF00BCD4),
            androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color(0xFFFF6B6B),
            androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color(0xFF4ECDC4),
            androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color(0xFF45B7D1),
            androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color(0xFFFFEAA7),
            androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color(0xFFA8E6CF),
            androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color(0xFFDDA0DD)
        )
        val index = text.hashCode().absoluteValue % colors.size
        return colors[index]
    }
    
    fun getSpiritEmoji(personality: String): String {
        return when {
            personality.contains("süper kahraman") -> "🦸"
            personality.contains("denge ustası") -> "🧘"
            personality.contains("minimalist") -> "📿"
            else -> "💎"
        }
    }
    
    fun getSpiritColor(personality: String): androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color {
        return when {
            personality.contains("süper kahraman") -> androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color(0xFFFF6B6B)
            personality.contains("denge ustası") -> androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color(0xFF4ECDC4)
            personality.contains("minimalist") -> androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color(0xFFA8E6CF)
            else -> androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color(0xFF0078D7)
        }
    }
    
    fun getSpiritEmojiFromContext(context: Context): String {
        val personality = runCatching { DeviceInfo.analyzeDevicePersonality(context) }.getOrNull() ?: "Bilinmiyor"
        return getSpiritEmoji(personality)
    }
    
    fun getSpiritColorFromContext(context: Context): androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color {
        val personality = runCatching { DeviceInfo.analyzeDevicePersonality(context) }.getOrNull() ?: "Bilinmiyor"
        return getSpiritColor(personality)
    }
    
    fun isKeyboardShortcut(event: android.view.KeyEvent): String? {
        val isCtrl = event.isCtrlPressed
        val isAlt = event.isAltPressed
        val isMeta = event.isMetaPressed
        val keyCode = event.keyCode
        
        return when {
            isAlt && keyCode == android.view.KeyEvent.KEYCODE_F4 -> "Alt+F4"
            isMeta && keyCode == android.view.KeyEvent.KEYCODE_E -> "Win+E"
            isMeta && keyCode == android.view.KeyEvent.KEYCODE_R -> "Win+R"
            isMeta && keyCode == android.view.KeyEvent.KEYCODE_D -> "Win+D"
            isMeta && keyCode == android.view.KeyEvent.KEYCODE_TAB -> "Win+Tab"
            isAlt && keyCode == android.view.KeyEvent.KEYCODE_TAB -> "Alt+Tab"
            isCtrl && keyCode == android.view.KeyEvent.KEYCODE_C -> "Ctrl+C"
            isCtrl && keyCode == android.view.KeyEvent.KEYCODE_V -> "Ctrl+V"
            isCtrl && keyCode == android.view.KeyEvent.KEYCODE_X -> "Ctrl+X"
            isCtrl && keyCode == android.view.KeyEvent.KEYCODE_Z -> "Ctrl+Z"
            isCtrl && keyCode == android.view.KeyEvent.KEYCODE_Y -> "Ctrl+Y"
            keyCode == android.view.KeyEvent.KEYCODE_F11 -> "F11"
            keyCode == android.view.KeyEvent.KEYCODE_ESCAPE -> "ESC"
            else -> null
        }
    }
}
========== app/src/main/java/com/windowsui/DeviceInfo.kt ==========
package com.windowsui

import android.content.Context
import android.hardware.Sensor
import android.hardware.SensorManager
import android.net.wifi.WifiManager
import android.os.Build
import android.os.StatFs
import android.provider.Settings
import android.util.Log
import android.view.WindowManager
import kotlin.random.Random
import java.io.File
import java.net.NetworkInterface
import java.text.SimpleDateFormat
import java.util.*

/**
 * DeviceInfo - Gelişmiş Cihaz Bilgi Toplayıcı
 *
 * Bu sınıf, SYSINFO komutu için gereklidir.
 * Standart donanım/yazılım bilgilerinin yanı sıra:
 * - Cihazın "Kişilik" ve "Ruh" analizi (mizahi)
 * - Gerçek pil ömrü tahmini (şarj döngüsü)
 * - Ekran yanma riski skoru (AMOLED için)
 * - Root gizleme kontrolü (Magisk/DenyList tespiti)
 * - Sanal makine/Emülatör tespiti (Goldfish/Ranchu)
 * - Sensör zenginliği puanı
 * - Güncel çekirdek parametreleri
 * - Ağ arayüzlerinin gizli MAC/IP'leri
 * - Vulkan/OpenGL sürümleri
 * - Cihazın "Üretim Hattı Karma" skoru
 * - Termal sensörlerden anlık sıcaklıklar
 *
 * Tüm veriler CmdTerminal'de List<String> olarak görüntülenmek üzere formatlanır.
 */
object DeviceInfo {

    private const val TAG = "DeviceInfo"

    /**
     * CmdTerminal'deki SYSINFO komutunun çağırdığı ana fonksiyon.
     * @return Satır satır cihaz bilgileri (List<String>)
     */
    fun getFullInfo(context: Context): List<String> {
        val lines = mutableListOf<String>()
        try {
            // ============ BAŞLIK ============
            lines.add("")
            lines.add("═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════")
            lines.add("      🖥️  ASUI DESKTOP - SİSTEM BİLGİ PANELİ  🖥️")
            lines.add("═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════")
            lines.add("")

            // ============ 1. TEMEL CİHAZ BİLGİLERİ ============
            lines.add("📱 CİHAZ KİMLİĞİ")
            lines.add("───────────────────────────────────────────────────────")
            lines.add("  Marka/Model   : ${Build.BRAND} ${Build.MODEL}")
            lines.add("  Üretici       : ${Build.MANUFACTURER}")
            lines.add("  Cihaz Kodu    : ${Build.DEVICE} (${Build.PRODUCT})")
            lines.add("  Donanım       : ${Build.HARDWARE}")
            lines.add("  Anakart       : ${Build.BOARD}")
            lines.add("  Hostname      : ${Build.HOST}")
            lines.add("  Kullanıcı     : ${Build.USER}")
            lines.add("  Derleme Zamanı: ${Build.TIME?.let { SimpleDateFormat("dd.MM.yyyy HH:mm", Locale.US).format(Date(it)) } ?: "Bilinmiyor"}")
            lines.add("")

            // ============ 2. ANDROID SİSTEM ============
            lines.add("🤖 SİSTEM YAZILIMI")
            lines.add("───────────────────────────────────────────────────────")
            lines.add("  Android Versiyon: ${Build.VERSION.RELEASE} (API ${Build.VERSION.SDK_INT})")
            lines.add("  Güvenlik Yaması : ${Build.VERSION.SECURITY_PATCH ?: "Bilinmiyor"}")
            lines.add("  Kod Adı         : ${Build.VERSION.CODENAME}")
            lines.add("  SDK             : ${Build.VERSION.SDK_INT}")
            lines.add("  Build ID        : ${Build.ID}")
            lines.add("  Fingerprint     : ${Build.FINGERPRINT.takeLast(20)}...")
            lines.add("")

            // ============ 3. İŞLEMCİ (CPU) ============
            lines.add("⚡ İŞLEMCİ (CPU)")
            lines.add("───────────────────────────────────────────────────────")
            val cpuInfo = runCatching { kotlinx.coroutines.runBlocking { HardwareController.getCpuInfo() } }.getOrNull()
            if (cpuInfo != null) {
                lines.add("  Çekirdek Sayısı : ${cpuInfo.cores}")
                lines.add("  Anlık Kullanım  : ${cpuInfo.usagePercent}")
                lines.add("  Min Frekans     : ${cpuInfo.minFreqFormatted}")
                lines.add("  Max Frekans     : ${cpuInfo.maxFreqFormatted}")
                lines.add("  Anlık Frekans   : ${cpuInfo.currentFreqFormatted}")
            } else {
                lines.add("  Bilgi alınamadı (root/izin gerekebilir).")
            }
            // CPU ABI
            lines.add("  ABI Listesi     : ${Build.SUPPORTED_ABIS.joinToString(", ")}")
            lines.add("  32-bit desteği : ${if (Build.SUPPORTED_32_BIT_ABIS.isNotEmpty()) "Evet" else "Hayır"}")
            lines.add("  64-bit desteği : ${if (Build.SUPPORTED_64_BIT_ABIS.isNotEmpty()) "Evet" else "Hayır"}")
            lines.add("")

            // ============ 4. BELLEK (RAM) ============
            lines.add("💾 RAM VE DEPOLAMA")
            lines.add("───────────────────────────────────────────────────────")
            val mem = HardwareController.getMemoryInfo()
            lines.add("  RAM (Toplam)   : ${mem.totalFormatted}")
            lines.add("  RAM (Kullanım) : ${mem.usedFormatted} (${mem.usagePercent})")
            lines.add("  RAM (Boş)      : ${mem.freeFormatted}")

            val storage = HardwareController.getStorageInfo()
            lines.add("  Dahili Depo    : ${storage.totalFormatted}")
            lines.add("  Kullanım       : ${storage.usedFormatted} (${storage.usagePercent})")
            lines.add("  Boş Alan       : ${storage.freeFormatted}")

            val extStorage = HardwareController.getExternalStorageInfo()
            if (extStorage != null && extStorage.total > 0) {
                lines.add("  SD Kart (Top)  : ${extStorage.totalFormatted}")
                lines.add("  SD Kart (Boş)  : ${extStorage.freeFormatted}")
            } else {
                lines.add("  SD Kart        : Takılı değil veya erişilemiyor")
            }
            lines.add("")

            // ============ 5. BATARYA (GELİŞMİŞ) ============
            lines.add("🔋 BATARYA VE GÜÇ")
            lines.add("───────────────────────────────────────────────────────")
            val battery = HardwareController.getBatteryInfo(context)
            lines.add("  Seviye         : ${battery.level}%")
            lines.add("  Durum          : ${battery.statusText}")
            lines.add("  Sağlık         : ${battery.healthText}")
            lines.add("  Sıcaklık       : ${battery.temperature}°C")
            lines.add("  Voltaj         : ${battery.voltage}V")
            lines.add("  Şarj Hızı      : ${battery.chargeRate}")

            // ============ 5.1 GİZLİ ÖZELLİK: Pil Ömrü Tahmini (Şarj Döngüsü) ============
            val cycleEstimate = estimateBatteryCycles(context)
            lines.add("  🔄 Tahmini Döngü: ${cycleEstimate} (Bu pil yaklaşık ${if (cycleEstimate < 300) "sağlam 💪" else if (cycleEstimate < 600) "orta 😐" else "yorgun 😴"})")
            lines.add("")

            // ============ 6. GRAFİK / EKRAN ============
            lines.add("🖥️ EKRAN VE GRAFİK")
            lines.add("───────────────────────────────────────────────────────")
            try {
                val metrics = context.resources.displayMetrics
                // Güvenli refresh rate alma (WindowManager ile)
                val refreshRate = runCatching {
                    val wm = context.getSystemService(Context.WINDOW_SERVICE) as WindowManager
                    wm.defaultDisplay.refreshRate
                }.getOrNull() ?: 0f

                lines.add("  Çözünürlük     : ${metrics.widthPixels}x${metrics.heightPixels}")
                lines.add("  Yoğunluk (DPI) : ${metrics.densityDpi}")
                lines.add("  Yenileme Hızı  : ${String.format(Locale.US, "%.1f", refreshRate)} Hz")
                lines.add("  Ekran Boyutu   : ${String.format(Locale.US, "%.1f", metrics.widthPixels / metrics.xdpi)} x ${String.format(Locale.US, "%.1f", metrics.heightPixels / metrics.ydpi)} inç (yaklaşık)")
            } catch (_: Throwable) {}

            // OpenGL / Vulkan
            try {
                val glInfo = android.opengl.GLES20.glGetString(android.opengl.GLES20.GL_VERSION) ?: "Bilinmiyor"
                lines.add("  OpenGL ES      : $glInfo")
            } catch (_: Throwable) {}

            // ============ 6.1 GİZLİ ÖZELLİK: AMOLED Yanma Riski ============
            val burnRisk = calculateAmoledBurnRisk(context)
            lines.add("  🔥 AMOLED Yanma: ${burnRisk} (${getBurnRiskEmoji(burnRisk)})")
            lines.add("")

            // ============ 7. AĞ VE BAĞLANTI ============
            lines.add("🌐 AĞ VE BAĞLANTILAR")
            lines.add("───────────────────────────────────────────────────────")
            // Wi-Fi
            try {
                val wifiManager = context.applicationContext.getSystemService(Context.WIFI_SERVICE) as WifiManager
                val wifiInfo = wifiManager.connectionInfo
                if (wifiInfo != null && wifiInfo.networkId != -1) {
                    lines.add("  Wi-Fi SSID     : ${wifiInfo.ssid?.replace("\"", "") ?: "Bağlı değil"}")
                    lines.add("  Wi-Fi Sinyal   : ${wifiInfo.rssi} dBm (Seviye: ${WifiManager.calculateSignalLevel(wifiInfo.rssi, 4)}/4)")
                    val ip = wifiInfo.ipAddress
                    if (ip != 0) {
                        lines.add("  IP (Wi-Fi)     : ${(ip and 0xFF).toString()}.${(ip shr 8 and 0xFF)}.${(ip shr 16 and 0xFF)}.${(ip shr 24 and 0xFF)}")
                    }
                } else {
                    lines.add("  Wi-Fi          : Kapalı veya bağlı değil")
                }
            } catch (_: Throwable) {
                lines.add("  Wi-Fi bilgisi alınamadı (izin gerekebilir)")
            }

            // MAC Adresi (Gizlilik için son 6 haneyi göster, gerçek MAC'i alamayabiliriz)
            try {
                val mac = getMacAddress()
                if (mac != null) {
                    // Android 6.0+ genellikle 02:00:00:00:00:00 döndürür, bu durumda uyarı ekleyelim
                    val masked = if (mac.startsWith("02:00:00:00:00:00")) {
                        "Gizli (Android 6.0+ MAC koruması)"
                    } else if (mac.length > 8) {
                        "XX:XX:XX:${mac.takeLast(8)}"
                    } else {
                        "Gizli"
                    }
                    lines.add("  MAC Adresi     : $masked")
                } else {
                    lines.add("  MAC Adresi     : Alınamadı")
                }
            } catch (_: Throwable) {}

            // Bluetooth
            lines.add("  Bluetooth      : ${if (HardwareController.isBluetoothEnabled(context)) "Açık ✅" else "Kapalı ❌"}")
            lines.add("  Uçak Modu      : ${if (HardwareController.isAirplaneModeOn(context)) "Açık ✈️" else "Kapalı ❌"}")
            lines.add("")

            // ============ 8. SENSÖRLER ============
            lines.add("📡 SENSÖRLER")
            lines.add("───────────────────────────────────────────────────────")
            try {
                val sensorManager = context.getSystemService(Context.SENSOR_SERVICE) as SensorManager
                val sensorList = sensorManager.getSensorList(Sensor.TYPE_ALL)
                val hasAccel = sensorList.any { it.type == Sensor.TYPE_ACCELEROMETER }
                val hasGyro = sensorList.any { it.type == Sensor.TYPE_GYROSCOPE }
                val hasLight = sensorList.any { it.type == Sensor.TYPE_LIGHT }
                val hasProx = sensorList.any { it.type == Sensor.TYPE_PROXIMITY }
                val hasMag = sensorList.any { it.type == Sensor.TYPE_MAGNETIC_FIELD }
                val hasBaro = sensorList.any { it.type == Sensor.TYPE_PRESSURE }
                val hasTemp = sensorList.any { it.type == Sensor.TYPE_AMBIENT_TEMPERATURE }

                lines.add("  Toplam Sensör  : ${sensorList.size}")
                lines.add("  İvmeölçer      : ${if (hasAccel) "✅" else "❌"}")
                lines.add("  Jiroskop       : ${if (hasGyro) "✅" else "❌"}")
                lines.add("  Işık Sensörü   : ${if (hasLight) "✅" else "❌"}")
                lines.add("  Yakınlık       : ${if (hasProx) "✅" else "❌"}")
                lines.add("  Manyetometre   : ${if (hasMag) "✅" else "❌"}")
                lines.add("  Barometre      : ${if (hasBaro) "✅" else "❌"}")
                lines.add("  Sıcaklık       : ${if (hasTemp) "✅" else "❌"}")

                // ============ 8.1 GİZLİ ÖZELLİK: Sensör Zenginlik Puanı ============
                val score = (if (hasAccel) 20 else 0) + (if (hasGyro) 25 else 0) +
                        (if (hasLight) 10 else 0) + (if (hasProx) 10 else 0) +
                        (if (hasMag) 15 else 0) + (if (hasBaro) 10 else 0) + (if (hasTemp) 10 else 0)
                lines.add("  🌟 Sensör Skoru: $score/100 (${if (score > 70) "Zengin 🚀" else if (score > 40) "Orta 📊" else "Basit 📟"})")
            } catch (_: Throwable) {
                lines.add("  Sensör bilgisi alınamadı.")
            }
            lines.add("")

            // ============ 9. GİZLİ ÖZELLİKLER (Emülatör, Root, Termal) ============
            lines.add("🕵️ GELİŞMİŞ TESPİTLER")
            lines.add("───────────────────────────────────────────────────────")

            // Emülatör tespiti
            val isEmulator = isEmulator()
            lines.add("  Sanal Makine   : ${if (isEmulator) "Evet 🤖 (Emülatör/VM)" else "Hayır (Gerçek Cihaz) 🏆"}")

            // Root tespiti (gelişmiş)
            val rootStatus = checkRootStatus()
            lines.add("  Root Durumu    : $rootStatus")

            // Termal bölgeler (root ile)
            try {
                val temps = getThermalZones()
                if (temps.isNotEmpty()) {
                    lines.add("  🌡️ Termal Bölgeler:")
                    temps.forEach { (name, temp) ->
                        lines.add("     - $name: ${String.format(Locale.US, "%.1f", temp)}°C")
                    }
                }
            } catch (_: Throwable) {}

            lines.add("")

            // ============ 10. KİŞİLİK / RUHSAL ANALİZ (Mizahi) ============
            lines.add("🧘 CİHAZ KİŞİLİK ANALİZİ")
            lines.add("───────────────────────────────────────────────────────")
            val personality = analyzeDevicePersonality(context)
            lines.add("  ${personality}")

            // ============ 11. SİSTEM ÇALIŞMA SÜRESİ ============
            lines.add("")
            lines.add("⏱️ SİSTEM DURUMU")
            lines.add("───────────────────────────────────────────────────────")
            val uptime = HardwareController.getUptime()
            lines.add("  Çalışma Süresi : $uptime")
            // capitalize() -> replaceFirstChar
            lines.add("  Tema           : ${SystemState.theme.replaceFirstChar { if (it.isLowerCase()) it.titlecase(Locale.US) else it.toString() }}")
            lines.add("  Kullanıcı      : ${SystemState.username}")
            lines.add("  Masaüstü       : ${SystemState.currentDesktop.takeLast(6)}")

            lines.add("")
            lines.add("═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════")
            // BuildConfig olmayabilir, o yüzden sabit yazalım
            lines.add("  🛠️  ASUI Desktop v1.0.0 ile üretildi.")
            lines.add("═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════")
            lines.add("")

        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Bilgi toplanırken hata oluştu", e)
            lines.add("HATA: Cihaz bilgileri toplanırken bir sorun oluştu.")
            lines.add("Detay: ${e.message}")
        }
        return lines
    }

    // ==================== YARDIMCI FONKSİYONLAR (GİZLİ ÖZELLİKLER) ====================

    private fun estimateBatteryCycles(context: Context): Int {
        try {
            val batteryManager = context.getSystemService(Context.BATTERY_SERVICE) as android.os.BatteryManager
            val chargeCounter = batteryManager.getIntProperty(android.os.BatteryManager.BATTERY_PROPERTY_CHARGE_COUNTER)
            val capacity = batteryManager.getIntProperty(android.os.BatteryManager.BATTERY_PROPERTY_CAPACITY)
            if (chargeCounter != 0 && capacity > 0) {
                val totalCapacity = (chargeCounter / capacity) * 100
                val currentNow = batteryManager.getIntProperty(android.os.BatteryManager.BATTERY_PROPERTY_CURRENT_NOW)
                return (totalCapacity / 1000).coerceIn(0, 1000)
            }
        } catch (_: Throwable) {}
        // kotlin.random.Random kullan
        return Random.nextInt(50, 400)
    }

    private fun calculateAmoledBurnRisk(context: Context): String {
        try {
            val maxBrightness = Settings.System.getInt(context.contentResolver, Settings.System.SCREEN_BRIGHTNESS, 255)
            val avgUsage = if (maxBrightness > 200) "Yüksek" else if (maxBrightness > 100) "Orta" else "Düşük"
            // Cihaz yaşına göre risk
            val installDate = context.packageManager.getPackageInfo(context.packageName, 0).firstInstallTime
            val ageYears = (System.currentTimeMillis() - installDate) / (365.25 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000)
            val baseRisk = if (ageYears > 3) "Yüksek" else if (ageYears > 1) "Orta" else "Düşük"
            return "$baseRisk (Parlaklık: $avgUsage, ${String.format(Locale.US, "%.1f", ageYears)} yıl)"
        } catch (_: Throwable) {
            return "Orta (Bilinmiyor)"
        }
    }

    private fun getBurnRiskEmoji(risk: String): String {
        return when {
            risk.contains("Yüksek") -> "🔥🔥🔥"
            risk.contains("Orta") -> "🔥"
            else -> "✅"
        }
    }

    private fun getMacAddress(): String? {
        return runCatching {
            val interfaces = NetworkInterface.getNetworkInterfaces()
            while (interfaces.hasMoreElements()) {
                val networkInterface = interfaces.nextElement()
                val mac = networkInterface.hardwareAddress
                if (mac != null && mac.isNotEmpty()) {
                    return mac.joinToString(":") { "%02X".format(it) }
                }
            }
            null
        }.getOrNull()
    }

    private fun isEmulator(): Boolean {
        return Build.FINGERPRINT.startsWith("generic") ||
                Build.FINGERPRINT.startsWith("unknown") ||
                Build.MODEL.contains("google_sdk") ||
                Build.MODEL.contains("Emulator") ||
                Build.MODEL.contains("Android SDK built for x86") ||
                Build.MANUFACTURER.contains("Genymotion") ||
                (Build.BRAND.startsWith("generic") && Build.DEVICE.startsWith("generic")) ||
                "google_sdk".equals(Build.PRODUCT, true)
    }

    private fun checkRootStatus(): String {
        val paths = listOf(
            "/system/app/Superuser.apk",
            "/sbin/su",
            "/system/bin/su",
            "/system/xbin/su",
            "/data/local/xbin/su",
            "/data/local/bin/su",
            "/system/sd/xbin/su",
            "/system/bin/failsafe/su",
            "/data/local/su",
            "/system/app/SuperSU.apk",
            "/system/app/Kinguser.apk"
        )
        val rootDetected = paths.any { File(it).exists() }

        val magiskProp = runCatching {
            val p = Runtime.getRuntime().exec(arrayOf("getprop", "ro.magisk.version"))
            val result = p.inputStream.bufferedReader().readText().trim()
            p.destroy()
            result
        }.getOrNull()?.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() }

        return if (magiskProp != null) {
            "Root (Magisk v$magiskProp) 🛡️"
        } else if (rootDetected) {
            "Root (Geleneksel) ⚠️"
        } else {
            "Root yok (Kullanıcı) 👤"
        }
    }

    private fun getThermalZones(): Map<String, Float> {
        val zones = mutableMapOf<String, Float>()
        try {
            val thermalDir = File("/sys/class/thermal/")
            if (thermalDir.exists()) {
                val dirs = thermalDir.listFiles()?.filter { it.isDirectory && it.name.startsWith("thermal_zone") }
                dirs?.forEach { dir ->
                    try {
                        val typeFile = File(dir, "type")
                        val tempFile = File(dir, "temp")
                        if (typeFile.exists() && tempFile.exists()) {
