import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import java.text.SimpleDateFormat
import java.util.*

// ==================== VERİ SINIFLARI ====================

data class StoreApp(
    val packageName: String,
    val label: String,
    val icon: androidx.compose.ui.graphics.ImageBitmap? = null,
    val developer: String = "",
    val rating: Float = 0f,
    val installs: String = "",
    val isInstalled: Boolean = false,
    val isUpdatable: Boolean = false,
    val version: String = "",
    val description: String = "",
    val category: String = "Diğer",
    val screenshots: List<String> = emptyList(),
    val downloadCount: Int = 0
)

data class StoreCategory(
    val name: String,
    val icon: Int
)

data class DownloadProgress(
    val packageName: String,
    val progress: Float,
    val status: String // "downloading", "installing", "done", "failed"
)

// ==================== ASUI STORE ====================

@OptIn(ExperimentalAnimationApi::class)
@Composable
fun StoreWindow(
    onClose: () -> Unit
) {
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val pm = context.packageManager
    val scope = rememberCoroutineScope()

    // ============ STATE ============
    var searchQuery by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var selectedCategory by remember { mutableStateOf("Tümü") }
    var selectedApp by remember { mutableStateOf<StoreApp?>(null) }
    var isLoading by remember { mutableStateOf(true) }
    var installedApps by remember { mutableStateOf<List<StoreApp>>(emptyList()) }
    var recommendedApps by remember { mutableStateOf<List<StoreApp>>(emptyList()) }
    var downloadProgresses by remember { mutableStateOf<Map<String, DownloadProgress>>(emptyMap()) }

    // Kategoriler (Windows Store tarzı)
    val categories = listOf(
        "Tümü" to Icons.Default.Apps,
        "Oyun" to Icons.Default.Games,
        "Sosyal" to Icons.Default.Chat,
        "Müzik" to Icons.Default.MusicNote,
        "Fotoğraf" to Icons.Default.PhotoCamera,
        "Üretkenlik" to Icons.Default.Work,
        "Finans" to Icons.Default.AttachMoney,
        "Eğitim" to Icons.Default.School
    )

    // Varsayılan uygulama listesi (örnek)
    val defaultApps = listOf(
        StoreApp(
            packageName = "com.android.chrome",
            label = "Chrome",
            developer = "Google LLC",
            rating = 4.5f,
            installs = "5B+",
            category = "Üretkenlik",
            description = "Hızlı, güvenli ve kişiselleştirilebilir web tarayıcısı."
        ),
        StoreApp(
            packageName = "com.whatsapp",
            label = "WhatsApp",
            developer = "WhatsApp LLC",
            rating = 4.3f,
            installs = "2B+",
            category = "Sosyal",
            description = "Dünyanın en popüler mesajlaşma uygulaması."
        ),
        StoreApp(
            packageName = "com.spotify.music",
            label = "Spotify",
            developer = "Spotify AB",
            rating = 4.4f,
            installs = "1B+",
            category = "Müzik",
            description = "Milyonlarca şarkı ve podcast."
        ),
        StoreApp(
            packageName = "com.instagram.android",
            label = "Instagram",
            developer = "Meta Platforms",
            rating = 4.2f,
            installs = "1B+",
            category = "Sosyal",
            description = "Fotoğraf ve video paylaşma platformu."
        ),
        StoreApp(
            packageName = "com.netflix.mediaclient",
            label = "Netflix",
            developer = "Netflix Inc.",
            rating = 4.6f,
            installs = "500M+",
            category = "Eğlence",
            description = "Dizi, film ve belgesel platformu."
        ),
        StoreApp(
            packageName = "com.microsoft.office.officehubrow",
            label = "Microsoft Office",
            developer = "Microsoft Corporation",
            rating = 4.1f,
            installs = "500M+",
            category = "Üretkenlik",
            description = "Word, Excel, PowerPoint ve daha fazlası."
        )
    )

    // ============ YÜKLEME ============
    LaunchedEffect(Unit) {
        isLoading = true
        // Cihazdaki yüklü uygulamaları kontrol et
        val allApps = DesktopManager.loadApps(context, includeSystem = false)
        val installedPackages = allApps.map { it.packageName }.toSet()

        val updatedApps = defaultApps.map { app ->
            val isInstalled = installedPackages.contains(app.packageName)
            val isUpdatable = isInstalled // Gerçek güncelleme kontrolü ileride yapılabilir
            app.copy(
                isInstalled = isInstalled,
                isUpdatable = isUpdatable,
                // İkonları al (placeholder)
                icon = try {
                    val appInfo = pm.getApplicationInfo(app.packageName, 0)
                    appInfo.loadIcon(pm).toBitmap(96, 96).asImageBitmap()
                } catch (e: Exception) {
                    null
                }
            )
        }

        installedApps = updatedApps.filter { it.isInstalled }
        recommendedApps = updatedApps.filter { !it.isInstalled }
        isLoading = false
    }

    // ============ FİLTRELEME ============
    val filteredApps = remember(recommendedApps, searchQuery, selectedCategory) {
        recommendedApps.filter { app ->
            val matchesSearch = searchQuery.isEmpty() ||
                    app.label.contains(searchQuery, ignoreCase = true) ||
                    app.packageName.contains(searchQuery, ignoreCase = true)
            val matchesCategory = selectedCategory == "Tümü" || app.category == selectedCategory
            matchesSearch && matchesCategory
        }
    }

    // ============ UYGULAMA AÇMA / YÜKLEME / KALDIRMA ============
    fun openApp(app: StoreApp) {
        try {
            val intent = pm.getLaunchIntentForPackage(app.packageName)
            if (intent != null) {
                intent.addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK)
                context.startActivity(intent)
            } else {
                // Play Store'a yönlendir
                context.startActivity(Intent(Intent.ACTION_VIEW, Uri.parse("market://details?id=${app.packageName}")).addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK))
            }
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e("StoreWindow", "Uygulama açılamadı", e)
        }
    }

    fun downloadApp(app: StoreApp) {
        scope.launch {
            try {
                val intent = Intent(Intent.ACTION_VIEW, Uri.parse("market://details?id=${app.packageName}"))
                intent.addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK)
                context.startActivity(intent)
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                try {
                    val intent = Intent(Intent.ACTION_VIEW, Uri.parse("https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?id=${app.packageName}"))
                    intent.addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK)
                    context.startActivity(intent)
                } catch (ex: Exception) {
                    Log.e("StoreWindow", "Uygulama açılamadı", ex)
                }
            }
        }
    }

    fun updateApp(app: StoreApp) {
        downloadApp(app) // Güncelleme için Play Store'a yönlendir
    }

    fun uninstallApp(app: StoreApp) {
        scope.launch {
            try {
                val intent = Intent(Intent.ACTION_DELETE, Uri.parse("package:${app.packageName}"))
                intent.addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK)
                context.startActivity(intent)
            } catch (e: Exception) {}
        }
    }

    // ============ ANA UI ============
    Box(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .background(Color(0xCC000000))
            .clickable { /* Kapatma için değil, sadece arka plan */ }
    ) {
        Card(
            modifier = Modifier
                .align(Alignment.Center)
                .fillMaxSize(0.85f)
                .padding(16.dp)
                .shadow(32.dp, RoundedCornerShape(16.dp))
                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(16.dp))
                .background(
                    if (SystemState.theme == "dark") Color(0xFF1C1C1C) else Color.White
                )
                .clickable { /* Kartın içine tıklamayı engelleme */ },
            colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = Color.Transparent)
        ) {
            Column(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
                // ============ BAŞLIK ÇUBUĞU ============
                Row(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .background(
                            if (SystemState.theme == "dark") Color(0xFF2D2D2D) else Color(0xFFF0F0F0)
                        )
                        .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 12.dp),
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                ) {
                    Row(
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                        modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
                    ) {
                        Text("🛒", fontSize = 20.sp)
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(8.dp))
                        Text(
                            text = "ASUI Store",
                            color = if (SystemState.theme == "dark") Color.White else Color.Black,
                            fontSize = 20.sp,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                        )
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(8.dp))
                        Box(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
                                .background(Color(0xFF0078D7))
                                .padding(horizontal = 8.dp, vertical = 2.dp)
                        ) {
                            Text("Beta", color = Color.White, fontSize = 10.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                        }
                    }
                    IconButton(onClick = onClose) {
                        Icon(
                            Icons.Default.Close,
                            contentDescription = "Kapat",
                            tint = if (SystemState.theme == "dark") Color.White else Color.Black
                        )
                    }
                }

                // ============ ARAMA ÇUBUĞU ============
                Row(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 8.dp),
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)
                ) {
                    OutlinedTextField(
                        value = searchQuery,
                        onValueChange = { searchQuery = it },
                        modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                        placeholder = {
                            Text(
                                "Uygulama ara...",
                                color = if (SystemState.theme == "dark") Color.LightGray else Color.DarkGray
                            )
                        },
                        leadingIcon = { Icon(Icons.Default.Search, contentDescription = null) },
                        trailingIcon = {
                            if (searchQuery.isNotEmpty()) {
                                IconButton(onClick = { searchQuery = "" }) {
                                    Icon(Icons.Default.Close, contentDescription = "Temizle")
                                }
                            }
                        },
                        colors = OutlinedTextFieldDefaults.colors(
                            focusedBorderColor = Color(0xFF0078D7),
                            unfocusedBorderColor = if (SystemState.theme == "dark") Color.DarkGray else Color.LightGray,
                            focusedTextColor = if (SystemState.theme == "dark") Color.White else Color.Black,
                            unfocusedTextColor = if (SystemState.theme == "dark") Color.White else Color.Black
                        ),
                        singleLine = true,
                        keyboardOptions = KeyboardOptions(imeAction = ImeAction.Search),
                        keyboardActions = KeyboardActions(onSearch = { /* Arama yap */ })
                    )

                    // 🟢 FIX: DropdownMenu expanded kontrolü
                    var showCategoryMenu by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
                    Box {
                        OutlinedButton(
                            onClick = { showCategoryMenu = !showCategoryMenu },
                            colors = ButtonDefaults.outlinedButtonColors(
                                contentColor = if (SystemState.theme == "dark") Color.White else Color.Black
                            )
                        ) {
                            Icon(Icons.Default.FilterList, contentDescription = "Filtre")
                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(4.dp))
                            Text(selectedCategory.take(12))
                        }

                        DropdownMenu(
                            expanded = showCategoryMenu,
                            onDismissRequest = { showCategoryMenu = false },
                            modifier = Modifier.background(
                                if (SystemState.theme == "dark") Color(0xFF2D2D2D) else Color.White
                            )
                        ) {
                            categories.forEach { (name, icon) ->
                                DropdownMenuItem(
                                    text = {
                                        Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                                            Icon(icon, contentDescription = null, modifier = Modifier.size(16.dp))
                                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(8.dp))
                                            Text(name)
                                        }
                                    },
                                    onClick = {
                                        selectedCategory = name
                                        showCategoryMenu = false
                                    }
                                )
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }

                // ============ İÇERİK ============
                if (isLoading) {
                    Box(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(), contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) {
                        Column(horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally) {
                            CircularProgressIndicator()
                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(16.dp))
                            Text(
                                "Uygulamalar yükleniyor...",
                                color = if (SystemState.theme == "dark") Color.White else Color.Black
                            )
                        }
                    }
                } else {
                    if (selectedApp != null) {
                        StoreAppDetail(
                            app = selectedApp!!,
                            onBack = { selectedApp = null },
                            onOpen = { openApp(it) },
                            onDownload = { downloadApp(it) },
                            onUpdate = { updateApp(it) },
                            onUninstall = { uninstallApp(it) }
                        )
                    } else {
                        Column(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .fillMaxSize()
                                .padding(horizontal = 16.dp)
                        ) {
                            // Yüklü uygulamalar
                            if (installedApps.isNotEmpty()) {
                                Text(
                                    text = "📦 Yüklü Uygulamalar",
                                    color = if (SystemState.theme == "dark") Color.White else Color.Black,
                                    fontSize = 16.sp,
                                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                    modifier = Modifier.padding(vertical = 8.dp)
                                )
                                LazyRow(
                                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp),
                                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()
                                ) {
                                    items(installedApps.take(10)) { app ->
                                        StoreAppCard(
                                            app = app,
                                            onClick = { selectedApp = app },
                                            onAction = {
                                                if (app.isUpdatable) updateApp(app)
                                                else if (app.isInstalled) openApp(app)
                                                else downloadApp(app)
                                            }
                                        )
                                    }
                                }
                            }

                            // Önerilen uygulamalar
                            Text(
                                text = "✨ Önerilen Uygulamalar",
                                color = if (SystemState.theme == "dark") Color.White else Color.Black,
                                fontSize = 16.sp,
                                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                modifier = Modifier.padding(vertical = 8.dp)
                            )
                            LazyVerticalGrid(
                                columns = GridCells.Adaptive(minSize = 140.dp),
                                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp),
                                verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp),
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .fillMaxSize()
                                    .padding(bottom = 80.dp)
                            ) {
                                items(filteredApps) { app ->
                                    StoreAppCard(
                                        app = app,
                                        onClick = { selectedApp = app },
                                        onAction = {
                                            if (app.isUpdatable) updateApp(app)
                                            else if (app.isInstalled) openApp(app)
                                            else downloadApp(app)
                                        }
                                    )
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

// ==================== UYGULAMA KARTI ====================

@Composable
private fun StoreAppCard(
    app: StoreApp,
    onClick: () -> Unit,
    onAction: () -> Unit
) {
    val isDarkTheme = SystemState.theme == "dark"
    val cardColor = if (isDarkTheme) Color(0xFF2D2D2D) else Color(0xFFF5F5F5)
    val textColor = if (isDarkTheme) Color.White else Color.Black
    val secondaryTextColor = if (isDarkTheme) Color.LightGray else Color.DarkGray

    Card(
        modifier = Modifier
            .width(140.dp)
            .clickable { onClick() }
            .shadow(4.dp, RoundedCornerShape(12.dp)),
        colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = cardColor),
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(12.dp)
    ) {
        Column(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .padding(12.dp),
            horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally
        ) {
            if (app.icon != null) {
                Image(
                    bitmap = app.icon,
                    contentDescription = null,
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .size(64.dp)
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
                )
            } else {
                Box(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .size(64.dp)
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
                        .background(Color(0xFF0078D7)),
                    contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                ) {
                    Text(
                        text = app.label.take(2).uppercase(),
                        color = Color.White,
                        fontSize = 18.sp,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                    )
                }
            }

            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(8.dp))

            Text(
                text = app.label,
                color = textColor,
                fontSize = 13.sp,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium,
                maxLines = 1,
                overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
            )

            Text(
                text = app.developer,
                color = secondaryTextColor,
                fontSize = 10.sp,
                maxLines = 1,
                overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
            )

            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(4.dp))

            Row(
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.Center
            ) {
                Text("⭐", fontSize = 12.sp)
                // 🟢 FIX: Locale.US ile formatlama
                Text(
                    text = String.format(Locale.US, "%.1f", app.rating),
                    color = textColor,
                    fontSize = 12.sp
                )
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(4.dp))
                Text(
                    text = app.installs,
                    color = secondaryTextColor,
                    fontSize = 10.sp
                )
            }

            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(8.dp))

            Button(
                onClick = onAction,
                colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(
                    containerColor = when {
                        app.isUpdatable -> Color(0xFFFFA000)
                        app.isInstalled -> Color(0xFF4CAF50)
                        else -> Color(0xFF0078D7)
                    }
                ),
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                shape = RoundedCornerShape(20.dp)
            ) {
                Text(
                    text = when {
                        app.isUpdatable -> "Güncelle"
                        app.isInstalled -> "Aç"
                        else -> "Yükle"
                    },
                    color = Color.White,
                    fontSize = 11.sp,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

// ==================== UYGULAMA DETAYI ====================

@Composable
private fun StoreAppDetail(
    app: StoreApp,
    onBack: () -> Unit,
    onOpen: (StoreApp) -> Unit,
    onDownload: (StoreApp) -> Unit,
    onUpdate: (StoreApp) -> Unit,
    onUninstall: (StoreApp) -> Unit
) {
    val isDarkTheme = SystemState.theme == "dark"
    val textColor = if (isDarkTheme) Color.White else Color.Black
    val secondaryTextColor = if (isDarkTheme) Color.LightGray else Color.DarkGray
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val pm = context.packageManager

    // 🟢 FIX: Uygulama açma kontrolü
    val canOpen = try {
        pm.getLaunchIntentForPackage(app.packageName) != null
    } catch (e: Exception) {
        false
    }

    Column(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .padding(16.dp)
    ) {
        Row(
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()
        ) {
            IconButton(onClick = onBack) {
                Icon(Icons.Default.ArrowBack, contentDescription = "Geri", tint = textColor)
            }
            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(8.dp))
            Text(
                text = "Uygulama Detayı",
                color = textColor,
                fontSize = 18.sp,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
            )
        }

        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(16.dp))

        Row(
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(16.dp)
        ) {
            if (app.icon != null) {
                Image(
                    bitmap = app.icon,
                    contentDescription = null,
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .size(96.dp)
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(16.dp))
                )
            } else {
                Box(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .size(96.dp)
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(16.dp))
                        .background(Color(0xFF0078D7)),
                    contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                ) {
                    Text(
                        text = app.label.take(2).uppercase(),
                        color = Color.White,
                        fontSize = 32.sp,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                    )
                }
            }

            Column(
                modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                verticalArrangement = Arrangement.Center
            ) {
                Text(
                    text = app.label,
                    color = textColor,
                    fontSize = 22.sp,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                )
                Text(
                    text = app.developer,
                    color = secondaryTextColor,
                    fontSize = 14.sp
                )
                Row(
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)
                ) {
                    Text("⭐ ${String.format(Locale.US, "%.1f", app.rating)}", color = textColor)
                    Text("•", color = secondaryTextColor)
                    Text(app.installs, color = secondaryTextColor)
                    Text("•", color = secondaryTextColor)
                    Text(app.category, color = secondaryTextColor)
                }
                Text(
                    text = if (app.isInstalled) "✅ Yüklü" else "❌ Yüklü değil",
                    color = if (app.isInstalled) Color(0xFF4CAF50) else Color(0xFFE53935),
                    fontSize = 12.sp
                )
            }
        }

        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(16.dp))

        Card(
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
            colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(
                containerColor = if (isDarkTheme) Color(0xFF2D2D2D) else Color(0xFFF5F5F5)
            )
        ) {
            Column(modifier = Modifier.padding(16.dp)) {
                Text(
                    text = "Açıklama",
                    color = textColor,
                    fontSize = 16.sp,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                )
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(8.dp))
                Text(
                    text = app.description,
                    color = secondaryTextColor,
                    fontSize = 14.sp
                )
            }
        }

        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(16.dp))

        Row(
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)
        ) {
            // 🟢 FIX: Ana buton – Aç / Güncelle / Yükle
            Button(
                onClick = {
                    when {
                        app.isUpdatable -> onUpdate(app)
                        app.isInstalled && canOpen -> onOpen(app)
                        app.isInstalled -> onUninstall(app)
                        else -> onDownload(app)
                    }
                },
                colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(
                    containerColor = when {
                        app.isUpdatable -> Color(0xFFFFA000)
                        app.isInstalled && canOpen -> Color(0xFF4CAF50)
                        else -> Color(0xFF0078D7)
                    }
                ),
                modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
            ) {
                Text(
                    text = when {
                        app.isUpdatable -> "Güncelle"
                        app.isInstalled && canOpen -> "Aç"
                        else -> "Play Store'da Aç"
                    },
                    color = Color.White
                )
            }

            if (app.isInstalled) {
                Button(
                    onClick = { onUninstall(app) },
                    colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = Color(0xFFE53935)),
                    modifier = Modifier.weight(0.5f)
                ) {
                    Text("Kaldır", color = Color.White)
                }
            }
        }

        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(8.dp))
        Text(
            text = "📌 Bu uygulama Play Store üzerinden yönetilmektedir.",
            color = secondaryTextColor,
            fontSize = 11.sp
        )
    }
}
========== app/src/main/java/com/windowsui/WifiController.kt ==========
// ====================================================================
// 3. WIFICONTROLLER.KT – TAM VE HATASIZ (Root ile Çalışır)
// ====================================================================
package com.windowsui

import android.Manifest
import android.content.Context
import android.content.pm.PackageManager
import android.net.wifi.WifiConfiguration
import android.net.wifi.WifiManager
import android.os.Build
import android.util.Log
import androidx.core.content.ContextCompat
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
import java.util.*

/**
 * WifiController – Wi-Fi Kontrolcüsü
 * NOT: Android 10+ için bağlanma işlemleri root gerektirir.
 */
object WifiController {

    private const val TAG = "WifiController"
    private const val PREFS_NAME = "ASUI_WifiController"

    data class WifiNetwork(
        val ssid: String,
        val bssid: String,
        val signalStrength: Int,
        val frequency: Int,
        val capabilities: String,
        val timestamp: Long
    ) {
        val signalLevel: String get() = when {
            signalStrength > -50 -> "📶 Mükemmel"
            signalStrength > -60 -> "📶 İyi"
            signalStrength > -70 -> "📶 Orta"
            else -> "📶 Zayıf"
        }
        
        val isOpen: Boolean get() = capabilities.contains("ESS") && 
                                     !capabilities.contains("WPA") && 
                                     !capabilities.contains("WEP")
        
        val isSecure: Boolean get() = !isOpen
        
        val securityType: String get() = when {
            capabilities.contains("WPA3") -> "🔐 WPA3"
            capabilities.contains("WPA2") -> "🔐 WPA2"
            capabilities.contains("WPA") -> "🔐 WPA"
            capabilities.contains("WEP") -> "🔐 WEP"
            else -> "🌐 Açık"
        }
    }

    private val _isEnabled = MutableStateFlow(false)
    val isEnabled: StateFlow<Boolean> = _isEnabled.asStateFlow()

    private val _ssid = MutableStateFlow("")
    val ssid: StateFlow<String> = _ssid.asStateFlow()

    private val _signalStrength = MutableStateFlow(0)
    val signalStrength: StateFlow<Int> = _signalStrength.asStateFlow()

    private val _ipAddress = MutableStateFlow("")
    val ipAddress: StateFlow<String> = _ipAddress.asStateFlow()

    private val _networkHistory = MutableStateFlow<List<String>>(emptyList())
    val networkHistory: StateFlow<List<String>> = _networkHistory.asStateFlow()

    private var appContext: Context? = null
    private var wifiManager: WifiManager? = null

    fun init(ctx: Context) {
        appContext = ctx.applicationContext
        wifiManager = ctx.applicationContext.getSystemService(Context.WIFI_SERVICE) as WifiManager
        loadState()
        updateStatus()
        Log.d(TAG, "WifiController initialized")
    }

    private fun hasLocationPermission(): Boolean {
        val ctx = appContext ?: return false
        return if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.M) {
            ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(ctx, Manifest.permission.ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION) == PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED
        } else true
    }

    suspend fun enableWifi(enabled: Boolean): Boolean = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val wm = wifiManager ?: return@withContext false
        try {
            wm.isWifiEnabled = enabled
            _isEnabled.value = enabled
            saveState()
            updateStatus()
            Log.d(TAG, "Wi-Fi ${if (enabled) "enabled" else "disabled"}")
            true
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Wi-Fi enable error: ${e.message}")
            false
        }
    }

    suspend fun scanNetworks(): List<WifiNetwork> = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val wm = wifiManager ?: return@withContext emptyList()
        try {
            if (!hasLocationPermission()) {
                Log.w(TAG, "Location permission required for Wi-Fi scan")
                return@withContext emptyList()
            }
            
            wm.startScan()
            val results = wm.scanResults ?: return@withContext emptyList()
            
            results.map { result ->
                WifiNetwork(
                    ssid = result.SSID ?: "Bilinmiyor",
                    bssid = result.BSSID ?: "",
                    signalStrength = result.level,
                    frequency = result.frequency,
                    capabilities = result.capabilities ?: "",
                    timestamp = result.timestamp
                )
            }.distinctBy { it.ssid }.sortedByDescending { it.signalStrength }
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Scan error: ${e.message}")
            emptyList()
        }
    }

    suspend fun connectToNetwork(ssid: String, password: String? = null): Boolean = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val wm = wifiManager ?: return@withContext false
        try {
            // Root ile bağlanmayı dene
            val result = SystemUtils.runRootCommand("svc wifi enable && cmd wifi connect-network \"$ssid\" wpa2 \"$password\"")
            if (result.contains("Connected")) {
                _ssid.value = ssid
                saveState()
                updateStatus()
                Log.d(TAG, "Connected to: $ssid (via root)")
                return@withContext true
            }
            
            // Root yoksa normal yöntem (Android 10- çalışır)
            val scanResults = wm.scanResults
            val network = scanResults.find { it.SSID == ssid }
            if (network == null) {
                Log.e(TAG, "Network not found: $ssid")
                return@withContext false
            }
            
            val isOpen = network.capabilities.contains("ESS") && 
                        !network.capabilities.contains("WPA") &&
                        !network.capabilities.contains("WEP")
            
            val config = WifiConfiguration().apply {
                this.SSID = "\"$ssid\""
                if (isOpen) {
                    allowedKeyManagement.set(WifiConfiguration.KeyMgmt.NONE)
                } else if (password != null) {
                    allowedKeyManagement.set(WifiConfiguration.KeyMgmt.WPA_PSK)
                    this.preSharedKey = "\"$password\""
                } else {
                    return@withContext false
                }
            }
            
            val networkId = wm.addNetwork(config)
            if (networkId == -1) {
                Log.e(TAG, "Failed to add network")
                return@withContext false
            }
            
            wm.enableNetwork(networkId, true)
            wm.reconnect()
            
            _ssid.value = ssid
            saveState()
            updateStatus()
            
            Log.d(TAG, "Connected to: $ssid")
            true
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Connect error: ${e.message}")
            false
        }
    }

    suspend fun disconnect(): Boolean = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val wm = wifiManager ?: return@withContext false
        try {
            wm.disconnect()
            _ssid.value = ""
            saveState()
            updateStatus()
            Log.d(TAG, "Disconnected")
            true
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Disconnect error: ${e.message}")
            false
        }
    }

    suspend fun forgetNetwork(ssid: String): Boolean = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val wm = wifiManager ?: return@withContext false
        try {
            val configs = wm.configuredNetworks
            configs?.find { it.SSID == "\"$ssid\"" }?.let { config ->
                wm.removeNetwork(config.networkId)
                wm.saveConfiguration()
                Log.d(TAG, "Network forgotten: $ssid")
                return@withContext true
            }
            false
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Forget error: ${e.message}")
            false
        }
    }

    fun updateStatus() {
        val wm = wifiManager ?: return
        _isEnabled.value = wm.isWifiEnabled
        
        if (wm.isWifiEnabled) {
            val info = wm.connectionInfo
            info?.let {
                _ssid.value = it.ssid?.replace("\"", "") ?: ""
                _signalStrength.value = WifiManager.calculateSignalLevel(it.rssi, 5)
                val ip = it.ipAddress
                if (ip != 0) {
                    _ipAddress.value = "${(ip and 0xFF)}.${(ip shr 8 and 0xFF)}.${(ip shr 16 and 0xFF)}.${(ip shr 24 and 0xFF)}"
                }
            }
        } else {
            _ssid.value = ""
            _signalStrength.value = 0
            _ipAddress.value = ""
        }
    }

    fun getSignalBars(): String {
        val level = _signalStrength.value
        return when (level) {
            0 -> "📶"
            1 -> "📶▁"
            2 -> "📶▁▂"
            3 -> "📶▁▂▃"
            4 -> "📶▁▂▃▄"
            5 -> "📶▁▂▃▄▅"
            else -> "📶"
        }
    }

    fun getSpiritNetwork(context: Context): String {
        val personality = runCatching { DeviceInfo.analyzeDevicePersonality(context) }.getOrNull() ?: "Bilinmiyor"
        return when {
            personality.contains("süper kahraman") -> "✨ ASUI_SUPER"
            personality.contains("denge ustası") -> "🧘 ASUI_BALANCE"
            personality.contains("minimalist") -> "📿 ASUI_MINIMAL"
            else -> "💎 ASUI_DESKTOP"
        }
    }

    suspend fun getNetworkReport(): String = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val networks = scanNetworks()
        val total = networks.size
        val secured = networks.count { it.isSecure }
        val open = networks.count { it.isOpen }
        val best = networks.maxByOrNull { it.signalStrength }

        buildString {
            appendLine("📊 AĞ ANALİZ RAPORU")
            appendLine("─────────────────────────")
            appendLine("Toplam Ağ: $total")
            appendLine("Güvenli: $secured")
            appendLine("Açık: $open")
            appendLine("Bağlı: ${_ssid.value}")
            appendLine("Sinyal: ${_signalStrength.value}/5")
            best?.let {
                appendLine("En İyi Sinyal: ${it.ssid} (${it.signalStrength} dBm)")
                appendLine("Güvenlik: ${it.securityType}")
            }
            appendLine("─────────────────────────")
            appendLine("📡 AĞ LİSTESİ")
            networks.take(10).forEach { network ->
                appendLine("${network.securityType} ${network.ssid} (${network.signalLevel})")
            }
            if (networks.size > 10) {
                appendLine("... ve ${networks.size - 10} ağ daha")
            }
        }
    }

    private fun saveState() {
        val ctx = appContext ?: return
        val prefs = ctx.getSharedPreferences(PREFS_NAME, Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
        prefs.edit().apply {
            putString("lastSsid", _ssid.value)
            putString("history", _networkHistory.value.joinToString("|"))
        }.apply()
    }

    private fun loadState() {
        val ctx = appContext ?: return
        val prefs = ctx.getSharedPreferences(PREFS_NAME, Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
        
        _ssid.value = prefs.getString("lastSsid", "") ?: ""
        
        val historyStr = prefs.getString("history", "") ?: ""
        if (historyStr.isNotEmpty()) {
            _networkHistory.value = historyStr.split("|").filter { it.isNotEmpty() }
        }
    }

    fun resetToDefaults() {
        _networkHistory.value = emptyList()
        saveState()
        Log.d(TAG, "Reset to defaults")
    }
}
========== app/src/main/java/com/windowsui/WallpaperManager.kt ==========
package com.windowsui

import android.Manifest
import android.app.WallpaperManager as AndroidWallpaperManager
import android.content.Context
import android.content.pm.PackageManager
import android.graphics.Bitmap
import android.graphics.BitmapFactory
import android.net.Uri
import android.os.Build
import android.provider.MediaStore
import android.util.Log
import androidx.core.content.ContextCompat
import kotlinx.coroutines.*
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow
import java.util.*
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.toArgb

/**
 * WallpaperManager – Gelişmiş Duvar Kağıdı Yöneticisi
 */
object WallpaperManager {

    private const val TAG = "WallpaperManager"
    private const val PREFS_NAME = "ASUI_WallpaperManager"
    private const val CACHE_SIZE = 10

    private val _currentUri = MutableStateFlow<Uri?>(null)
    val currentUri: StateFlow<Uri?> = _currentUri.asStateFlow()

    private val _currentBitmap = MutableStateFlow<Bitmap?>(null)
    val currentBitmap: StateFlow<Bitmap?> = _currentBitmap.asStateFlow()

    private val _slideshowEnabled = MutableStateFlow(false)
    val slideshowEnabled: StateFlow<Boolean> = _slideshowEnabled.asStateFlow()

    private val _slideshowInterval = MutableStateFlow(30)
    val slideshowInterval: StateFlow<Int> = _slideshowInterval.asStateFlow()

    private val _blurEnabled = MutableStateFlow(false)
    val blurEnabled: StateFlow<Boolean> = _blurEnabled.asStateFlow()

    private val _blurRadius = MutableStateFlow(12)
    val blurRadius: StateFlow<Int> = _blurRadius.asStateFlow()

    private var appContext: Context? = null
    private var wallpaperHistory = mutableListOf<Uri>()
    private var slideshowJob: Job? = null
    private val scope = CoroutineScope(Dispatchers.IO + SupervisorJob())

    fun init(ctx: Context) {
        appContext = ctx.applicationContext
        loadState()
        Log.d(TAG, "WallpaperManager initialized")
    }

    private fun hasStoragePermission(): Boolean {
        val ctx = appContext ?: return false
        return if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.TIRAMISU) {
            ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(ctx, Manifest.permission.READ_MEDIA_IMAGES) == PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED
        } else {
            ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(ctx, Manifest.permission.READ_EXTERNAL_STORAGE) == PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED
        }
    }

    suspend fun setWallpaper(uri: Uri): Boolean = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val ctx = appContext ?: return@withContext false
        try {
            val bitmap = loadBitmapFromUri(uri) ?: return@withContext false
            
            val wallpaperManager = AndroidWallpaperManager.getInstance(ctx)
            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.N) {
                wallpaperManager.setBitmap(bitmap, null, false, AndroidWallpaperManager.FLAG_SYSTEM)
            } else {
                @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
                wallpaperManager.setBitmap(bitmap)
            }
            
            _currentUri.value = uri
            _currentBitmap.value = bitmap
            addToHistory(uri)
            saveState()
            
            Log.d(TAG, "Wallpaper set: $uri")
            true
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Wallpaper set error: ${e.message}")
            false
        }
    }

    suspend fun setWallpaperBitmap(bitmap: Bitmap): Boolean = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val ctx = appContext ?: return@withContext false
        try {
            val wallpaperManager = AndroidWallpaperManager.getInstance(ctx)
            
            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.N) {
                wallpaperManager.setBitmap(bitmap, null, false, AndroidWallpaperManager.FLAG_SYSTEM)
            } else {
                @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
                wallpaperManager.setBitmap(bitmap)
            }
            
            _currentBitmap.value = bitmap
            saveState()
            
            Log.d(TAG, "Wallpaper set from bitmap")
            true
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Wallpaper set error: ${e.message}")
            false
        }
    }

    suspend fun resetWallpaper(): Boolean = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val ctx = appContext ?: return@withContext false
        try {
            val wallpaperManager = AndroidWallpaperManager.getInstance(ctx)
            wallpaperManager.clear(AndroidWallpaperManager.FLAG_SYSTEM)
            
            _currentUri.value = null
            _currentBitmap.value = null
            saveState()
            
            Log.d(TAG, "Wallpaper reset to default")
            true
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Wallpaper reset error: ${e.message}")
            false
        }
    }

    fun getSpiritColor(context: Context): androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color {
        val personality = runCatching { DeviceInfo.analyzeDevicePersonality(context) }.getOrNull() ?: "Bilinmiyor"
        return Utils.getSpiritColor(personality)
    }

    suspend fun createSpiritWallpaper(context: Context): Boolean = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val ctx = appContext ?: return@withContext false
        try {
            val color = getSpiritColor(context)
            val bitmap = Bitmap.createBitmap(1080, 1920, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888)
            val canvas = android.graphics.Canvas(bitmap)
            canvas.drawColor(color.toArgb())
            
            val gradient = android.graphics.LinearGradient(
                0f, 0f, 1080f, 1920f,
                intArrayOf(color.toArgb(), androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color.White.toArgb()),
                null,
                android.graphics.Shader.TileMode.CLAMP
            )
            val paint = android.graphics.Paint().apply { shader = gradient }
            canvas.drawRect(0f, 0f, 1080f, 1920f, paint)
            
            setWallpaperBitmap(bitmap)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Spirit wallpaper error: ${e.message}")
            false
        }
    }

    suspend fun magicWallpaper(context: Context): Boolean = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val ctx = appContext ?: return@withContext false
        try {
            if (!hasStoragePermission()) {
                Log.w(TAG, "Storage permission required for magic wallpaper")
                return@withContext createSpiritWallpaper(context)
            }
            val uris = getAllWallpaperUris()
            if (uris.isNotEmpty()) {
                val randomUri = uris.random()
                setWallpaper(randomUri)
            } else {
                createSpiritWallpaper(context)
            }
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Magic wallpaper error: ${e.message}")
            false
        }
    }

    suspend fun applyBlurEffect(radius: Int = 12): Boolean = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        try {
            _blurRadius.value = radius
            _blurEnabled.value = true
            saveState()
            Log.d(TAG, "Blur effect enabled: radius=$radius")
            true
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Blur effect error: ${e.message}")
            false
        }
    }

    suspend fun extractColorPalette(): List<Int> = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val bitmap = _currentBitmap.value ?: return@withContext emptyList()
        try {
            val colors = mutableListOf<Int>()
            val width = bitmap.width
            val height = bitmap.height
            val step = 50
            
            for (x in 0 until width step step) {
                for (y in 0 until height step step) {
                    val pixel = bitmap.getPixel(x, y)
                    colors.add(pixel)
                    if (colors.size >= 8) break
                }
                if (colors.size >= 8) break
            }
            colors
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Palette extraction error: ${e.message}")
            emptyList()
        }
    }

    suspend fun startSlideshow(intervalSeconds: Int = 30) {
        val ctx = appContext ?: return
        stopSlideshow()
        _slideshowEnabled.value = true
        _slideshowInterval.value = intervalSeconds
        saveState()
        
        slideshowJob = scope.launch {
            if (!hasStoragePermission()) {
                _slideshowEnabled.value = false
                Log.w(TAG, "Storage permission required for slideshow")
                return@launch
            }
            
            val uris = getAllWallpaperUris()
            if (uris.isEmpty()) {
                _slideshowEnabled.value = false
                return@launch
            }
            
            var index = 0
            while (_slideshowEnabled.value) {
                val uri = uris[index % uris.size]
                setWallpaper(uri)
                index++
                delay(intervalSeconds * 1000L)
            }
        }
    }

    fun stopSlideshow() {
        _slideshowEnabled.value = false
        slideshowJob?.cancel()
        slideshowJob = null
        saveState()
        Log.d(TAG, "Slideshow stopped")
    }

    private suspend fun getAllWallpaperUris(): List<Uri> = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val ctx = appContext ?: return@withContext emptyList()
        val uris = mutableListOf<Uri>()
        try {
            if (!hasStoragePermission()) {
                Log.w(TAG, "Storage permission required for gallery scan")
                return@withContext emptyList()
            }
            
            val projection = arrayOf(MediaStore.Images.Media._ID, MediaStore.Images.Media.DATA)
            val cursor = ctx.contentResolver.query(
                MediaStore.Images.Media.EXTERNAL_CONTENT_URI,
                projection,
                null,
                null,
                null
            )
            cursor?.use {
                val idColumn = it.getColumnIndexOrThrow(MediaStore.Images.Media._ID)
                while (it.moveToNext()) {
                    val id = it.getLong(idColumn)
                    val uri = Uri.withAppendedPath(
                        MediaStore.Images.Media.EXTERNAL_CONTENT_URI,
                        id.toString()
                    )
                    uris.add(uri)
                }
            }
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Gallery scan error: ${e.message}")
        }
        uris
    }

    private suspend fun loadBitmapFromUri(uri: Uri): Bitmap? = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val ctx = appContext ?: return@withContext null
        try {
            ctx.contentResolver.openInputStream(uri)?.use { inputStream ->
                BitmapFactory.decodeStream(inputStream)
            }
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Bitmap load error: ${e.message}")
            null
        }
    }

    private fun addToHistory(uri: Uri) {
        wallpaperHistory.add(0, uri)
        if (wallpaperHistory.size > CACHE_SIZE) {
            wallpaperHistory.removeAt(wallpaperHistory.size - 1)
        }
    }

    fun getHistory(): List<Uri> = wallpaperHistory.toList()

    fun clearHistory() {
        wallpaperHistory.clear()
    }

    private fun saveState() {
        val ctx = appContext ?: return
        val prefs = ctx.getSharedPreferences(PREFS_NAME, Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
        prefs.edit().apply {
            putString("currentUri", _currentUri.value?.toString())
            putBoolean("slideshowEnabled", _slideshowEnabled.value)
            putInt("slideshowInterval", _slideshowInterval.value)
            putBoolean("blurEnabled", _blurEnabled.value)
            putInt("blurRadius", _blurRadius.value)
            putString("history", wallpaperHistory.joinToString("|") { it.toString() })
        }.apply()
    }

    private fun loadState() {
        val ctx = appContext ?: return
        val prefs = ctx.getSharedPreferences(PREFS_NAME, Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
        
        val uriString = prefs.getString("currentUri", null)
        _currentUri.value = uriString?.let { Uri.parse(it) }
        
        _slideshowEnabled.value = prefs.getBoolean("slideshowEnabled", false)
        _slideshowInterval.value = prefs.getInt("slideshowInterval", 30)
        _blurEnabled.value = prefs.getBoolean("blurEnabled", false)
        _blurRadius.value = prefs.getInt("blurRadius", 12)
        
        val historyStr = prefs.getString("history", "") ?: ""
        wallpaperHistory.clear()
        if (historyStr.isNotEmpty()) {
            historyStr.split("|").forEach { str ->
                try {
                    wallpaperHistory.add(Uri.parse(str))
                } catch (_: Exception) {}
            }
        }
    }

    fun resetToDefaults() {
        stopSlideshow()
        _currentUri.value = null
        _currentBitmap.value = null
        _blurEnabled.value = false
        _blurRadius.value = 12
        wallpaperHistory.clear()
        saveState()
    }
}
========== app/src/main/java/com/windowsui/DesktopState.kt ==========
package com.windowsui

import android.content.Context
import android.util.Log
import androidx.compose.runtime.*
import androidx.compose.ui.geometry.Offset
import com.google.gson.Gson
import com.google.gson.GsonBuilder
import com.google.gson.JsonDeserializationContext
import com.google.gson.JsonDeserializer
import com.google.gson.JsonElement
import com.google.gson.JsonObject
import com.google.gson.JsonSerializationContext
import com.google.gson.JsonSerializer
import com.google.gson.reflect.TypeToken
import java.lang.reflect.Type
import java.util.*

/**
 * DesktopState – Masaüstü durum yöneticisi (Singleton)
 *
 * Tüm masaüstü öğelerinin (simgeler, widget'lar, klasörler) durumunu,
 * konumlarını, özel isimlerini, seçimlerini ve kalıcı ayarlarını yönetir.
 *
 * 🟢 FIX:
 * - saveState() artık sadece sürükleme bittiğinde çağrılır (sürükleme sırasında çağrılmaz)
 * - Context doğrudan parametre olarak geçilir, AppGlobal kaldırıldı
 * - Offset için özel Gson serializer/deserializer eklendi
 * - Tüm koleksiyonlar için güvenli serileştirme
 *
 * Özellikler:
 * - Simge konumları ve özel isimler (kalıcı)
 * - Çoklu seçim (selectedPackages)
 * - Yeniden adlandırma durumu (renamingPackage)
 * - Widget yönetimi (ekle, kaldır, konumlandır, boyutlandır)
 * - Klasör yönetimi (oluştur, sil, içine öğe ekle/çıkar)
 * - Masaüstü panosu (kopyala/kes/yapıştır)
 * - Geri Al / İleri Al (Undo/Redo) – son 20 işlem
 * - Masaüstü yakınlaştırma (zoom) seviyesi
 * - Masaüstü düzeni kilidi
 * - Otomatik düzenleme modu
 * - Duvar kağıdı slayt gösterisi ayarları
 * - Anlık görüntü (snapshot) alma ve geri yükleme
 * - Kalıcılık (SharedPreferences ile JSON serileştirme)
 */
object DesktopState {

    private const val TAG = "DesktopState"
    private const val PREFS_NAME = "ASUI_DesktopStatePrefs"

    // 🟢 FIX: Gson Offset adapter ile yapılandırıldı
    private val gson: Gson = GsonBuilder()
        .registerTypeAdapter(Offset::class.java, OffsetSerializer())
        .registerTypeAdapter(Offset::class.java, OffsetDeserializer())
        .create()

    // ==================== SİMGELER ====================
    val selectedPackages = mutableStateListOf<String>()
    val iconOffsets = mutableStateMapOf<String, Offset>()
    val customNames = mutableStateMapOf<String, String>()
    var renamingPackage by mutableStateOf<String?>(null)

    // ==================== WİDGET'LAR ====================
    val widgets = mutableStateMapOf<String, DesktopWidgetData>()
    val widgetOrder = mutableStateListOf<String>()

    // ==================== KLASÖRLER (Offset yerine x,y ile) ====================
    // 🟢 FIX: Offset yerine ayrı x,y alanları kullanıldı
    val folders = mutableStateMapOf<String, DesktopFolderData>()

    // ==================== PANO ====================
    val clipboardItems = mutableStateListOf<String>()
    var isCutOperation by mutableStateOf(false)

    // ==================== GERİ AL / İLERİ AL ====================
    private val undoStack = mutableListOf<DesktopAction>()
    private val redoStack = mutableListOf<DesktopAction>()
    private var isUndoRedoInProgress = false

    // ==================== MASAÜSTÜ DÜZENİ ====================
    var desktopZoom by mutableStateOf(1.0f)
    var isLayoutLocked by mutableStateOf(false)
    var arrangementMode by mutableStateOf("name")
    var showHidden by mutableStateOf(false)

    // ==================== DUVAR KAĞIDI SLAYT ====================
    var wallpaperSlideshowEnabled by mutableStateOf(false)
    var wallpaperSlideshowInterval by mutableStateOf(30)
    val wallpaperSlideshowUris = mutableStateListOf<String>()

    // ==================== KALICILIK (Context ile) ====================
    private var isSavingScheduled = false

    /**
     * 🟢 FIX: saveState artık context parametresi alıyor ve sürükleme sırasında çağrılmıyor.
     * Bu fonksiyon sadece sürükleme bittiğinde veya önemli değişikliklerde çağrılmalıdır.
     */
    fun saveState(context: Context) {
        try {
            val prefs = context.getSharedPreferences(PREFS_NAME, Context.MODE_PRIVATE)

            // Offset'leri serileştirilebilir hale getir
            val offsetData = iconOffsets.map { (key, offset) ->
                mapOf("key" to key, "x" to offset.x, "y" to offset.y)
            }

            val data = mapOf(
                "iconOffsets" to gson.toJson(offsetData),
                "customNames" to gson.toJson(customNames),
                "widgets" to gson.toJson(widgets.values.toList()),
                "widgetOrder" to gson.toJson(widgetOrder),
                "folders" to gson.toJson(folders.values.toList()),
                "desktopZoom" to desktopZoom,
                "isLayoutLocked" to isLayoutLocked,
                "arrangementMode" to arrangementMode,
                "showHidden" to showHidden,
                "wallpaperSlideshowEnabled" to wallpaperSlideshowEnabled,
                "wallpaperSlideshowInterval" to wallpaperSlideshowInterval,
                "wallpaperSlideshowUris" to gson.toJson(wallpaperSlideshowUris)
            )

            prefs.edit().putString("desktop_state", gson.toJson(data)).apply()
            Log.d(TAG, "Durum kaydedildi (${iconOffsets.size} simge, ${widgets.size} widget)")
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Kaydetme hatası", e)
        }
    }

    /**
     * 🟢 FIX: loadState context parametresi alıyor.
     */
    fun loadState(context: Context) {
        try {
            val prefs = context.getSharedPreferences(PREFS_NAME, Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
            val json = prefs.getString("desktop_state", null) ?: return

            val type = object : TypeToken<Map<String, String>>() {}.type
            val data: Map<String, String> = gson.fromJson(json, type)

            // IconOffsets (JSON'dan offset verilerini oku)
            data["iconOffsets"]?.let { offsetJson ->
                val listType = object : TypeToken<List<Map<String, Any>>>() {}.type
                val list: List<Map<String, Any>> = gson.fromJson(offsetJson, listType)
                list.forEach { entry ->
                    val key = entry["key"] as? String ?: return@forEach
                    val x = (entry["x"] as? Number)?.toFloat() ?: 0f
                    val y = (entry["y"] as? Number)?.toFloat() ?: 0f
                    iconOffsets[key] = Offset(x, y)
                }
            }

            // CustomNames
            data["customNames"]?.let {
                val mapType = object : TypeToken<Map<String, String>>() {}.type
                val map: Map<String, String> = gson.fromJson(it, mapType)
                customNames.putAll(map)
            }

            // Widgets
            data["widgets"]?.let {
                val listType = object : TypeToken<List<DesktopWidgetData>>() {}.type
                val list: List<DesktopWidgetData> = gson.fromJson(it, listType)
                widgets.clear()
                list.forEach { widgets[it.id] = it }
            }

            // WidgetOrder
            data["widgetOrder"]?.let {
                val listType = object : TypeToken<List<String>>() {}.type
                val list: List<String> = gson.fromJson(it, listType)
                widgetOrder.clear()
                widgetOrder.addAll(list)
            }

            // Folders
            data["folders"]?.let {
                val listType = object : TypeToken<List<DesktopFolderData>>() {}.type
                val list: List<DesktopFolderData> = gson.fromJson(it, listType)
                folders.clear()
                list.forEach { folders[it.id] = it }
            }

            // Diğer ayarlar
            desktopZoom = (data["desktopZoom"]?.toFloatOrNull() ?: 1.0f)
            isLayoutLocked = data["isLayoutLocked"]?.toBoolean() ?: false
            arrangementMode = data["arrangementMode"] ?: "name"
            showHidden = data["showHidden"]?.toBoolean() ?: false
            wallpaperSlideshowEnabled = data["wallpaperSlideshowEnabled"]?.toBoolean() ?: false
            wallpaperSlideshowInterval = data["wallpaperSlideshowInterval"]?.toIntOrNull() ?: 30

            data["wallpaperSlideshowUris"]?.let {
                val listType = object : TypeToken<List<String>>() {}.type
                val list: List<String> = gson.fromJson(it, listType)
                wallpaperSlideshowUris.clear()
                wallpaperSlideshowUris.addAll(list)
            }

            Log.d(TAG, "Durum yüklendi: ${iconOffsets.size} simge, ${widgets.size} widget")
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Yükleme hatası", e)
        }
    }

    // ==================== WİDGET YÖNETİMİ ====================
    fun addWidget(type: String, x: Float = 100f, y: Float = 100f, context: Context): String {
        val id = "widget_${System.currentTimeMillis()}"
        val width = when (type) {
            "clock" -> 200
            "weather" -> 220
            "notes" -> 240
            "system_monitor" -> 260
            "calendar" -> 280
            else -> 200
        }
        val height = when (type) {
            "clock" -> 140
            "weather" -> 180
            "notes" -> 200
            "system_monitor" -> 160
            "calendar" -> 220
            else -> 150
        }
        widgets[id] = DesktopWidgetData(
            id = id,
            type = type,
            x = x,
            y = y,
            width = width,
            height = height
        )
        if (!widgetOrder.contains(id)) widgetOrder.add(id)
        saveState(context) // Önemli değişiklik, kaydedilir
        Log.d(TAG, "Widget eklendi: $type ($id)")
        return id
    }

    fun removeWidget(id: String, context: Context) {
        widgets.remove(id)
        widgetOrder.remove(id)
        saveState(context)
        Log.d(TAG, "Widget kaldırıldı: $id")
    }

    /**
     * 🟢 FIX: Widget güncellemesi sırasında saveState çağrılmaz.
     * Bu fonksiyon sadece konum/boyut günceller, kaydetme işlemi çağıran tarafından yapılmalıdır.
     */
    fun updateWidget(id: String, x: Float, y: Float, width: Int, height: Int) {
        widgets[id]?.let {
            widgets[id] = it.copy(x = x, y = y, width = width, height = height)
        }
    }

    /**
     * Widget güncellemesini kaydeder (sürükleme bittiğinde çağrılır).
     */
    fun saveWidgetState(context: Context) {
        saveState(context)
    }

    // ==================== KLASÖR YÖNETİMİ ====================
    fun createFolder(name: String, itemIds: List<String> = emptyList(), context: Context): String {
        val id = "folder_${System.currentTimeMillis()}"
        // Varsayılan konum (masaüstünün sol üstüne yakın)
        folders[id] = DesktopFolderData(
            id = id,
            name = name,
            items = itemIds.toMutableList(),
            posX = 50f,
            posY = 50f,
            isExpanded = false
        )
        itemIds.forEach { itemId ->
            if (selectedPackages.contains(itemId)) {
                selectedPackages.remove(itemId)
            }
            iconOffsets.remove(itemId)
        }
        iconOffsets[id] = Offset(50f, 50f)
        saveState(context)
        Log.d(TAG, "Klasör oluşturuldu: $name ($id), ${itemIds.size} öğe")
        return id
    }

    fun deleteFolder(id: String, context: Context) {
        folders[id]?.let { folder ->
            folder.items.forEach { itemId ->
                if (!selectedPackages.contains(itemId)) {
                    selectedPackages.add(itemId)
                }
            }
            folders.remove(id)
            iconOffsets.remove(id)
            customNames.remove(id)
            saveState(context)
            Log.d(TAG, "Klasör silindi: ${folder.name} ($id)")
        }
    }

    fun addItemToFolder(folderId: String, itemId: String, context: Context) {
        folders[folderId]?.let { folder ->
            if (!folder.items.contains(itemId)) {
                folder.items.add(itemId)
                selectedPackages.remove(itemId)
                saveState(context)
            }
        }
    }

    fun removeItemFromFolder(folderId: String, itemId: String, context: Context) {
        folders[folderId]?.let { folder ->
            if (folder.items.contains(itemId)) {
                folder.items.remove(itemId)
                if (!selectedPackages.contains(itemId)) {
                    selectedPackages.add(itemId)
                }
                saveState(context)
            }
        }
    }

    fun renameFolder(folderId: String, newName: String, context: Context) {
        folders[folderId]?.let {
            folders[folderId] = it.copy(name = newName)
            saveState(context)
        }
    }

    // ==================== PANO ====================
    fun copyItems(itemIds: List<String>) {
        clipboardItems.clear()
        clipboardItems.addAll(itemIds)
        isCutOperation = false
        Log.d(TAG, "${itemIds.size} öğe kopyalandı")
    }

    fun cutItems(itemIds: List<String>) {
        clipboardItems.clear()
        clipboardItems.addAll(itemIds)
        isCutOperation = true
        Log.d(TAG, "${itemIds.size} öğe kesildi")
    }

    fun pasteItems(targetId: String? = null, context: Context) {
        if (clipboardItems.isEmpty()) return
        val itemsToPaste = clipboardItems.toList()
        clipboardItems.clear()

        if (isCutOperation) {
            if (targetId != null) {
                itemsToPaste.forEach { itemId ->
                    addItemToFolder(targetId, itemId, context)
                }
            } else {
                selectedPackages.clear()
                selectedPackages.addAll(itemsToPaste)
            }
            isCutOperation = false
        } else {
            Log.d(TAG, "Kopyalama işlemi (kısayol oluşturma ileride)")
        }
        saveState(context)
    }

    // ==================== GERİ AL / İLERİ AL ====================
    fun pushAction(action: DesktopAction) {
        if (isUndoRedoInProgress) return
        redoStack.clear()
        undoStack.add(action)
        if (undoStack.size > 20) {
            undoStack.removeAt(0)
        }
    }

    fun undo(context: Context) {
        if (undoStack.isEmpty()) return
        isUndoRedoInProgress = true
        val action = undoStack.removeAt(undoStack.size - 1)
        action.undo()
        redoStack.add(action)
        isUndoRedoInProgress = false
        saveState(context)
        Log.d(TAG, "Geri alındı: ${action.type}")
    }

    fun redo(context: Context) {
        if (redoStack.isEmpty()) return
        isUndoRedoInProgress = true
        val action = redoStack.removeAt(redoStack.size - 1)
        action.redo()
        undoStack.add(action)
        isUndoRedoInProgress = false
        saveState(context)
        Log.d(TAG, "İleri alındı: ${action.type}")
    }

    // ==================== SEÇİM ====================
    fun clearSelection() {
        selectedPackages.clear()
        renamingPackage = null
    }

    fun selectSingle(id: String) {
        selectedPackages.clear()
        selectedPackages.add(id)
    }

    fun toggleSelection(id: String) {
        if (selectedPackages.contains(id)) {
            selectedPackages.remove(id)
        } else {
            selectedPackages.add(id)
        }
    }

    fun selectAll(visibleIds: List<String>) {
        selectedPackages.clear()
        selectedPackages.addAll(visibleIds)
    }

    // ==================== YARDIMCI ====================
    fun getItemType(id: String): String {
        return when {
            widgets.containsKey(id) -> "widget"
            folders.containsKey(id) -> "folder"
            id == "recycle_bin" -> "system"
            else -> "app"
        }
    }

    fun getDisplayName(id: String, defaultName: String): String {
        return customNames[id] ?: defaultName
    }

    // ==================== ANLIK GÖRÜNTÜ (SNAPSHOT) ====================
    fun createSnapshot(): DesktopSnapshot {
        return DesktopSnapshot(
            iconOffsets = iconOffsets.toMap(),
            customNames = customNames.toMap(),
            widgetData = widgets.values.toList(),
            widgetOrder = widgetOrder.toList(),
            folderData = folders.values.toList(),
            timestamp = System.currentTimeMillis()
        )
    }

    fun restoreSnapshot(snapshot: DesktopSnapshot, context: Context) {
        iconOffsets.clear()
        iconOffsets.putAll(snapshot.iconOffsets)

        customNames.clear()
        customNames.putAll(snapshot.customNames)

        widgets.clear()
        snapshot.widgetData.forEach { widgets[it.id] = it }

        widgetOrder.clear()
        widgetOrder.addAll(snapshot.widgetOrder)

        folders.clear()
        snapshot.folderData.forEach { folders[it.id] = it }

        saveState(context)
        Log.d(TAG, "Anlık görüntü geri yüklendi: ${snapshot.timestamp}")
    }
}

// ==================== OFFSET SERIALIZER / DESERIALIZER ====================

/**
 * 🟢 FIX: Offset için özel Gson serializer (serileştirme)
 */
class OffsetSerializer : JsonSerializer<Offset> {
    override fun serialize(
        src: Offset,
        typeOfSrc: Type,
        context: JsonSerializationContext
    ): JsonElement {
        val json = JsonObject()
        json.addProperty("x", src.x)
        json.addProperty("y", src.y)
        return json
    }
}

/**
 * 🟢 FIX: Offset için özel Gson deserializer (okuma)
 */
class OffsetDeserializer : JsonDeserializer<Offset> {
    override fun deserialize(
        json: JsonElement,
        typeOfT: Type,
        context: JsonDeserializationContext
    ): Offset {
        val obj = json.asJsonObject
        val x = obj.get("x")?.asFloat ?: 0f
        val y = obj.get("y")?.asFloat ?: 0f
        return Offset(x, y)
    }
}
